# More Rental Categories Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Besides wedding dresses, the site lists cars, national clothes, tents and car decorations. Brides browse, filter and search them like dresses, and shops and admins add them like dresses. Each category's fields are defined in one place, so they are cheap to change later.

**Architecture:**
- **Backend:**
  - A new `internal/listing` package holds what every listing shares: card types, the browse query and search, the basic checks and the ownership check. Dresses move onto it with no behaviour change.
  - `internal/catalog` defines the categories and their fields. It validates `listings.attributes` (a new jsonb column), builds the attribute filters, and generates `frontend/src/lib/catalog.json` through a golden-file test.
  - A new `internal/items` package serves the non-dress categories, with the same `Scope` pattern as dresses for shop owners. Its public list and search cover every category.
  - Busy days, the shop page and link previews are widened to every listing.
- **Frontend:**
  - `lib/catalog.ts` reads `catalog.json`. The filter sheet, the item details, the item editor and the category bar are all built from it.
  - New pages: `/c/:slug`, `/items/:id` and `/search`.
  - The admin "Dresses" list becomes "Listings", with a "New listing" chooser and a shared item editor.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27 with Gin, pgx v5, goose and PostgreSQL 17 jsonb (backend); Solid 1.9, @solidjs/router, Vite 7, Tailwind 4 and Deno 2.4 (frontend). There are no new dependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-categories-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- **Dresses are unchanged:** their pages, API, URLs and `dress_details` stay as they are. Every existing dress test must keep passing without edits, except where a task says so.
- **Categories:** the slugs are `dresses`, `cars`, `national-clothes`, `tents` and `car-decorations`, in that display order.
- **Fields:** they are exactly the spec's §4 table:
  - **cars:**
    - `brand_model`: text, at most 100 characters, required;
    - `body`: one of sedan, suv, limousine or minivan, required, filtered by `body`;
    - `color`: one of white, black, silver or other, required, filtered by `color`;
    - `seats`: a number from 1 to 60, optional, filtered by `seats_min`;
    - `price_unit`: hour or day, required, not a filter.
  - **national-clothes:**
    - `for_whom`: one of bride, groom, woman, man or child, required, filtered by `for_whom`;
    - `sizes`: one or more of XS, S, M, L, XL or XXL, required, filtered by `size` (items that have that size).
  - **tents:**
    - `guests`: a number from 1 to 5000, required, filtered by `guests_min`;
    - `area_m2`: a number from 1 to 10000, optional, not a filter.
  - **car-decorations:**
    - `color`: one of white, red, pink, gold or other, required, filtered by `color`.
- **Validation codes:** they go under `attributes.<key>`:
  - `unknown` for a key the category doesn't have;
  - `required` for a missing required field;
  - `invalid` for the wrong kind of value or an option that isn't allowed;
  - `too_long` for text over its limit;
  - `must_be_positive` for a number below its minimum;
  - `too_large` for a number above its maximum.
  An empty optional field is dropped.
- **Ownership:**
  - Under `/api/shop`, the shop always comes from the session, never from the request body or query.
  - Another shop's item, image or busy day answers 404.
  - A dress id on `/items/:id` answers 404, and a non-dress id on `/dresses/:id` answers 404.
  - An item's category never changes after it is created.
- **`frontend/src/lib/catalog.json`** is generated by `cd backend && go test ./internal/catalog/ -update`. Never edit it by hand.
- **Migration `00005_categories.sql`** must be byte-identical to the plan. The test database may already have it applied, and goose identifies migrations by version number.
- **Text:**
  - Every user-visible string goes through `frontend/src/i18n/{tk,ru,en}.json`, with identical keys and placeholders, which a test enforces.
  - Catalog texts use the keys `cat.<slug>`, `attr.<slug>.<key>` and `attr.<slug>.<key>.<option>`. Pass these to `tr(...)`, because they are built at runtime; a test checks that every one exists.
- **Look:** Gyrmyzy tokens only (garnet, blush, veil, silver-ink and so on). There are no new colors, shadows or gradients.
- **Keyboard and form controls:** reuse `Field.tsx`, `Sheet` and the existing chip classes.
- **Backend tests:**
  - Go tests run against the real test database through `internal/testutil`.
  - Run them from `backend/` with `set -a; . ./.env; set +a`, then `GOPROXY=off go test -count=1 ./internal/<pkg>/`.
  - `proxy.golang.org` is DNS-blocked on this network. Never run `go env -w`.
- **Frontend tools:** they run only through Deno, from `frontend/`: `deno task test`, `deno task check` and `deno task build`. Never run Vite with Node.
- **Data:** never write to the `wedding_dev` database, which holds the product owner's real shops.
- **Commits:** every commit message ends with the `Co-Authored-By:` trailer line the controller gives you.

## How to read the steps

- **Full files:** a path in backticks on its own line, directly followed by a code block, gives the **whole file**. Create or overwrite it with exactly that content. From the repository root you can extract it with:
  `python3 -I .superpowers/sdd/cats/block.py <brief> <path> > <path>`
- **Replacements:** "In `path`, replace:" gives an exact text to find (it occurs once) and the text to put in its place.
- **Appends:** "Append to `path`:" gives text to add at the very end of the file.
- **i18n entries:** under **en.json**, **ru.json** and **tk.json**, a block of `"key": "value"` lines goes at the end of that file. Add a comma to the file's current last entry, and keep the closing `}` last. Changed and removed entries are given as replacements.
- **Renames:** `Run: git mv A B` moves a file. Run it before you write the new content.

### Task 1: The shared listing package, with dresses moved onto it

There is no behaviour change in this task. The existing dress, shop, web and server tests are the regression net.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `backend/internal/listing/listing.go`
  - `backend/internal/listing/query.go`
  - `backend/internal/listing/owner.go`
- Modify:
  - `backend/internal/dresses/model.go`
  - `backend/internal/dresses/filter.go`
  - `backend/internal/dresses/public.go`
  - `backend/internal/dresses/repo.go`
  - `backend/internal/dresses/handler.go`
- Test: `backend/internal/listing/query_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `textnorm.Norm`, `textnorm.EscapeLike`, `httpx.Abort`, `httpx.Validation`, `httpx.ErrNotFound` and `db.DBTX`.
- Produces (package `listing`):
  - Types: `CityRef`, `ShopRef`, `ShopCard`, `Page[T]`, and `Basics{ShopID, Title, PriceManat, PriceIsFrom, Description, IsPublished}` with `Normalize()` and `Validate(errs)`. Also `NullIfEmpty(s) *string`.
  - The list query:
    - `Query{City, PriceMin, PriceMax, Q, IDs, ShopSlug, Page, PerPage}` and `ParseQuery(url.Values) (Query, map[string]string)`;
    - `Where` with `Add(cond, val)` (each `?` becomes `$n`), `Apply(Query)`, `SQL()`, `Args()`, `OrderBy()` and `Limit(Query) (string, []any)`.
  - Handler helpers: `OwnedBy(c, d, id, shopID) bool`, `CheckShop(ctx, d, shopID, errs) error` and `ShopIDParam(c) (int64, bool)`.
  - `dresses.CityRef`, `ShopRef`, `ShopCard` and `Page` become aliases of the `listing` types.
  - `dresses.Filter` embeds `listing.Query`.

`backend/internal/listing/query_test.go`
```go
package listing_test

import (
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

func TestParseQuery(t *testing.T) {
	q, errs := listing.ParseQuery(url.Values{"city": {" mary "}, "price_min": {"100"}, "ids": {"3, 4"}, "per_page": {"500"}})
	if len(errs) > 0 || q.City != "mary" || q.PriceMin != 100 || len(q.IDs) != 2 || q.PerPage != 60 || q.Page != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("q = %+v, errs = %v", q, errs)
	}
	_, errs = listing.ParseQuery(url.Values{
		"price_max": {"-1"}, "page": {"0"}, "ids": {"1,x"}, "q": {strings.Repeat("a", 101)},
	})
	for _, key := range []string{"price_max", "page", "ids", "q"} {
		if errs[key] == "" {
			t.Errorf("no error for %s (all: %v)", key, errs)
		}
	}
	ids := make([]string, 101)
	for i := range ids {
		ids[i] = "1"
	}
	if _, errs := listing.ParseQuery(url.Values{"ids": {strings.Join(ids, ",")}}); errs["ids"] != "too_many" {
		t.Errorf("101 ids: errs = %v", errs)
	}
}

func TestWhereNumbersItsPlaceholders(t *testing.T) {
	w := &listing.Where{}
	w.Add("cat.slug = ?", "cars")
	w.Apply(listing.Query{City: "mary", Q: "ak", Page: 2, PerPage: 24})
	sql := w.SQL()
	for _, want := range []string{"l.is_published AND s.is_published", "cat.slug = $1", "ci.slug = $2", "$3 <% norm(l.title)", "LIKE $4"} {
		if !strings.Contains(sql, want) {
			t.Errorf("missing %q in %s", want, sql)
		}
	}
	if !strings.Contains(w.OrderBy(), "word_similarity($3") {
		t.Errorf("order by = %s", w.OrderBy())
	}
	limit, args := w.Limit(listing.Query{Page: 2, PerPage: 24})
	if limit != " LIMIT $5 OFFSET $6" || len(args) != 6 || args[4] != 24 || args[5] != 24 || len(w.Args()) != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("limit = %q, args = %v", limit, args)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Add the shared package**

`backend/internal/listing/listing.go`
```go
// Package listing holds what every kind of listing shares: the public card types,
// the browse query (city, price, search, ids, paging) and the basic field checks.
package listing

import (
	"math"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

type CityRef struct {
	Slug   string `json:"slug"`
	NameTK string `json:"name_tk"`
	NameRU string `json:"name_ru"`
	NameEN string `json:"name_en"`
}

type ShopRef struct {
	Name string `json:"name"`
	Slug string `json:"slug"`
}

type ShopCard struct {
	Name         string  `json:"name"`
	Slug         string  `json:"slug"`
	Address      string  `json:"address"`
	Phone        string  `json:"phone"`
	InstagramURL *string `json:"instagram_url"`
	TikTokURL    *string `json:"tiktok_url"`
	Logo         *string `json:"logo"`
	City         CityRef `json:"city"`
}

// Page is one page of public list results.
type Page[T any] struct {
	Items   []T `json:"items"`
	Page    int `json:"page"`
	PerPage int `json:"per_page"`
	Total   int `json:"total"`
}

// Basics are the fields every listing has.
type Basics struct {
	ShopID      int64  `json:"shop_id"`
	Title       string `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int    `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool   `json:"price_is_from"`
	Description string `json:"description"`
	IsPublished bool   `json:"is_published"`
}

func (b *Basics) Normalize() {
	b.Title = strings.TrimSpace(b.Title)
	b.Description = strings.TrimSpace(b.Description)
}

// Validate adds the basic fields' errors to errs.
func (b Basics) Validate(errs map[string]string) {
	if b.ShopID <= 0 {
		errs["shop_id"] = "required"
	}
	if b.Title == "" {
		errs["title"] = "required"
	} else if utf8.RuneCountInString(b.Title) > 200 {
		errs["title"] = "too_long"
	}
	if b.PriceManat <= 0 {
		errs["price_manat"] = "must_be_positive"
	} else if b.PriceManat > math.MaxInt32 {
		errs["price_manat"] = "too_large"
	}
}

func NullIfEmpty(s string) *string {
	if s == "" {
		return nil
	}
	return &s
}
```

`backend/internal/listing/query.go`
```go
package listing

import (
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"net/url"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/textnorm"
)

const (
	defaultPerPage = 24
	maxPerPage     = 60
	maxIDs         = 100
	maxPage        = 10000
)

// Query is the part of a public list request that every category shares.
type Query struct {
	City     string
	PriceMin int
	PriceMax int
	Q        string
	IDs      []int64
	ShopSlug string // set internally by the shop page, not a query param
	Page     int
	PerPage  int
}

// ParseQuery reads city, price_min, price_max, q, ids, page and per_page.
func ParseQuery(v url.Values) (Query, map[string]string) {
	q := Query{
		City:    strings.TrimSpace(v.Get("city")),
		Q:       strings.TrimSpace(v.Get("q")),
		Page:    1,
		PerPage: defaultPerPage,
	}
	errs := map[string]string{}
	intParam := func(key string, dst *int, lo, hi int) {
		if s := v.Get(key); s != "" {
			n, err := strconv.Atoi(s)
			if err != nil || n < lo || n > hi {
				errs[key] = "invalid"
				return
			}
			*dst = n
		}
	}
	intParam("price_min", &q.PriceMin, 0, math.MaxInt32)
	intParam("price_max", &q.PriceMax, 0, math.MaxInt32)
	intParam("page", &q.Page, 1, maxPage)
	intParam("per_page", &q.PerPage, 1, math.MaxInt32)
	q.PerPage = min(q.PerPage, maxPerPage)
	if s := v.Get("ids"); s != "" {
		for _, part := range strings.Split(s, ",") {
			n, err := strconv.ParseInt(strings.TrimSpace(part), 10, 64)
			if err != nil || n <= 0 {
				errs["ids"] = "invalid"
				break
			}
			q.IDs = append(q.IDs, n)
		}
		if len(q.IDs) > maxIDs {
			errs["ids"] = "too_many"
		}
	}
	if utf8.RuneCountInString(q.Q) > 100 {
		errs["q"] = "too_long"
	}
	return q, errs
}

// Where builds a public list's WHERE clause over listings l, shops s and cities ci.
// It always requires the listing and its shop to be published.
type Where struct {
	conds  []string
	args   []any
	qParam string
}

// Add appends a condition; each "?" in cond becomes the placeholder of val.
func (w *Where) Add(cond string, val any) {
	w.args = append(w.args, val)
	w.conds = append(w.conds, strings.ReplaceAll(cond, "?", "$"+strconv.Itoa(len(w.args))))
}

// Apply adds the shared query's conditions, including the search.
func (w *Where) Apply(q Query) {
	if q.City != "" {
		w.Add("ci.slug = ?", q.City)
	}
	if q.PriceMin > 0 {
		w.Add("l.price_manat >= ?", q.PriceMin)
	}
	if q.PriceMax > 0 {
		w.Add("l.price_manat <= ?", q.PriceMax)
	}
	if len(q.IDs) > 0 {
		w.Add("l.id = ANY(?)", q.IDs)
	}
	if q.ShopSlug != "" {
		w.Add("s.slug = ?", q.ShopSlug)
	}
	if q.Q != "" {
		qn := textnorm.Norm(q.Q)
		w.args = append(w.args, qn, "%"+textnorm.EscapeLike(qn)+"%")
		w.qParam = "$" + strconv.Itoa(len(w.args)-1)
		like := "$" + strconv.Itoa(len(w.args))
		w.conds = append(w.conds, fmt.Sprintf(`(
			%[1]s <%% norm(l.title) OR %[1]s <%% norm(s.name)
			OR norm(l.title) LIKE %[2]s OR norm(s.name) LIKE %[2]s
			OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shop_aliases a WHERE a.shop_id = s.id
			           AND (%[1]s <%% norm(a.alias) OR norm(a.alias) LIKE %[2]s)))`, w.qParam, like))
	}
}

// SQL is " WHERE …" with the published rules first.
func (w *Where) SQL() string {
	return " WHERE " + strings.Join(append([]string{"l.is_published", "s.is_published"}, w.conds...), " AND ")
}

func (w *Where) Args() []any { return w.args }

// OrderBy ranks search results by how well they match, otherwise newest first.
func (w *Where) OrderBy() string {
	if w.qParam == "" {
		return " ORDER BY l.created_at DESC, l.id DESC"
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf(` ORDER BY GREATEST(
		word_similarity(%[1]s, norm(l.title)),
		word_similarity(%[1]s, norm(s.name)),
		COALESCE((SELECT max(word_similarity(%[1]s, norm(a.alias))) FROM shop_aliases a WHERE a.shop_id = s.id), 0)
	) DESC, l.created_at DESC, l.id DESC`, w.qParam)
}

// Limit is " LIMIT … OFFSET …" for the query's page, with its two args appended.
func (w *Where) Limit(q Query) (string, []any) {
	args := append(append([]any{}, w.args...), q.PerPage, (q.Page-1)*q.PerPage)
	return fmt.Sprintf(" LIMIT $%d OFFSET $%d", len(args)-1, len(args)), args
}
```

`backend/internal/listing/owner.go`
```go
package listing

import (
	"context"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
)

// OwnedBy answers 404 and returns false unless listing id belongs to shopID.
// A shopID of 0 means an admin, who may touch every listing.
func OwnedBy(c *gin.Context, d db.DBTX, id, shopID int64) bool {
	if shopID == 0 {
		return true
	}
	var mine bool
	err := d.QueryRow(c.Request.Context(),
		`SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM listings WHERE id = $1 AND shop_id = $2)`, id, shopID).Scan(&mine)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return false
	}
	if !mine {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.ErrNotFound)
		return false
	}
	return true
}

// CheckShop sets errs["shop_id"] = "invalid" when no shop has that id. It does nothing
// when shop_id already has an error.
func CheckShop(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, shopID int64, errs map[string]string) error {
	if _, bad := errs["shop_id"]; bad {
		return nil
	}
	var exists bool
	if err := d.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM shops WHERE id = $1)`, shopID).Scan(&exists); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !exists {
		errs["shop_id"] = "invalid"
	}
	return nil
}

// ShopIDParam reads the optional ?shop_id= of an admin list (0 when absent). On a bad
// value it answers 400 and returns false.
func ShopIDParam(c *gin.Context) (int64, bool) {
	s := c.Query("shop_id")
	if s == "" {
		return 0, true
	}
	n, err := strconv.ParseInt(s, 10, 64)
	if err != nil || n <= 0 {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(map[string]string{"shop_id": "invalid"}))
		return 0, false
	}
	return n, true
}
```

`backend/internal/dresses/model.go`
```go
package dresses

import (
	"slices"
	"time"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

var (
	Colors      = []string{"white", "ivory", "champagne", "blush", "other"}
	Silhouettes = []string{"ball_gown", "a_line", "mermaid", "straight", "short"}
	SleeveTypes = []string{"long", "short", "none"}
	Sizes       = []string{"XS", "S", "M", "L", "XL", "XXL"}
)

type Input struct {
	listing.Basics
	Color      string   `json:"color"`
	Silhouette string   `json:"silhouette"`
	Sleeves    string   `json:"sleeves"`
	Sizes      []string `json:"sizes"`
}

type Dress struct {
	ID          int64          `json:"id"`
	ShopID      int64          `json:"shop_id"`
	Title       string         `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int            `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool           `json:"price_is_from"`
	Description *string        `json:"description"`
	IsPublished bool           `json:"is_published"`
	Color       string         `json:"color"`
	Silhouette  string         `json:"silhouette"`
	Sleeves     string         `json:"sleeves"`
	Sizes       []string       `json:"sizes"`
	Images      []images.Image `json:"images"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
}

func (in *Input) normalize() { in.Basics.Normalize() }

func (in Input) validate() map[string]string {
	errs := map[string]string{}
	in.Basics.Validate(errs)
	if !slices.Contains(Colors, in.Color) {
		errs["color"] = "invalid"
	}
	if !slices.Contains(Silhouettes, in.Silhouette) {
		errs["silhouette"] = "invalid"
	}
	if !slices.Contains(SleeveTypes, in.Sleeves) {
		errs["sleeves"] = "invalid"
	}
	if len(in.Sizes) == 0 {
		errs["sizes"] = "required"
	}
	for _, s := range in.Sizes {
		if !slices.Contains(Sizes, s) {
			errs["sizes"] = "invalid"
		}
	}
	return errs
}

// canonicalSizes de-duplicates valid sizes and orders them XS..XXL.
func canonicalSizes(in []string) []string {
	out := []string{}
	for _, s := range Sizes {
		if slices.Contains(in, s) {
			out = append(out, s)
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

`backend/internal/dresses/filter.go`
```go
package dresses

import (
	"net/url"
	"slices"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

type Filter struct {
	listing.Query
	Size    string
	Color   string
	Style   string // matches dress_details.silhouette
	Sleeves string
}

func ParseFilter(v url.Values) (Filter, map[string]string) {
	q, errs := listing.ParseQuery(v)
	f := Filter{Query: q, Size: v.Get("size"), Color: v.Get("color"), Style: v.Get("style"), Sleeves: v.Get("sleeves")}
	enum := func(key, val string, allowed []string) {
		if val != "" && !slices.Contains(allowed, val) {
			errs[key] = "invalid"
		}
	}
	enum("size", f.Size, Sizes)
	enum("color", f.Color, Colors)
	enum("style", f.Style, Silhouettes)
	enum("sleeves", f.Sleeves, SleeveTypes)
	return f, errs
}
```

`backend/internal/dresses/public.go`
```go
package dresses

import (
	"context"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

type (
	CityRef  = listing.CityRef
	ShopRef  = listing.ShopRef
	ShopCard = listing.ShopCard
	Page     = listing.Page[ListItem]
)

type ListItem struct {
	ID          int64   `json:"id"`
	Title       string  `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int     `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool    `json:"price_is_from"`
	Photo       *string `json:"photo"`
	Shop        ShopRef `json:"shop"`
	City        CityRef `json:"city"`
}

type PublicDress struct {
	ID          int64          `json:"id"`
	Title       string         `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int            `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool           `json:"price_is_from"`
	Description *string        `json:"description"`
	Color       string         `json:"color"`
	Silhouette  string         `json:"silhouette"`
	Sleeves     string         `json:"sleeves"`
	Sizes       []string       `json:"sizes"`
	Images      []images.Image `json:"images"`
	Shop        ShopCard       `json:"shop"`
}

const publicFrom = `
	FROM listings l
	JOIN dress_details d ON d.listing_id = l.id
	JOIN shops s ON s.id = l.shop_id
	JOIN cities ci ON ci.id = s.city_id`

func where(f Filter) *listing.Where {
	w := &listing.Where{}
	if f.Size != "" {
		w.Add("? = ANY(d.sizes)", f.Size)
	}
	if f.Color != "" {
		w.Add("d.color = ?", f.Color)
	}
	if f.Style != "" {
		w.Add("d.silhouette = ?", f.Style)
	}
	if f.Sleeves != "" {
		w.Add("d.sleeves = ?", f.Sleeves)
	}
	w.Apply(f.Query)
	return w
}

func ListPublic(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, f Filter) (Page, error) {
	w := where(f)
	page := Page{Items: []ListItem{}, Page: f.Page, PerPage: f.PerPage}
	if err := dbx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*)`+publicFrom+w.SQL(), w.Args()...).Scan(&page.Total); err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	limit, args := w.Limit(f.Query)
	sql := `SELECT l.id, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from,
		       (SELECT i.path_small FROM images i WHERE i.listing_id = l.id ORDER BY i.position, i.id LIMIT 1),
		       s.name, s.slug, ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en` +
		publicFrom + w.SQL() + w.OrderBy() + limit
	rows, err := dbx.Query(ctx, sql, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	for rows.Next() {
		var it ListItem
		var photo *string
		if err := rows.Scan(&it.ID, &it.Title, &it.PriceManat, &it.PriceIsFrom, &photo,
			&it.Shop.Name, &it.Shop.Slug, &it.City.Slug, &it.City.NameTK, &it.City.NameRU, &it.City.NameEN); err != nil {
			return Page{}, err
		}
		if photo != nil {
			u := images.URL(*photo)
			it.Photo = &u
		}
		page.Items = append(page.Items, it)
	}
	return page, rows.Err()
}

func GetPublic(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, id int64) (PublicDress, error) {
	var d PublicDress
	var logo *string
	err := dbx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT l.id, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.description,
		       d.color, d.silhouette, d.sleeves, d.sizes,
		       s.name, s.slug, s.address, s.phone, s.instagram_url, s.tiktok_url,
		       (SELECT path_small FROM images WHERE id = s.logo_image_id),
		       ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en`+publicFrom+`
		WHERE l.id = $1 AND l.is_published AND s.is_published`, id).Scan(
		&d.ID, &d.Title, &d.PriceManat, &d.PriceIsFrom, &d.Description,
		&d.Color, &d.Silhouette, &d.Sleeves, &d.Sizes,
		&d.Shop.Name, &d.Shop.Slug, &d.Shop.Address, &d.Shop.Phone, &d.Shop.InstagramURL, &d.Shop.TikTokURL,
		&logo, &d.Shop.City.Slug, &d.Shop.City.NameTK, &d.Shop.City.NameRU, &d.Shop.City.NameEN)
	if err != nil {
		return PublicDress{}, err
	}
	if logo != nil {
		u := images.URL(*logo)
		d.Shop.Logo = &u
	}
	d.Images, err = images.ListForListing(ctx, dbx, id)
	return d, err
}
```

`backend/internal/dresses/handler.go`
```go
package dresses

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

type Handler struct {
	DB      db.DBTX
	Storage images.Storage
	// Scope, when set, limits the admin routes to one shop: it returns the signed-in shop
	// owner's shop id. Admins leave it nil and may touch every shop.
	Scope func(*gin.Context) int64
}

func (h *Handler) scope(c *gin.Context) int64 {
	if h.Scope == nil {
		return 0
	}
	return h.Scope(c)
}

// own answers 404 unless the dress id belongs to the scoped shop (always true for admins).
func (h *Handler) own(c *gin.Context, id int64) bool {
	return listing.OwnedBy(c, h.DB, id, h.scope(c))
}

func (h *Handler) RegisterAdmin(g *gin.RouterGroup) {
	if h.Scope != nil {
		g = g.Group("", httpx.RequireScope(h.Scope))
	}
	g.GET("/dresses", h.listAdmin)
	g.POST("/dresses", h.create)
	g.GET("/dresses/:id", h.getAdmin)
	g.PUT("/dresses/:id", h.update)
	g.DELETE("/dresses/:id", h.delete)
}

func (h *Handler) RegisterPublic(g *gin.RouterGroup) {
	g.GET("/dresses", h.listPublic)
	g.GET("/dresses/:id", h.getPublic)
}

func (h *Handler) listPublic(c *gin.Context) {
	f, errs := ParseFilter(c.Request.URL.Query())
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(errs))
		return
	}
	page, err := ListPublic(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, f)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, page)
}

func (h *Handler) getPublic(c *gin.Context) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	d, err := GetPublic(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, d)
}

func (h *Handler) bindInput(c *gin.Context) (Input, bool) {
	var in Input
	if !httpx.BindJSON(c, &in) {
		return in, false
	}
	if shopID := h.scope(c); shopID != 0 {
		in.ShopID = shopID // a shop owner always saves into their own shop
	}
	in.normalize()
	errs := in.validate()
	if err := listing.CheckShop(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, in.ShopID, errs); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return in, false
	}
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(errs))
		return in, false
	}
	in.Sizes = canonicalSizes(in.Sizes)
	return in, true
}

func (h *Handler) listAdmin(c *gin.Context) {
	shopID, ok := listing.ShopIDParam(c)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	if scoped := h.scope(c); scoped != 0 {
		shopID = scoped
	}
	list, err := ListAdmin(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, shopID)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": list})
}

func (h *Handler) create(c *gin.Context) {
	in, ok := h.bindInput(c)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	ctx := c.Request.Context()
	id, err := Create(ctx, h.DB, in)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	d, err := Get(ctx, h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusCreated, d)
}

func (h *Handler) getAdmin(c *gin.Context) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok || !h.own(c, id) {
		return
	}
	d, err := Get(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, d)
}

func (h *Handler) update(c *gin.Context) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok || !h.own(c, id) {
		return
	}
	in, ok := h.bindInput(c)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	ctx := c.Request.Context()
	if err := Update(ctx, h.DB, id, in); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	d, err := Get(ctx, h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, d)
}

func (h *Handler) delete(c *gin.Context) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok || !h.own(c, id) {
		return
	}
	if err := Delete(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, h.Storage, id); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.Status(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

### Task 2: Categories and their field definitions

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `backend/migrations/00005_categories.sql`
  - `backend/internal/catalog/catalog.go`
  - `backend/internal/catalog/validate.go`
  - `backend/internal/catalog/filter.go`
  - `frontend/src/lib/catalog.json` (generated)
- Test: `backend/internal/catalog/catalog_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `testutil.Tx`.
- Produces (package `catalog`):
  - Field kinds `Text`, `Choice`, `Choices` and `Number`, and filter kinds `FilterEq`, `FilterHas` and `FilterMin`.
  - Types:
    - `Field{Key, Kind, Required, Options, Min, Max, MaxLen, Filter *Filter{Param, Kind}}`;
    - `Category{Slug, OwnPages, Fields}`;
    - `Cond{SQL, Value}`.
  - `var Categories []Category` (dresses first, `OwnPages: true`, no fields) and `Find(slug) (Category, bool)`.
  - `Validate(c, raw map[string]any) (map[string]any, map[string]string)`
  - `ParseFilters(c, url.Values) ([]Cond, map[string]string)`. The SQL uses `l.attributes`, and each `?` is the value's placeholder.
  - The migration adds the four category rows and `listings.attributes jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'`.

```sql
-- +goose Up
INSERT INTO categories (slug, name_tk, name_ru, name_en) VALUES
  ('cars', 'Maşynlar', 'Машины', 'Cars'),
  ('national-clothes', 'Milli eşikler', 'Национальная одежда', 'National clothes'),
  ('tents', 'Çadyrlar', 'Шатры', 'Tents'),
  ('car-decorations', 'Maşyn bezegleri', 'Украшения для машин', 'Car decorations');

-- Each category's own fields (internal/catalog defines them); dresses keep dress_details.
ALTER TABLE listings ADD COLUMN attributes jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}';
CREATE INDEX listings_attributes_idx ON listings USING gin (attributes jsonb_path_ops);

-- +goose Down
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS listings_attributes_idx;
ALTER TABLE listings DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS attributes;
DELETE FROM listings WHERE category_id IN
  (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug IN ('cars', 'national-clothes', 'tents', 'car-decorations'));
DELETE FROM categories WHERE slug IN ('cars', 'national-clothes', 'tents', 'car-decorations');
```

`backend/internal/catalog/catalog_test.go`
```go
package catalog_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"flag"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"reflect"
	"testing"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/catalog"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/testutil"
)

var update = flag.Bool("update", false, "rewrite frontend/src/lib/catalog.json")

const frontendFile = "../../../frontend/src/lib/catalog.json"

func cat(t *testing.T, slug string) catalog.Category {
	t.Helper()
	c, ok := catalog.Find(slug)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("no category %q", slug)
	}
	return c
}

// decode turns a JSON object into what handlers pass to Validate.
func decode(t *testing.T, s string) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	var m map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(s), &m); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return m
}

func TestValidateCleansGoodAttributes(t *testing.T) {
	clean, errs := catalog.Validate(cat(t, "cars"), decode(t,
		`{"brand_model": "  Mercedes S-Class ", "body": "limousine", "color": "white", "seats": 4, "price_unit": "hour"}`))
	want := map[string]any{"brand_model": "Mercedes S-Class", "body": "limousine", "color": "white", "seats": 4, "price_unit": "hour"}
	if len(errs) > 0 || !reflect.DeepEqual(clean, want) {
		t.Fatalf("clean = %v, errs = %v", clean, errs)
	}
	clean, errs = catalog.Validate(cat(t, "national-clothes"), decode(t, `{"for_whom": "bride", "sizes": ["L", "S", "L"]}`))
	if len(errs) > 0 || !reflect.DeepEqual(clean["sizes"], []string{"S", "L"}) {
		t.Fatalf("sizes = %v, errs = %v", clean["sizes"], errs)
	}
	// An empty optional field is dropped.
	clean, errs = catalog.Validate(cat(t, "tents"), decode(t, `{"guests": 150, "area_m2": null}`))
	if len(errs) > 0 || !reflect.DeepEqual(clean, map[string]any{"guests": 150}) {
		t.Fatalf("clean = %v, errs = %v", clean, errs)
	}
}

func TestValidateReportsEveryKindOfError(t *testing.T) {
	_, errs := catalog.Validate(cat(t, "cars"), decode(t,
		`{"brand_model": "`+string(bytes.Repeat([]byte("x"), 101))+`", "body": "truck", "seats": 0, "colour": "white"}`))
	want := map[string]string{
		"attributes.brand_model": "too_long",
		"attributes.body":        "invalid",
		"attributes.color":       "required",
		"attributes.seats":       "must_be_positive",
		"attributes.price_unit":  "required",
		"attributes.colour":      "unknown",
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(errs, want) {
		t.Fatalf("errs = %v\nwant  %v", errs, want)
	}
	_, errs = catalog.Validate(cat(t, "tents"), decode(t, `{"guests": 5001, "area_m2": 1.5}`))
	if errs["attributes.guests"] != "too_large" || errs["attributes.area_m2"] != "invalid" {
		t.Fatalf("errs = %v", errs)
	}
	_, errs = catalog.Validate(cat(t, "national-clothes"), decode(t, `{"for_whom": 3, "sizes": []}`))
	if errs["attributes.for_whom"] != "invalid" || errs["attributes.sizes"] != "required" {
		t.Fatalf("errs = %v", errs)
	}
	_, errs = catalog.Validate(cat(t, "national-clothes"), decode(t, `{"for_whom": "bride", "sizes": ["M", "XXXL"]}`))
	if errs["attributes.sizes"] != "invalid" {
		t.Fatalf("errs = %v", errs)
	}
}

func TestParseFilters(t *testing.T) {
	conds, errs := catalog.ParseFilters(cat(t, "cars"), url.Values{"body": {"suv"}, "seats_min": {"5"}, "price_unit": {"hour"}})
	if len(errs) > 0 || len(conds) != 2 || conds[0].Value != "suv" || conds[1].Value != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("conds = %+v, errs = %v", conds, errs)
	}
	_, errs = catalog.ParseFilters(cat(t, "cars"), url.Values{"body": {"truck"}, "seats_min": {"61"}})
	if errs["body"] != "invalid" || errs["seats_min"] != "invalid" {
		t.Fatalf("errs = %v", errs)
	}
	conds, _ = catalog.ParseFilters(cat(t, "national-clothes"), url.Values{"size": {"M"}})
	if len(conds) != 1 || conds[0].Value != "M" {
		t.Fatalf("conds = %+v", conds)
	}
}

func TestEveryCategoryIsInTheDatabase(t *testing.T) {
	tx := testutil.Tx(t)
	for _, c := range catalog.Categories {
		var ok bool
		if err := tx.QueryRow(context.Background(), `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM categories WHERE slug = $1)`, c.Slug).Scan(&ok); err != nil || !ok {
			t.Errorf("category %q missing from the categories table (err %v)", c.Slug, err)
		}
	}
}

// The frontend builds its forms and filters from this file. Run
// `go test ./internal/catalog/ -update` after changing the definitions.
func TestFrontendCatalogIsUpToDate(t *testing.T) {
	want, err := json.MarshalIndent(map[string]any{"categories": catalog.Categories}, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want = append(want, '\n')
	if *update {
		if err := os.WriteFile(frontendFile, want, 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	got, err := os.ReadFile(frontendFile)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%v (run go test ./internal/catalog/ -update)", err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Fatal("frontend/src/lib/catalog.json is out of date: run go test ./internal/catalog/ -update")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Define the categories, validation and filters**

`backend/internal/catalog/catalog.go`
```go
// Package catalog defines the rental categories and each one's own fields.
//
// This list is the one place to change when shops ask for different fields: it drives
// validation, the public filters, and (through frontend/src/lib/catalog.json, which
// TestFrontendCatalogIsUpToDate regenerates with -update) the forms and filter panels.
// Every key and option also needs texts in the frontend i18n files.
package catalog

// Field kinds.
const (
	Text    = "text"    // a string up to MaxLen characters
	Choice  = "choice"  // one of Options
	Choices = "choices" // one or more of Options, stored in Options order
	Number  = "number"  // a whole number from Min to Max
)

// How a field filters the public list.
const (
	FilterEq  = "eq"  // param=<option>: the field equals it
	FilterHas = "has" // param=<option>: the field's choices include it
	FilterMin = "min" // param=<n>: the field is at least n
)

type Filter struct {
	Param string `json:"param"`
	Kind  string `json:"kind"`
}

type Field struct {
	Key      string   `json:"key"`
	Kind     string   `json:"kind"`
	Required bool     `json:"required"`
	Options  []string `json:"options,omitempty"`
	Min      int      `json:"min,omitempty"`
	Max      int      `json:"max,omitempty"`
	MaxLen   int      `json:"max_len,omitempty"`
	Filter   *Filter  `json:"filter,omitempty"`
}

type Category struct {
	Slug string `json:"slug"`
	// OwnPages: dresses keep their own pages, API and dress_details; Fields is empty.
	OwnPages bool    `json:"own_pages"`
	Fields   []Field `json:"fields"`
}

var sizes = []string{"XS", "S", "M", "L", "XL", "XXL"}

// Categories in display order. Slugs match the categories table.
var Categories = []Category{
	{Slug: "dresses", OwnPages: true, Fields: []Field{}},
	{Slug: "cars", Fields: []Field{
		{Key: "brand_model", Kind: Text, Required: true, MaxLen: 100},
		{Key: "body", Kind: Choice, Required: true, Options: []string{"sedan", "suv", "limousine", "minivan"},
			Filter: &Filter{Param: "body", Kind: FilterEq}},
		{Key: "color", Kind: Choice, Required: true, Options: []string{"white", "black", "silver", "other"},
			Filter: &Filter{Param: "color", Kind: FilterEq}},
		{Key: "seats", Kind: Number, Min: 1, Max: 60, Filter: &Filter{Param: "seats_min", Kind: FilterMin}},
		{Key: "price_unit", Kind: Choice, Required: true, Options: []string{"hour", "day"}},
	}},
	{Slug: "national-clothes", Fields: []Field{
		{Key: "for_whom", Kind: Choice, Required: true, Options: []string{"bride", "groom", "woman", "man", "child"},
			Filter: &Filter{Param: "for_whom", Kind: FilterEq}},
		{Key: "sizes", Kind: Choices, Required: true, Options: sizes, Filter: &Filter{Param: "size", Kind: FilterHas}},
	}},
	{Slug: "tents", Fields: []Field{
		{Key: "guests", Kind: Number, Required: true, Min: 1, Max: 5000,
			Filter: &Filter{Param: "guests_min", Kind: FilterMin}},
		{Key: "area_m2", Kind: Number, Min: 1, Max: 10000},
	}},
	{Slug: "car-decorations", Fields: []Field{
		{Key: "color", Kind: Choice, Required: true, Options: []string{"white", "red", "pink", "gold", "other"},
			Filter: &Filter{Param: "color", Kind: FilterEq}},
	}},
}

// Find returns the category with this slug.
func Find(slug string) (Category, bool) {
	for _, c := range Categories {
		if c.Slug == slug {
			return c, true
		}
	}
	return Category{}, false
}
```

`backend/internal/catalog/validate.go`
```go
package catalog

import (
	"math"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

// Validate checks raw (decoded JSON) against the category's fields. It returns the
// clean attributes (trimmed text, whole numbers, choices in Options order, empty
// optional fields dropped) and field errors keyed "attributes.<key>".
func Validate(c Category, raw map[string]any) (map[string]any, map[string]string) {
	clean := map[string]any{}
	errs := map[string]string{}
	known := map[string]bool{}
	for _, f := range c.Fields {
		known[f.Key] = true
		v, present := raw[f.Key]
		value, code := check(f, v, present)
		if code != "" {
			errs["attributes."+f.Key] = code
		} else if value != nil {
			clean[f.Key] = value
		}
	}
	for key := range raw {
		if !known[key] {
			errs["attributes."+key] = "unknown"
		}
	}
	return clean, errs
}

// check returns the clean value (nil when empty) or an error code.
func check(f Field, v any, present bool) (any, string) {
	empty := !present || v == nil
	switch f.Kind {
	case Text:
		s, ok := v.(string)
		if !empty && !ok {
			return nil, "invalid"
		}
		s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
		if s == "" {
			return nil, required(f)
		}
		if utf8.RuneCountInString(s) > f.MaxLen {
			return nil, "too_long"
		}
		return s, ""
	case Choice:
		s, ok := v.(string)
		if !empty && !ok {
			return nil, "invalid"
		}
		if s == "" {
			return nil, required(f)
		}
		if !slices.Contains(f.Options, s) {
			return nil, "invalid"
		}
		return s, ""
	case Choices:
		list, ok := v.([]any)
		if !empty && !ok {
			return nil, "invalid"
		}
		picked := map[string]bool{}
		for _, item := range list {
			s, ok := item.(string)
			if !ok || !slices.Contains(f.Options, s) {
				return nil, "invalid"
			}
			picked[s] = true
		}
		out := []string{}
		for _, o := range f.Options {
			if picked[o] {
				out = append(out, o)
			}
		}
		if len(out) == 0 {
			return nil, required(f)
		}
		return out, ""
	case Number:
		if empty {
			return nil, required(f)
		}
		n, ok := v.(float64)
		if !ok || n != math.Trunc(n) {
			return nil, "invalid"
		}
		if n < float64(f.Min) {
			return nil, "must_be_positive"
		}
		if n > float64(f.Max) {
			return nil, "too_large"
		}
		return int(n), ""
	}
	return nil, "invalid"
}

func required(f Field) string {
	if f.Required {
		return "required"
	}
	return ""
}
```

`backend/internal/catalog/filter.go`
```go
package catalog

import (
	"net/url"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
)

// Cond is one SQL condition on l.attributes, with "?" standing for Value.
type Cond struct {
	SQL   string
	Value any
}

// ParseFilters reads the category's filter params from v. Field keys come from the
// definitions above, never from the request, so they are safe to put in the SQL.
func ParseFilters(c Category, v url.Values) ([]Cond, map[string]string) {
	var conds []Cond
	errs := map[string]string{}
	for _, f := range c.Fields {
		if f.Filter == nil {
			continue
		}
		s := v.Get(f.Filter.Param)
		if s == "" {
			continue
		}
		switch f.Filter.Kind {
		case FilterEq:
			if !slices.Contains(f.Options, s) {
				errs[f.Filter.Param] = "invalid"
				continue
			}
			conds = append(conds, Cond{SQL: "l.attributes @> jsonb_build_object('" + f.Key + "', ?::text)", Value: s})
		case FilterHas:
			if !slices.Contains(f.Options, s) {
				errs[f.Filter.Param] = "invalid"
				continue
			}
			conds = append(conds, Cond{
				SQL: "l.attributes @> jsonb_build_object('" + f.Key + "', jsonb_build_array(?::text))", Value: s})
		case FilterMin:
			n, err := strconv.Atoi(s)
			if err != nil || n < f.Min || n > f.Max {
				errs[f.Filter.Param] = "invalid"
				continue
			}
			conds = append(conds, Cond{SQL: "(l.attributes->>'" + f.Key + "')::int >= ?", Value: n})
		}
	}
	return conds, errs
}
```

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `backend/internal/items/model.go`
  - `backend/internal/items/filter.go`
  - `backend/internal/items/public.go`
  - `backend/internal/items/repo.go`

- Modify:
  - `backend/internal/testutil/fixtures.go`
  - `backend/internal/dresses/repo.go`
- Test:
  - `backend/internal/items/items_test.go`
  - `backend/internal/dresses/admin_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - from Task 1: `listing.*`;
  - from Task 2: `catalog.Find`, `catalog.Validate` and `catalog.ParseFilters`;
  - existing: `images.ListForListing`, `images.PathsForListing`, `images.DeleteFiles`, `images.URL` and `httpx.RequireScope`.
- Produces (package `items`):
  - Types:
    - `Input{listing.Basics; Category; Attributes}`;
    - `Item` (admin view, with `Images`);
    - `Summary` (an admin or owner list row, any category, with `Photo`);
    - `ListItem` (public: `id, category, title, price_manat, price_is_from, attributes, photo, shop, city`);
    - `Page = listing.Page[ListItem]`;
    - `PublicItem` (with `description`, `images` and `shop` ShopCard);
    - `Filter{listing.Query; Category}`.
  - Functions: `ParseFilter(url.Values)`, `ListPublic`, `GetPublic`, `Create`, `Update`, `Get`, `ListAdmin(ctx, d, shopID, category)` and `Delete`.
  - `Handler{DB, Storage, Scope}`:
    - `RegisterPublic(g)` adds `GET /items` and `GET /items/:id`;
    - `RegisterAdmin(g)` adds `GET/POST /items` and `GET/PUT/DELETE /items/:id`, behind `httpx.RequireScope` when `Scope` is set.
  - `testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID, Category, Title, Price, Attributes, Hidden}` and `testutil.InsertItem(t, d, f) int64`.
  - `dresses.Delete` now deletes only dresses.

```go
	Attributes map[string]any // stored as given, not validated

}

func InsertItem(t *testing.T, d db.DBTX, f ItemFixture) int64 {
	t.Helper()
	if f.Title == "" {
		f.Title = "Test item"
	}
	if f.Price == 0 {
		f.Price = 1000
	}
	if f.Attributes == nil {
		f.Attributes = map[string]any{}
	}
	var id int64
	err := d.QueryRow(context.Background(), `
		INSERT INTO listings (shop_id, category_id, title, price_manat, is_published, attributes)
		VALUES ($1, (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = $2), $3, $4, $5, $6)
		RETURNING id`, f.ShopID, f.Category, f.Title, f.Price, !f.Hidden, f.Attributes).Scan(&id)

	}
	return id
```

```go
package items_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"testing"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/items"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/testutil"
)

type env struct {
	r  *gin.Engine
	tx pgx.Tx
}

func setup(t *testing.T) env {
	e := env{r: gin.New(), tx: testutil.Tx(t)}
	h := &items.Handler{DB: e.tx, Storage: images.LocalStorage{Dir: t.TempDir()}}
	h.RegisterPublic(e.r.Group("/api"))
	h.RegisterAdmin(e.r.Group("/api/admin"))
	return e
}

func car(shopID int64) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		"shop_id": shopID, "category": "cars", "title": "Ak Mercedes", "price_manat": 800, "is_published": true,
		"attributes": map[string]any{"brand_model": "Mercedes S-Class", "body": "sedan", "color": "white", "price_unit": "hour"},
	}
}

func path(id int64) string { return "/api/admin/items/" + strconv.FormatInt(id, 10) }

func TestCreateReadUpdateDelete(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/items", car(shop.ID), "")
	if rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("create: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	it := testutil.Decode[items.Item](t, rec)
	if it.Category != "cars" || it.ShopID != shop.ID || it.Attributes["body"] != "sedan" || it.Images == nil {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", it)
	}
	// Update: category cannot change, attributes are re-checked against the stored one.
	body := car(shop.ID)
	body["category"] = "tents"
	body["attributes"].(map[string]any)["body"] = "limousine"
	body["attributes"].(map[string]any)["seats"] = 4
	rec = testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, path(it.ID), body, "")
	got := testutil.Decode[items.Item](t, rec)
	if rec.Code != http.StatusOK || got.Category != "cars" || got.Attributes["body"] != "limousine" || got.Attributes["seats"] != float64(4) {
		t.Fatalf("update: %d %+v", rec.Code, got)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path(it.ID), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("get: %d", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodDelete, path(it.ID), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatalf("delete: %d", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path(it.ID), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("get after delete: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestValidation(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	cases := []struct {
		name  string
		edit  func(map[string]any)
		field string
		code  string
	}{
		{"no category", func(b map[string]any) { delete(b, "category") }, "category", "required"},
		{"unknown category", func(b map[string]any) { b["category"] = "boats" }, "category", "invalid"},
		{"dresses go through /dresses", func(b map[string]any) { b["category"] = "dresses" }, "category", "invalid"},
		{"bad shop", func(b map[string]any) { b["shop_id"] = 999999999 }, "shop_id", "invalid"},
		{"no title", func(b map[string]any) { b["title"] = " " }, "title", "required"},
		{"no price", func(b map[string]any) { b["price_manat"] = 0 }, "price_manat", "must_be_positive"},
		{"missing attribute", func(b map[string]any) { delete(b["attributes"].(map[string]any), "body") }, "attributes.body", "required"},
		{"bad attribute", func(b map[string]any) { b["attributes"].(map[string]any)["color"] = "green" }, "attributes.color", "invalid"},
		{"unknown attribute", func(b map[string]any) { b["attributes"].(map[string]any)["wheels"] = 4 }, "attributes.wheels", "unknown"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		body := car(shop.ID)
		tc.edit(body)
		rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/items", body, "")
		got := testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, rec)
		if rec.Code != http.StatusBadRequest || got.Error.Fields[tc.field] != tc.code {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d %v, want %s=%s", tc.name, rec.Code, got.Error.Fields, tc.field, tc.code)
		}
	}
}

func TestDressesAreNotItems(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	dress := testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID})
	for _, method := range []string{http.MethodGet, http.MethodPut, http.MethodDelete} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, method, path(dress), car(shop.ID), ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s dress on /items: %d, want 404", method, rec.Code)
		}
	}
	// The admin list still shows every category, dresses included.
	list := testutil.Decode[struct{ Items []items.Summary }](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/admin/items?shop_id="+strconv.FormatInt(shop.ID, 10), nil, ""))
	if len(list.Items) != 1 || list.Items[0].Category != "dresses" {
		t.Fatalf("admin list = %+v", list.Items)
	}
}

type seeded struct{ limo, suv, tent, gown, robe int64 }

func seed(t *testing.T, tx pgx.Tx) seeded {
	a := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Ak Gelin Wedding House"})
	b := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Toý Maşyn", CitySlug: "mary"})
	hidden := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Hidden: true})
	var s seeded
	s.gown = testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: a.ID, Title: "Princess", Price: 1500})
	s.limo = testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: b.ID, Category: "cars", Title: "Lincoln limuzin", Price: 900,
		Attributes: map[string]any{"brand_model": "Lincoln", "body": "limousine", "color": "white", "seats": 8, "price_unit": "hour"}})
	s.suv = testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: a.ID, Category: "cars", Title: "Gara Lexus", Price: 600,
		Attributes: map[string]any{"brand_model": "Lexus LX", "body": "suv", "color": "black", "seats": 4, "price_unit": "day"}})
	s.tent = testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: b.ID, Category: "tents", Title: "Uly çadyr", Price: 5000,
		Attributes: map[string]any{"guests": 300}})
	s.robe = testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: a.ID, Category: "national-clothes", Title: "Gelin kürte", Price: 400,
		Attributes: map[string]any{"for_whom": "bride", "sizes": []string{"S", "M"}}})
	testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: a.ID, Category: "cars", Title: "Draft car", Hidden: true,
		Attributes: map[string]any{"body": "sedan"}})
	testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: hidden.ID, Category: "tents", Title: "Hidden tent",
		Attributes: map[string]any{"guests": 1000}})
	return s
}

func ids(p items.Page) []int64 {
	out := []int64{}
	for _, it := range p.Items {
		out = append(out, it.ID)
	}
	return out
}

func TestListPublic(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	s := seed(t, e.tx)
	cases := map[string][]int64{
		"":                                       {s.robe, s.tent, s.suv, s.limo, s.gown},
		"category=cars":                          {s.suv, s.limo},
		"category=cars&body=limousine":           {s.limo},
		"category=cars&color=black":              {s.suv},
		"category=cars&seats_min=5":              {s.limo},
		"category=cars&seats_min=8":              {s.limo},
		"category=cars&seats_min=9":              {},
		"category=tents&guests_min=300":          {s.tent},
		"category=tents&guests_min=301":          {},
		"category=national-clothes&size=M":       {s.robe},
		"category=national-clothes&size=L":       {},
		"category=dresses":                       {s.gown},
		"city=mary":                              {s.tent, s.limo},
		"price_max=900":                          {s.robe, s.suv, s.limo},
		"q=ak+gelyn":                             {s.robe, s.suv, s.gown}, // shop name, with a typo
		"q=limuzin":                              {s.limo},
		fmt.Sprintf("ids=%d,%d", s.gown, s.tent): {s.tent, s.gown},
	}
	for query, want := range cases {
		rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/items?"+query, nil, "")
		if rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
			t.Fatalf("%q: status = %d: %s", query, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
		p := testutil.Decode[items.Page](t, rec)
		got := ids(p)
		if query == "q=ak+gelyn" {
			slices.Sort(got) // ranked by match, not by date
			slices.Sort(want)
		}
		if !slices.Equal(got, want) || p.Total != len(want) {
			t.Errorf("%q: ids = %v total = %d, want %v", query, got, p.Total, want)
		}
	}
	for _, query := range []string{"category=boats", "category=cars&body=truck", "category=tents&guests_min=0"} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/items?"+query, nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
			t.Errorf("%q: status = %d, want 400", query, rec.Code)
		}
	}
}

func TestGetPublic(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	s := seed(t, e.tx)
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/items/"+strconv.FormatInt(s.limo, 10), nil, "")
	it := testutil.Decode[items.PublicItem](t, rec)
	if rec.Code != http.StatusOK || it.Category != "cars" || it.Attributes["price_unit"] != "hour" || it.Shop.City.Slug != "mary" {
		t.Fatalf("limo: %d %+v", rec.Code, it)
	}
	dress := testutil.Decode[items.PublicItem](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/items/"+strconv.FormatInt(s.gown, 10), nil, ""))
	if dress.Category != "dresses" || len(dress.Attributes) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("dress: %+v", dress)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/items/999999999", nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("missing: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}
```

```go
package dresses_test

type env struct {

}

func setup(t *testing.T) env {
	e := env{r: gin.New(), tx: testutil.Tx(t), dir: t.TempDir()}
	h := &dresses.Handler{DB: e.tx, Storage: images.LocalStorage{Dir: e.dir}}

	h.RegisterPublic(e.r.Group("/api"))

func adminPath(id int64) string { return "/api/admin/dresses/" + strconv.FormatInt(id, 10) }

func validDress(shopID int64) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		"shop_id": shopID, "title": " Princess A-112 ", "price_manat": 1500, "price_is_from": true,
		"color": "ivory", "silhouette": "ball_gown", "sleeves": "long", "sizes": []string{"M", "S", "M"},
	}

	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/dresses", validDress(shop.ID), "")
	if rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d: %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	d := testutil.Decode[dresses.Dress](t, rec)
	if d.Title != "Princess A-112" || d.PriceManat != 1500 || !d.PriceIsFrom || d.Color != "ivory" || d.IsPublished {
		t.Fatalf("dress = %+v", d)
	}
	if !slices.Equal(d.Sizes, []string{"S", "M"}) || d.Images == nil || len(d.Images) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("sizes = %q images = %v", d.Sizes, d.Images)
	}
}

func TestCreateDressValidation(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	body := testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/dresses", map[string]any{}, ""))
	want := map[string]string{
		"shop_id": "required", "title": "required", "price_manat": "must_be_positive",
		"color": "invalid", "silhouette": "invalid", "sleeves": "invalid", "sizes": "required",
	}
	for f, v := range want {
		if body.Error.Fields[f] != v {
			t.Errorf("fields[%s] = %q, want %q", f, body.Error.Fields[f], v)
		}
	}
	huge := validDress(1)
	huge["price_manat"] = 2147483648
	body = testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/dresses", huge, ""))
	if body.Error.Fields["price_manat"] != "too_large" {
		t.Errorf("price overflow: fields = %v", body.Error.Fields)
	}
	in := validDress(999999999)
	in["sizes"] = []string{"M", "XXXL"}
	body = testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/dresses", in, ""))
	if body.Error.Fields["shop_id"] != "invalid" || body.Error.Fields["sizes"] != "invalid" {
		t.Fatalf("fields = %v", body.Error.Fields)
	}
}

func TestUpdateDress(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	created := testutil.Decode[dresses.Dress](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/admin/dresses", validDress(shop.ID), ""))
	in := validDress(shop.ID)
	in["price_manat"] = 2000
	in["color"] = "blush"
	in["is_published"] = true
	in["description"] = "Lace, long train"
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, adminPath(created.ID), in, "")
	if rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d: %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	d := testutil.Decode[dresses.Dress](t, rec)
	if d.PriceManat != 2000 || d.Color != "blush" || !d.IsPublished || d.Description == nil || *d.Description != "Lace, long train" {
		t.Fatalf("dress = %+v", d)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, adminPath(999999999), in, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("missing: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestListAdminFiltersByShop(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	a := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	b := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	da := testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: a.ID, Hidden: true})
	testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: b.ID})
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/admin/dresses?shop_id="+strconv.FormatInt(a.ID, 10), nil, "")
	list := testutil.Decode[struct{ Items []dresses.Dress }](t, rec)
	if len(list.Items) != 1 || list.Items[0].ID != da {
		t.Fatalf("items = %+v", list.Items)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/admin/dresses?shop_id=abc", nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("bad shop_id: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestDeleteDressRemovesFiles(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	id := testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID})
	testutil.InsertImage(t, e.tx, id, 0, "d")
	_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(e.dir, "d_s.jpg"), []byte("x"), 0o644)
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodDelete, adminPath(id), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d", rec.Code)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(e.dir, "d_s.jpg")); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatal("file still exists")
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, adminPath(id), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("get after delete: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

// The dress routes touch dresses only; other categories have /items.
func TestDressRoutesIgnoreOtherCategories(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	tent := testutil.InsertItem(t, e.tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Category: "tents", Attributes: map[string]any{"guests": 100}})
	for _, method := range []string{http.MethodGet, http.MethodDelete} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, method, adminPath(tent), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s tent on /dresses: %d, want 404", method, rec.Code)
		}
	}
	var n int
	if err := e.tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `SELECT count(*) FROM listings WHERE id = $1`, tent).Scan(&n); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("tent rows = %d (err %v), want 1", n, err)
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/dresses/repo.go`
```go
package dresses

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

const selectAdmin = `
	SELECT l.id, l.shop_id, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.description, l.is_published,
	       d.color, d.silhouette, d.sleeves, d.sizes, l.created_at, l.updated_at
	FROM listings l
	JOIN dress_details d ON d.listing_id = l.id`

func scanAdmin(row pgx.Row) (Dress, error) {
	var d Dress
	err := row.Scan(&d.ID, &d.ShopID, &d.Title, &d.PriceManat, &d.PriceIsFrom, &d.Description, &d.IsPublished,
		&d.Color, &d.Silhouette, &d.Sleeves, &d.Sizes, &d.CreatedAt, &d.UpdatedAt)
	return d, err
}

func Create(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, in Input) (int64, error) {
	var id int64
	err := db.InTx(ctx, d, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO listings (shop_id, category_id, title, price_manat, price_is_from, description, is_published)
			VALUES ($1, (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = 'dresses'), $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
			RETURNING id`,
			in.ShopID, in.Title, in.PriceManat, in.PriceIsFrom, listing.NullIfEmpty(in.Description), in.IsPublished).Scan(&id)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err = tx.Exec(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO dress_details (listing_id, color, silhouette, sleeves, sizes)
			VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`, id, in.Color, in.Silhouette, in.Sleeves, in.Sizes)
		return err
	})
	return id, err
}

func Update(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, id int64, in Input) error {
	return db.InTx(ctx, d, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		tag, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
			UPDATE listings SET shop_id = $2, title = $3, price_manat = $4, price_is_from = $5,
			       description = $6, is_published = $7, updated_at = now()
			WHERE id = $1 AND category_id = (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = 'dresses')`,
			id, in.ShopID, in.Title, in.PriceManat, in.PriceIsFrom, listing.NullIfEmpty(in.Description), in.IsPublished)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
			return pgx.ErrNoRows
		}
		_, err = tx.Exec(ctx, `
			UPDATE dress_details SET color = $2, silhouette = $3, sleeves = $4, sizes = $5, updated_at = now()
			WHERE listing_id = $1`, id, in.Color, in.Silhouette, in.Sleeves, in.Sizes)
		return err
	})
}

func Get(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, id int64) (Dress, error) {
	dress, err := scanAdmin(d.QueryRow(ctx, selectAdmin+` WHERE l.id = $1`, id))
	if err != nil {
		return Dress{}, err
	}
	dress.Images, err = images.ListForListing(ctx, d, id)
	return dress, err
}

// ListAdmin returns all dresses (published or not), optionally for one shop.
func ListAdmin(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, shopID int64) ([]Dress, error) {
	rows, err := d.Query(ctx, selectAdmin+` WHERE ($1::bigint = 0 OR l.shop_id = $1) ORDER BY l.created_at DESC, l.id DESC`, shopID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	list := []Dress{}
	for rows.Next() {
		dress, err := scanAdmin(rows)
		if err != nil {
			rows.Close()
			return nil, err
		}
		list = append(list, dress)
	}
	rows.Close()
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	for i := range list {
		if list[i].Images, err = images.ListForListing(ctx, d, list[i].ID); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return list, nil
}

func Delete(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, st images.Storage, id int64) error {
	paths, err := images.PathsForListing(ctx, d, id)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tag, err := d.Exec(ctx, `
		DELETE FROM listings WHERE id = $1 AND category_id = (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = 'dresses')`, id)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return pgx.ErrNoRows
	}
	images.DeleteFiles(st, paths)
	return nil
}
```

`backend/internal/items/model.go`
```go
// Package items serves every category except dresses (which keep their own package):
// cars, national clothes, tents and car decorations, with fields from internal/catalog.
// Its public list and search cover all categories, dresses included.
package items

import (
	"time"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

// Input is the admin or owner request body. On update, Category is ignored: an item
// keeps the category it was created with.
type Input struct {
	listing.Basics
	Category   string         `json:"category"`
	Attributes map[string]any `json:"attributes"`
}

// Item is one non-dress listing as admins and owners edit it.
type Item struct {
	ID          int64          `json:"id"`
	ShopID      int64          `json:"shop_id"`
	Category    string         `json:"category"`
	Title       string         `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int            `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool           `json:"price_is_from"`
	Description *string        `json:"description"`
	IsPublished bool           `json:"is_published"`
	Attributes  map[string]any `json:"attributes"`
	Images      []images.Image `json:"images"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
}

// Summary is one row of the admin and owner listing lists, in any category.
type Summary struct {
	ID          int64          `json:"id"`
	ShopID      int64          `json:"shop_id"`
	Category    string         `json:"category"`
	Title       string         `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int            `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool           `json:"price_is_from"`
	IsPublished bool           `json:"is_published"`
	Attributes  map[string]any `json:"attributes"`
	Photo       *string        `json:"photo"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
}

// ListItem is one public list result, in any category (dresses have empty attributes).
type ListItem struct {
	ID          int64           `json:"id"`
	Category    string          `json:"category"`
	Title       string          `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int             `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool            `json:"price_is_from"`
	Attributes  map[string]any  `json:"attributes"`
	Photo       *string         `json:"photo"`
	Shop        listing.ShopRef `json:"shop"`
	City        listing.CityRef `json:"city"`
}

type Page = listing.Page[ListItem]

// PublicItem is one published listing for its public page.
type PublicItem struct {
	ID          int64            `json:"id"`
	Category    string           `json:"category"`
	Title       string           `json:"title"`
	PriceManat  int              `json:"price_manat"`
	PriceIsFrom bool             `json:"price_is_from"`
	Description *string          `json:"description"`
	Attributes  map[string]any   `json:"attributes"`
	Images      []images.Image   `json:"images"`
	Shop        listing.ShopCard `json:"shop"`
}
```

`backend/internal/items/filter.go`
```go
package items

import (
	"net/url"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/catalog"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

type Filter struct {
	listing.Query
	Category string
	attrs    []catalog.Cond
}

// ParseFilter reads the shared list params, category, and that category's own filters.
func ParseFilter(v url.Values) (Filter, map[string]string) {
	q, errs := listing.ParseQuery(v)
	f := Filter{Query: q, Category: v.Get("category")}
	if f.Category == "" {
		return f, errs
	}
	c, ok := catalog.Find(f.Category)
	if !ok {
		errs["category"] = "invalid"
		return f, errs
	}
	conds, attrErrs := catalog.ParseFilters(c, v)
	f.attrs = conds
	for k, code := range attrErrs {
		errs[k] = code
	}
	return f, errs
}
```

`backend/internal/items/public.go`
```go
package items

import (
	"context"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

const publicFrom = `
	FROM listings l
	JOIN categories cat ON cat.id = l.category_id
	JOIN shops s ON s.id = l.shop_id
	JOIN cities ci ON ci.id = s.city_id`

func where(f Filter) *listing.Where {
	w := &listing.Where{}
	if f.Category != "" {
		w.Add("cat.slug = ?", f.Category)
	}
	for _, c := range f.attrs {
		w.Add(c.SQL, c.Value)
	}
	w.Apply(f.Query)
	return w
}

// ListPublic returns a page of published listings from published shops, in every
// category or one.
func ListPublic(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, f Filter) (Page, error) {
	w := where(f)
	page := Page{Items: []ListItem{}, Page: f.Page, PerPage: f.PerPage}
	if err := dbx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*)`+publicFrom+w.SQL(), w.Args()...).Scan(&page.Total); err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	limit, args := w.Limit(f.Query)
	rows, err := dbx.Query(ctx, `SELECT l.id, cat.slug, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.attributes,
		       (SELECT i.path_small FROM images i WHERE i.listing_id = l.id ORDER BY i.position, i.id LIMIT 1),
		       s.name, s.slug, ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en`+
		publicFrom+w.SQL()+w.OrderBy()+limit, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	for rows.Next() {
		var it ListItem
		var photo *string
		if err := rows.Scan(&it.ID, &it.Category, &it.Title, &it.PriceManat, &it.PriceIsFrom, &it.Attributes, &photo,
			&it.Shop.Name, &it.Shop.Slug, &it.City.Slug, &it.City.NameTK, &it.City.NameRU, &it.City.NameEN); err != nil {
			return Page{}, err
		}
		it.Photo = photoURL(photo)
		page.Items = append(page.Items, it)
	}
	return page, rows.Err()
}

// GetPublic returns one published listing of any category; pgx.ErrNoRows otherwise.
func GetPublic(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, id int64) (PublicItem, error) {
	var it PublicItem
	var logo *string
	err := dbx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT l.id, cat.slug, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.description, l.attributes,
		       s.name, s.slug, s.address, s.phone, s.instagram_url, s.tiktok_url,
		       (SELECT path_small FROM images WHERE id = s.logo_image_id),
		       ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en`+publicFrom+`
		WHERE l.id = $1 AND l.is_published AND s.is_published`, id).Scan(
		&it.ID, &it.Category, &it.Title, &it.PriceManat, &it.PriceIsFrom, &it.Description, &it.Attributes,
		&it.Shop.Name, &it.Shop.Slug, &it.Shop.Address, &it.Shop.Phone, &it.Shop.InstagramURL, &it.Shop.TikTokURL,
		&logo, &it.Shop.City.Slug, &it.Shop.City.NameTK, &it.Shop.City.NameRU, &it.Shop.City.NameEN)
	if err != nil {
		return PublicItem{}, err
	}
	it.Shop.Logo = photoURL(logo)
	it.Images, err = images.ListForListing(ctx, dbx, id)
	return it, err
}

func photoURL(path *string) *string {
	if path == nil {
		return nil
	}
	u := images.URL(*path)
	return &u
}
```

`backend/internal/items/repo.go`
```go
package items

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

// notDress limits a statement to the categories this package manages.
const notDress = `l.category_id <> (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = 'dresses')`

const selectItem = `
	SELECT l.id, l.shop_id, cat.slug, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.description, l.is_published,
	       l.attributes, l.created_at, l.updated_at
	FROM listings l JOIN categories cat ON cat.id = l.category_id`

func Create(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, in Input) (int64, error) {
	var id int64
	err := d.QueryRow(ctx, `
		INSERT INTO listings (shop_id, category_id, title, price_manat, price_is_from, description, is_published, attributes)
		VALUES ($1, (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = $2), $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)
		RETURNING id`,
		in.ShopID, in.Category, in.Title, in.PriceManat, in.PriceIsFrom, listing.NullIfEmpty(in.Description),
		in.IsPublished, in.Attributes).Scan(&id)
	return id, err
}

// Update saves everything but the category; pgx.ErrNoRows if id is no such item.
func Update(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, id int64, in Input) error {
	tag, err := d.Exec(ctx, `
		UPDATE listings l SET shop_id = $2, title = $3, price_manat = $4, price_is_from = $5,
		       description = $6, is_published = $7, attributes = $8, updated_at = now()
		WHERE l.id = $1 AND `+notDress,
		id, in.ShopID, in.Title, in.PriceManat, in.PriceIsFrom, listing.NullIfEmpty(in.Description), in.IsPublished,
		in.Attributes)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return pgx.ErrNoRows
	}
	return nil
}

// Get returns one non-dress item with its images; pgx.ErrNoRows for a dress or no row.
func Get(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, id int64) (Item, error) {
	var it Item
	err := d.QueryRow(ctx, selectItem+` WHERE l.id = $1 AND `+notDress, id).Scan(
		&it.ID, &it.ShopID, &it.Category, &it.Title, &it.PriceManat, &it.PriceIsFrom, &it.Description,
		&it.IsPublished, &it.Attributes, &it.CreatedAt, &it.UpdatedAt)
	if err != nil {
		return Item{}, err
	}
	it.Images, err = images.ListForListing(ctx, d, id)
	return it, err
}

// ListAdmin returns every listing in every category (published or not), newest first,
// optionally for one shop and one category.
func ListAdmin(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, shopID int64, category string) ([]Summary, error) {
	rows, err := d.Query(ctx, `
		SELECT l.id, l.shop_id, cat.slug, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.is_published, l.attributes,
		       (SELECT i.path_small FROM images i WHERE i.listing_id = l.id ORDER BY i.position, i.id LIMIT 1),
		       l.created_at, l.updated_at
		FROM listings l JOIN categories cat ON cat.id = l.category_id
		WHERE ($1::bigint = 0 OR l.shop_id = $1) AND ($2 = '' OR cat.slug = $2)
		ORDER BY l.created_at DESC, l.id DESC`, shopID, category)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	list := []Summary{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var s Summary
		var photo *string
		if err := rows.Scan(&s.ID, &s.ShopID, &s.Category, &s.Title, &s.PriceManat, &s.PriceIsFrom, &s.IsPublished,
			&s.Attributes, &photo, &s.CreatedAt, &s.UpdatedAt); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		s.Photo = photoURL(photo)
		list = append(list, s)
	}
	return list, rows.Err()
}

// Delete removes a non-dress item with its photos (files too) and busy days.
func Delete(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, st images.Storage, id int64) error {
	paths, err := images.PathsForListing(ctx, d, id)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tag, err := d.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM listings l WHERE l.id = $1 AND `+notDress, id)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return pgx.ErrNoRows
	}
	images.DeleteFiles(st, paths)
	return nil
}
```

`backend/internal/items/handler.go`
```go
package items

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/catalog"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

type Handler struct {
	DB      db.DBTX
	Storage images.Storage
	// Scope, when set, limits the admin routes to the signed-in shop owner's shop,
	// as for dresses. Admins leave it nil.
	Scope func(*gin.Context) int64
}

func (h *Handler) scope(c *gin.Context) int64 {
	if h.Scope == nil {
		return 0
	}
	return h.Scope(c)
}

func (h *Handler) RegisterPublic(g *gin.RouterGroup) {
	g.GET("/items", h.listPublic)
	g.GET("/items/:id", h.getPublic)
}

func (h *Handler) RegisterAdmin(g *gin.RouterGroup) {
	if h.Scope != nil {
		g = g.Group("", httpx.RequireScope(h.Scope))
	}
	g.GET("/items", h.listAdmin)
	g.POST("/items", h.create)
	g.GET("/items/:id", h.getAdmin)
	g.PUT("/items/:id", h.update)
	g.DELETE("/items/:id", h.delete)
}

func (h *Handler) listPublic(c *gin.Context) {
	f, errs := ParseFilter(c.Request.URL.Query())
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(errs))
		return
	}
	page, err := ListPublic(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, f)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, page)
}

func (h *Handler) getPublic(c *gin.Context) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	it, err := GetPublic(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, it)
}

func (h *Handler) listAdmin(c *gin.Context) {
	shopID, ok := listing.ShopIDParam(c)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	if scoped := h.scope(c); scoped != 0 {
		shopID = scoped
	}
	category := c.Query("category")
	if _, known := catalog.Find(category); category != "" && !known {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(map[string]string{"category": "invalid"}))
		return
	}
	list, err := ListAdmin(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, shopID, category)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"items": list})
}

// bindInput reads and checks the body against the item's category: the body's own
// category when creating, the stored one (current) when updating.
func (h *Handler) bindInput(c *gin.Context, current string) (Input, bool) {
	var in Input
	if !httpx.BindJSON(c, &in) {
		return in, false
	}
	if current != "" {
		in.Category = current
	}
	if shopID := h.scope(c); shopID != 0 {
		in.ShopID = shopID // a shop owner always saves into their own shop
	}
	in.Normalize()
	errs := map[string]string{}
	in.Basics.Validate(errs)
	if err := listing.CheckShop(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, in.ShopID, errs); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return in, false
	}
	cat, ok := catalog.Find(in.Category)
	switch {
	case in.Category == "":
		errs["category"] = "required"
	case !ok || cat.OwnPages:
		errs["category"] = "invalid" // dresses are saved through /dresses
	default:
		clean, attrErrs := catalog.Validate(cat, in.Attributes)
		in.Attributes = clean
		for k, code := range attrErrs {
			errs[k] = code
		}
	}
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(errs))
		return in, false
	}
	return in, true
}

func (h *Handler) create(c *gin.Context) {
	in, ok := h.bindInput(c, "")
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	ctx := c.Request.Context()
	id, err := Create(ctx, h.DB, in)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	it, err := Get(ctx, h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusCreated, it)
}

// item loads the item named by :id for an admin route, after the ownership check.
func (h *Handler) item(c *gin.Context) (Item, bool) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok || !listing.OwnedBy(c, h.DB, id, h.scope(c)) {
		return Item{}, false
	}
	it, err := Get(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id) // a dress id is pgx.ErrNoRows: 404
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return Item{}, false
	}
	return it, true
}

func (h *Handler) getAdmin(c *gin.Context) {
	if it, ok := h.item(c); ok {
		c.JSON(http.StatusOK, it)
	}
}

func (h *Handler) update(c *gin.Context) {
	current, ok := h.item(c)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	in, ok := h.bindInput(c, current.Category)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	ctx := c.Request.Context()
	if err := Update(ctx, h.DB, current.ID, in); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	it, err := Get(ctx, h.DB, current.ID)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, it)
}

func (h *Handler) delete(c *gin.Context) {
	current, ok := h.item(c)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	if err := Delete(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, h.Storage, current.ID); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.Status(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

### Task 4: Wire items in: busy days, shop page, link previews, routes

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `backend/internal/busydays/repo.go`
  - `backend/internal/busydays/handler.go`
  - `backend/internal/items/public.go`
  - `backend/internal/shops/public.go`
  - `backend/internal/web/web.go`
  - `backend/internal/server/server.go`
- Test:
  - `backend/internal/busydays/busydays_test.go`
  - `backend/internal/shops/public_test.go`
  - `backend/internal/web/web_test.go`
  - `backend/internal/server/shop_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: from Task 3, `items.Handler`, `items.ParseFilter`, `items.ListPublic`, `items.GetPublic`, `items.Page` and `testutil.InsertItem`.
- Produces:
  - **Busy days:** `GET /api/items/:id/busy-days`, plus `GET/PUT/DELETE /items/:id/busy-days[/:day]` under `/api/admin` and `/api/shop`. Both the `/dresses/…` and `/items/…` paths accept any listing.
  - `items.ShopCategories(ctx, d, shopSlug) ([]string, error)`.
  - `shops.PublicShop.Items items.Page` (JSON `items`) replaces `Dresses`, and `shops.PublicShop.Categories []string` (JSON `categories`) is new.
  - **Link previews:** Open Graph tags for `/items/:id`. The `og:url` is `/dresses/:id` for a dress.
  - **Routes:** items are mounted for the public, for admins, and for shop owners (`Scope: owners.ShopID`).
