`backend/internal/busydays/busydays_test.go`
```go
package busydays_test

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/busydays"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/testutil"
)

// 2026-10-20 10:00 UTC is 15:00 the same day in Turkmenistan.
var now = time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

type env struct {
	r  *gin.Engine
	tx pgx.Tx
}

func setup(t *testing.T) env {
	e := env{r: gin.New(), tx: testutil.Tx(t)}
	h := &busydays.Handler{DB: e.tx, Now: func() time.Time { return now }}
	h.RegisterPublic(e.r.Group("/api"))
	h.RegisterAdmin(e.r.Group("/api/admin"))
	return e
}

func path(prefix string, id int64, day string) string {
	p := prefix + "/dresses/" + strconv.FormatInt(id, 10) + "/busy-days"
	if day != "" {
		p += "/" + day
	}
	return p
}

func mustDay(t *testing.T, s string) time.Time {
	t.Helper()
	d, ok := busydays.ParseDay(s)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("bad day %q", s)
	}
	return d
}

func newDress(t *testing.T, tx pgx.Tx) int64 {
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	return testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID})
}

func TestWindowAt(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		now      time.Time
		from, to string
	}{
		{now, "2026-10-20", "2027-10-20"},
		// 19:30 UTC is already 00:30 the next day in Turkmenistan (UTC+5).
		{time.Date(2026, 10, 19, 19, 30, 0, 0, time.UTC), "2026-10-20", "2027-10-20"},
		{time.Date(2026, 10, 19, 18, 59, 0, 0, time.UTC), "2026-10-19", "2027-10-19"},
		// A leap day rolls over to 1 March a year later.
		{time.Date(2028, 2, 29, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), "2028-02-29", "2029-03-01"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		w := busydays.WindowAt(c.now)
		if got := w.From.Format("2006-01-02"); got != c.from {
			t.Errorf("%v: from = %s, want %s", c.now, got, c.from)
		}
		if got := w.To.Format("2006-01-02"); got != c.to {
			t.Errorf("%v: to = %s, want %s", c.now, got, c.to)
		}
	}
}

func TestParseDay(t *testing.T) {
	for _, s := range []string{"2026-11-14", "2028-02-29"} {
		if _, ok := busydays.ParseDay(s); !ok {
			t.Errorf("%q rejected", s)
		}
	}
	for _, s := range []string{"", "abc", "2026-1-5", "2026-02-30", "2027-02-29", "2026-11-14T00:00:00Z", "14.11.2026"} {
		if _, ok := busydays.ParseDay(s); ok {
			t.Errorf("%q accepted", s)
		}
	}
}

func TestPublicFollowsVisibility(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	hidden := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Hidden: true})
	visible := testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID})
	draft := testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Hidden: true})
	inHidden := testutil.InsertDress(t, e.tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: hidden.ID})

	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path("/api", visible, ""), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("visible: status = %d: %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	for name, id := range map[string]int64{"draft dress": draft, "hidden shop": inHidden, "missing": 999999999} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path("/api", id, ""), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s: status = %d, want 404", name, rec.Code)
		}
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/dresses/abc/busy-days", nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Errorf("non-numeric id: status = %d, want 404", rec.Code)
	}
	// Admins see the calendar of a draft too.
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path("/api/admin", draft, ""), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Errorf("admin draft: status = %d, want 200", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestListReturnsOnlyWindowDaysSorted(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	dress := newDress(t, e.tx)
	for _, s := range []string{"2026-12-31", "2026-10-19", "2026-11-14", "2027-10-21", "2026-10-20", "2027-10-20"} {
		if err := busydays.Mark(context.Background(), e.tx, dress, mustDay(t, s)); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	got := testutil.Decode[busydays.Response](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path("/api", dress, ""), nil, ""))
	want := []string{"2026-10-20", "2026-11-14", "2026-12-31", "2027-10-20"}
	if got.From != "2026-10-20" || got.To != "2027-10-20" || !slices.Equal(got.Days, want) {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v, want from 2026-10-20 to 2027-10-20 days %v", got, want)
	}
}

func TestEmptyCalendarIsAnEmptyList(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path("/api", newDress(t, e.tx), ""), nil, "")
	if !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), `"days":[]`) {
		t.Fatalf("body = %s, want days as an empty list", rec.Body)
	}
}

func TestMarkAndUnmarkAreIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	dress := newDress(t, e.tx)
	days := func() []string {
		return testutil.Decode[busydays.Response](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, path("/api/admin", dress, ""), nil, "")).Days
	}
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, path("/api/admin", dress, "2026-11-14"), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
			t.Fatalf("PUT #%d: status = %d: %s", i+1, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
	}
	if got := days(); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"2026-11-14"}) {
		t.Fatalf("after PUT: days = %v", got)
	}
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodDelete, path("/api/admin", dress, "2026-11-14"), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
			t.Fatalf("DELETE #%d: status = %d: %s", i+1, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
	}
	if got := days(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("after DELETE: days = %v", got)
	}
}

func TestDayValidation(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	dress := newDress(t, e.tx)
	cases := map[string]struct {
		code  int
		field string
	}{
		"2026-11-31": {http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid"},
		"14-11-2026": {http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid"},
		"tomorrow":   {http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid"},
		"2026-10-19": {http.StatusBadRequest, "out_of_range"},
		"2027-10-21": {http.StatusBadRequest, "out_of_range"},
		"2026-10-20": {http.StatusNoContent, ""},
		"2027-10-20": {http.StatusNoContent, ""},
	}
	for day, want := range cases {
		for _, method := range []string{http.MethodPut, http.MethodDelete} {
			rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, method, path("/api/admin", dress, day), nil, "")
			if rec.Code != want.code {
				t.Errorf("%s %s: status = %d, want %d", method, day, rec.Code, want.code)
				continue
			}
			if want.field != "" {
				body := testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, rec)
				if body.Error.Code != "validation_failed" || body.Error.Fields["day"] != want.field {
					t.Errorf("%s %s: error = %+v, want day %q", method, day, body.Error, want.field)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestUnknownListing(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	for _, req := range []struct{ method, path string }{
		{http.MethodGet, path("/api", 999999999, "")},
		{http.MethodGet, path("/api/admin", 999999999, "")},
		{http.MethodPut, path("/api/admin", 999999999, "2026-11-14")},
		{http.MethodDelete, path("/api/admin", 999999999, "2026-11-14")},
	} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, req.method, req.path, nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s %s: status = %d, want 404", req.method, req.path, rec.Code)
		}
	}
}

// Every category has busy days, under /items and (for older links) /dresses alike.
func TestAnyListingHasBusyDays(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, e.tx, testutil.ShopFixture{})
	var tent int64
	err := e.tx.QueryRow(context.Background(), `
		INSERT INTO listings (shop_id, category_id, title, price_manat, is_published, attributes)
		VALUES ($1, (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = 'tents'), 'Ak çadyr', 5000, true, '{"guests": 150}')
		RETURNING id`, shop.ID).Scan(&tent)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	items := fmt.Sprintf("/api/admin/items/%d/busy-days/2026-11-14", tent)
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, items, nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatalf("PUT %s: status = %d", items, rec.Code)
	}
	for _, p := range []string{fmt.Sprintf("/api/items/%d/busy-days", tent), path("/api", tent, "")} {
		got := testutil.Decode[busydays.Response](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, p, nil, ""))
		if len(got.Days) != 1 || got.Days[0] != "2026-11-14" {
			t.Errorf("GET %s: days = %v", p, got.Days)
		}
	}
}

func TestDeletingADressRemovesItsDays(t *testing.T) {
	e := setup(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	dress := newDress(t, e.tx)
	if err := busydays.Mark(ctx, e.tx, dress, mustDay(t, "2026-11-14")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := e.tx.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM listings WHERE id = $1`, dress); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var n int
	if err := e.tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM listing_busy_days WHERE listing_id = $1`, dress).Scan(&n); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("busy days left after delete: %d", n)
	}
}
```

```go
package shops_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"testing"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/shops"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/testutil"
)

func TestGetPublicShop(t *testing.T) {
	tx := testutil.Tx(t)
	r := gin.New()
	(&shops.Handler{DB: tx, Storage: images.LocalStorage{Dir: t.TempDir()}}).RegisterPublic(r.Group("/api"))

	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Gelin Öýi", CitySlug: "mary"})
	testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Title: "One"})
	testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Title: "Two"})
	testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Title: "Draft", Hidden: true})
	testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Category: "tents", Title: "Draft tent", Hidden: true})
	other := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Other Shop"})
	testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: other.ID, Title: "Elsewhere"})
	hidden := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Hidden: true})

	rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shops/"+shop.Slug, nil, "")
	if rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d: %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	s := testutil.Decode[shops.PublicShop](t, rec)
	if s.Name != "Gelin Öýi" || s.City.Slug != "mary" || s.Items.Total != 2 || len(s.Items.Items) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("shop = %+v", s)
	}
	// Only categories with published listings get a chip.
	if len(s.Categories) != 1 || s.Categories[0] != "dresses" {
		t.Fatalf("categories = %v", s.Categories)
	}
	testutil.InsertItem(t, tx, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Category: "cars", Title: "Ak limuzin"})
	s = testutil.Decode[shops.PublicShop](t, testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shops/"+shop.Slug+"?category=cars", nil, ""))
	if s.Items.Total != 1 || s.Items.Items[0].Category != "cars" || len(s.Categories) != 2 || s.Categories[1] != "cars" {
		t.Fatalf("cars only: items = %+v, categories = %v", s.Items, s.Categories)
	}
	for _, slug := range []string{hidden.Slug, "no-such-shop"} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shops/"+slug, nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s: status = %d", slug, rec.Code)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
package web_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/testutil"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/web"
)

const indexHTML = `<!doctype html><html><head><title>Toý</title></head><body><div id="root"></div></body></html>`

func setup(t *testing.T, withIndex bool) (*gin.Engine, *web.Handler) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if withIndex {
		_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "index.html"), []byte(indexHTML), 0o644)
	}
	_ = os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(dir, "assets"), 0o755)
	_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "assets", "app.js"), []byte("console.log(1)"), 0o644)
	h := &web.Handler{Dir: dir, DB: testutil.Tx(t), BaseURL: "https://toy.example"}
	r := gin.New()
	r.NoRoute(h.Serve)
	return r, h
}

func TestServesStaticFilesAndSPAFallback(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/assets/app.js", nil, ""); rec.Code != 200 || rec.Body.String() != "console.log(1)" {
		t.Fatalf("asset: %d %q", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	for _, p := range []string{"/", "/dresses", "/admin/shops", "/../../etc/passwd"} {
		rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, p, nil, "")
		if rec.Code != 200 || !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), `<div id="root">`) {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d %q", p, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
	}
}

func TestUnknownAPIPathIsJSON404(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/api/nope", nil, "")
	if rec.Code != 404 || testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, rec).Error.Code != "not_found" {
		t.Fatalf("got %d %q", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
}

func TestInjectsOpenGraphForPublishedDress(t *testing.T) {
	r, h := setup(t, true)
	tx := h.DB
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, tx, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Gelin Öýi & Co"})
	dress := testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Title: "Princess", Price: 1500})
	testutil.InsertImage(t, tx, dress, 0, "ab/pic")
	hidden := testutil.InsertDress(t, tx, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Hidden: true})

	body := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/dresses/"+strconv.FormatInt(dress, 10), nil, "").Body.String()
	for _, want := range []string{
		`<meta property="og:title" content="Princess · Gelin Öýi &amp; Co">`,
		`<meta property="og:image" content="https://toy.example/uploads/ab/pic_l.jpg">`,
		`<meta property="og:description" content="1500 TMT · Aşgabat">`,
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(body, want) {
			t.Errorf("missing %s in %s", want, body)
		}
	}
	if !strings.Contains(body, `<div id="root">`) {
		t.Fatal("index body missing")
	}
	hiddenBody := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/dresses/"+strconv.FormatInt(hidden, 10), nil, "").Body.String()
	if strings.Contains(hiddenBody, "og:title") {
		t.Fatal("OG tags leaked for unpublished dress")
	}
}

func TestInjectsOpenGraphForPublishedItem(t *testing.T) {
	r, h := setup(t, true)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, h.DB, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Toý Maşyn"})
	car := testutil.InsertItem(t, h.DB, testutil.ItemFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Category: "cars", Title: "Ak limuzin", Price: 900})
	body := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/items/"+strconv.FormatInt(car, 10), nil, "").Body.String()
	for _, want := range []string{
		`<meta property="og:title" content="Ak limuzin · Toý Maşyn">`,
		`<meta property="og:url" content="https://toy.example/items/` + strconv.FormatInt(car, 10) + `">`,
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(body, want) {
			t.Errorf("missing %s in %s", want, body)
		}
	}
}

func TestMissingIndexIs404(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, false)
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/dresses", nil, ""); rec.Code != 404 {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestMissingUploadIsJSON404(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/uploads/ab/nope_l.jpg", nil, "")
	if rec.Code != 404 || testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, rec).Error.Code != "not_found" {
		t.Fatalf("got %d %q", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
}

func TestOpenGraphEscapesMarkup(t *testing.T) {
	r, h := setup(t, true)
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, h.DB, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: `Shop"><script>alert(2)</script>`})
	dress := testutil.InsertDress(t, h.DB, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Title: `"><script>alert(1)</script>`, Price: 100})
	body := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/dresses/"+strconv.FormatInt(dress, 10), nil, "").Body.String()
	if strings.Contains(body, "<script>") {
		t.Fatalf("raw script in output: %s", body)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(body, "&#34;&gt;&lt;script&gt;alert(1)&lt;/script&gt;") {
		t.Fatalf("escaped title missing: %s", body)
	}
}

func TestMissingAssetIsJSON404(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	for _, p := range []string{"/assets/gone-abc123.js", "/assets/", "/assets"} {
		rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, p, nil, "")
		if rec.Code != 404 || testutil.Decode[testutil.ErrorBody](t, rec).Error.Code != "not_found" {
			t.Errorf("%s: got %d %q", p, rec.Code, rec.Body)
		}
	}
}

func TestAssetsAreCachedForever(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/assets/app.js", nil, "")
	if got := rec.Header().Get("Cache-Control"); rec.Code != 200 || got != "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" {
		t.Fatalf("asset: %d Cache-Control %q", rec.Code, got)
	}
	if got := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/", nil, "").Header().Get("Cache-Control"); got == "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" {
		t.Fatalf("index must not be immutable: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestIndexIsNotCached(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	for _, p := range []string{"/", "/dresses", "/index.html"} {
		rec := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, p, nil, "")
		if got := rec.Header().Get("Cache-Control"); rec.Code != 200 || got != "no-cache" {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d Cache-Control %q", p, rec.Code, got)
		}
	}
}

func TestInjectsContactPhoneMeta(t *testing.T) {
	r, h := setup(t, true)
	h.ContactPhone = "+993 12 34 56 78"
	body := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/shop/login", nil, "").Body.String()
	if !strings.Contains(body, `<meta name="wr-contact-phone" content="+993 12 34 56 78">`) {
		t.Fatalf("meta missing: %s", body)
	}
	shop := testutil.InsertShop(t, h.DB, testutil.ShopFixture{Name: "Gelin"})
	dress := testutil.InsertDress(t, h.DB, testutil.DressFixture{ShopID: shop.ID, Title: "Princess", Price: 1500})
	body = testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/dresses/"+strconv.FormatInt(dress, 10), nil, "").Body.String()
	if !strings.Contains(body, "wr-contact-phone") || !strings.Contains(body, "og:title") {
		t.Fatalf("dress page needs both: %s", body)
	}
}

func TestContactPhoneMetaIsEscaped(t *testing.T) {
	r, h := setup(t, true)
	h.ContactPhone = `1"><script>x</script>`
	body := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/shop/login", nil, "").Body.String()
	if strings.Contains(body, "<script>") || !strings.Contains(body, `content="1&#34;&gt;&lt;script&gt;x&lt;/script&gt;"`) {
		t.Fatalf("not escaped: %s", body)
	}
}

func TestNoContactPhoneMetaWhenEmpty(t *testing.T) {
	r, _ := setup(t, true)
	body := testutil.Request(t, r, http.MethodGet, "/shop/login", nil, "").Body.String()
	if strings.Contains(body, "wr-contact-phone") {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected meta: %s", body)
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/server/shop_test.go`
```go
package server_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"image"
	"image/color"
	"image/png"
	"mime/multipart"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/auth"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/busydays"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/dresses"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/server"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/testutil"
)

type shopEnv struct {
	r      *gin.Engine
	tx     pgx.Tx
	tokens *auth.Tokens
}

func newShopEnv(t *testing.T) shopEnv { return newShopEnvWith(t, nil) }

func newShopEnvWith(t *testing.T, uploads *auth.Limiter) shopEnv {
	tx := testutil.Tx(t)
	tokens := auth.NewTokens(strings.Repeat("k", 32), auth.TokenTTL)
	r, err := server.New(server.Deps{
		DB:            tx,
		Tokens:        tokens,
		Limiter:       auth.NewLimiter(100, time.Minute),
		ShopLimiter:   auth.NewLimiter(100, time.Minute),
		UploadLimiter: uploads,
		Storage:       images.LocalStorage{Dir: t.TempDir()},
		UploadDir:     t.TempDir(),
		WebDir:        t.TempDir(),
		BaseURL:       "http://localhost:8080",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return shopEnv{r: r, tx: tx, tokens: tokens}
}

type owner struct {
	token  string
	shopID int64
}

func signUp(t *testing.T, e shopEnv, name, phone string) owner {
	t.Helper()
	var city int64
	if err := e.tx.QueryRow(context.Background(), `SELECT id FROM cities WHERE slug = 'ashgabat'`).Scan(&city); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/shop/signup", map[string]any{
		"shop_name": name, "city_id": city, "address": "Aşgabat 1", "phone": phone, "password": "long-enough-1",
	}, "")
	if rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("signup %s: %d %s", name, rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	token := testutil.Decode[struct {
		Token string `json:"token"`
	}](t, rec).Token
	me := testutil.Decode[struct {
		Shop struct {
			ID int64 `json:"id"`
		} `json:"shop"`
	}](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shop/me", nil, token))
	return owner{token: token, shopID: me.Shop.ID}
}

func dressBody(shopID int64) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		"shop_id": shopID, "title": "Lale", "price_manat": 1500, "color": "ivory", "silhouette": "a_line",
		"sleeves": "long", "sizes": []string{"M"}, "is_published": true,
	}
}

func pngBytes(t *testing.T) []byte {
	img := image.NewRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, 60, 80))
	for x := 0; x < 60; x++ {
		for y := 0; y < 80; y++ {
			img.Set(x, y, color.RGBA{200, 180, 190, 255})
		}
	}
	var b bytes.Buffer
	if err := png.Encode(&b, img); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b.Bytes()
}

func upload(t *testing.T, e shopEnv, token, field string, id int64) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	t.Helper()
	var body bytes.Buffer
	w := multipart.NewWriter(&body)
	fw, _ := w.CreateFormFile("file", "photo.png")
	_, _ = fw.Write(pngBytes(t))
	_ = w.WriteField(field, strconv.FormatInt(id, 10))
	_ = w.Close()
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/shop/images", &body)
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", w.FormDataContentType())
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	e.r.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	return rec
}

func TestShopOwnersOnlyReachTheirOwnShop(t *testing.T) {
	e := newShopEnv(t)
	a := signUp(t, e, "Ak Gelin", "+99365111111")
	b := signUp(t, e, "Gelin Öýi", "+99365222222")

	// A dress is always created in the owner's own shop, whatever shop_id says.
	rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPost, "/api/shop/dresses", dressBody(b.shopID), a.token)
	if rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("create: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	dress := testutil.Decode[struct {
		ID     int64 `json:"id"`
		ShopID int64 `json:"shop_id"`
	}](t, rec)
	if dress.ShopID != a.shopID {
		t.Fatalf("dress landed in shop %d, want %d", dress.ShopID, a.shopID)
	}
	dressPath := "/api/shop/dresses/" + strconv.FormatInt(dress.ID, 10)

	// Owner A manages the dress: photo, calendar, update.
	photo := upload(t, e, a.token, "listing_id", dress.ID)
	if photo.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("own photo: %d %s", photo.Code, photo.Body)
	}
	imageID := testutil.Decode[struct {
		ID int64 `json:"id"`
	}](t, photo).ID
	logo := upload(t, e, a.token, "shop_id", a.shopID)
	if logo.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("own logo: %d %s", logo.Code, logo.Body)
	}
	logoID := testutil.Decode[struct {
		ID int64 `json:"id"`
	}](t, logo).ID
	busyDay := dressPath + "/busy-days/" + time.Now().Add(48*time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02")
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, busyDay, nil, a.token); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatalf("own busy day: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPut, dressPath, dressBody(a.shopID), a.token); rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("own update: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}

	// Owner B reaches none of it.
	imagePath := "/api/shop/images/" + strconv.FormatInt(imageID, 10)
	for _, req := range []struct{ method, path string }{
		{http.MethodGet, dressPath},
		{http.MethodPut, dressPath},
		{http.MethodDelete, dressPath},
		{http.MethodGet, dressPath + "/busy-days"},
		{http.MethodPut, dressPath + "/busy-days/" + time.Now().Add(72*time.Hour).Format("2006-01-02")},
		{http.MethodDelete, imagePath},
		{http.MethodDelete, "/api/shop/images/" + strconv.FormatInt(logoID, 10)},
		{http.MethodDelete, busyDay},
	} {
		var body any
		if req.method == http.MethodPut && !strings.Contains(req.path, "busy-days") {
			body = dressBody(b.shopID)
		}
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, req.method, req.path, body, b.token); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("B %s %s: status = %d, want 404", req.method, req.path, rec.Code)
		}
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodPatch, "/api/shop/images/order", map[string]any{"ids": []int64{imageID}}, b.token); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Errorf("B reorder A's photos: status = %d, want 404", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := upload(t, e, b.token, "listing_id", dress.ID); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Errorf("B photo on A's dress: status = %d, want 404", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := upload(t, e, b.token, "shop_id", a.shopID); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Errorf("B logo on A's shop: status = %d, want 404", rec.Code)
	}

	var logos int
	if err := e.tx.QueryRow(context.Background(), `SELECT count(*) FROM images WHERE id = $1 AND shop_id = $2`, logoID, a.shopID).Scan(&logos); err != nil || logos != 1 {
		t.Errorf("A's logo after B's attempts: count = %d, err = %v", logos, err)
	}

	// Each owner's list shows only their own dresses, even when asking for another shop.
	listB := testutil.Decode[struct {
		Items []struct{} `json:"items"`
	}](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shop/dresses?shop_id="+strconv.FormatInt(a.shopID, 10), nil, b.token))
	if len(listB.Items) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("B sees %d of A's dresses", len(listB.Items))
	}

	// Brides see A's published dress; A can delete it.
	pub := testutil.Decode[struct {
		Total int `json:"total"`
	}](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/dresses", nil, ""))
	if pub.Total != 1 {
		t.Errorf("public dresses = %d, want 1", pub.Total)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodDelete, dressPath, nil, a.token); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Errorf("own delete: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
}

func TestAdminAndShopTokensDoNotCross(t *testing.T) {
	e := newShopEnv(t)
	a := signUp(t, e, "Ak Gelin", "+99365111111")
	adminToken, _ := e.tokens.Issue(1)
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/admin/shops", nil, a.token); rec.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Errorf("shop token on admin route: status = %d, want 401", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shop/me", nil, adminToken); rec.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Errorf("admin token on shop route: status = %d, want 401", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shop/dresses", nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Errorf("no token on shop route: status = %d, want 401", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestUploadsAreLimitedPerOwner(t *testing.T) {
	e := newShopEnvWith(t, auth.NewLimiter(1, time.Minute))
	a := signUp(t, e, "Ak Gelin", "+99365111111")
	b := signUp(t, e, "Gelin Öýi", "+99365222222")
	if rec := upload(t, e, a.token, "shop_id", a.shopID); rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("A first upload: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	if rec := upload(t, e, a.token, "shop_id", a.shopID); rec.Code != http.StatusTooManyRequests {
		t.Errorf("A second upload: status = %d, want 429", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := upload(t, e, b.token, "shop_id", b.shopID); rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Errorf("B first upload: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
	// Only uploads are counted: A can still read and edit.
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shop/dresses", nil, a.token); rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Errorf("A list after limit: %d", rec.Code)
	}
}

func TestScopeThatReturnsNoShopFailsClosed(t *testing.T) {
	e := newShopEnv(t)
	r := gin.New()
	zero := func(*gin.Context) int64 { return 0 }
	g := r.Group("/x")
	(&dresses.Handler{DB: e.tx, Storage: images.LocalStorage{Dir: t.TempDir()}, Scope: zero}).RegisterAdmin(g)
	(&images.Handler{DB: e.tx, Storage: images.LocalStorage{Dir: t.TempDir()}, Scope: zero}).RegisterAdmin(g)
	(&busydays.Handler{DB: e.tx, Scope: zero}).RegisterAdmin(g)
	for _, req := range []struct{ method, path string }{
		{http.MethodGet, "/x/dresses"},
		{http.MethodDelete, "/x/dresses/1"},
		{http.MethodDelete, "/x/images/1"},
		{http.MethodGet, "/x/dresses/1/busy-days"},
	} {
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, r, req.method, req.path, nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
			t.Errorf("%s %s: status = %d, want 401", req.method, req.path, rec.Code)
		}
	}
}

	}
}

func TestShopOwnersOnlyReachTheirOwnItems(t *testing.T) {
	e := newShopEnv(t)
	a := signUp(t, e, "Ak Gelin", "+99365111111")

		ID     int64 `json:"id"`
		ShopID int64 `json:"shop_id"`
	}](t, rec)

		t.Fatalf("own update: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}

	// Owner B reaches none of it.
	for _, req := range []struct{ method, path string }{
		{http.MethodGet, itemPath},
		{http.MethodPut, itemPath},
		{http.MethodDelete, itemPath},
		{http.MethodGet, itemPath + "/busy-days"},
		{http.MethodPut, itemPath + "/busy-days/" + day},
		{http.MethodDelete, itemPath + "/busy-days/" + day},
	} {
		var body any
		if req.method == http.MethodPut && !strings.Contains(req.path, "busy-days") {
			body = tentBody(b.shopID)
		}
		if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, req.method, req.path, body, b.token); rec.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("B %s %s: status = %d, want 404", req.method, req.path, rec.Code)
		}
	}
	listB := testutil.Decode[struct {
		Items []struct{} `json:"items"`
	}](t, testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/shop/items?shop_id="+strconv.FormatInt(a.shopID, 10), nil, b.token))
	if len(listB.Items) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("B sees %d of A's items", len(listB.Items))
	}

	// Brides see the tent; A can delete it.
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodGet, "/api/items/"+strconv.FormatInt(item.ID, 10), nil, ""); rec.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Errorf("public item: %d", rec.Code)
	}
	if rec := testutil.Request(t, e.r, http.MethodDelete, itemPath, nil, a.token); rec.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Errorf("own delete: %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body)
	}
}
```

`backend/internal/busydays/repo.go`
```go
package busydays

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
)

// listingExists reports whether id is a listing of any category. With publicOnly it
// must also be published in a published shop, the v1 visibility rule; with a non-zero
// shopID it must belong to that shop.
func listingExists(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, id int64, publicOnly bool, shopID int64) (bool, error) {
	var ok bool
	err := d.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT EXISTS (
			SELECT 1 FROM listings l
			JOIN shops s ON s.id = l.shop_id
			WHERE l.id = $1 AND (NOT $2 OR (l.is_published AND s.is_published))
			  AND ($3::bigint = 0 OR l.shop_id = $3))`, id, publicOnly, shopID).Scan(&ok)
	return ok, err
}

// List returns the listing's busy days inside w as sorted YYYY-MM-DD strings, never nil.
func List(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, listingID int64, w Window) ([]string, error) {
	rows, err := d.Query(ctx, `
		SELECT to_char(day, 'YYYY-MM-DD') FROM listing_busy_days
		WHERE listing_id = $1 AND day BETWEEN $2::date AND $3::date
		ORDER BY day`, listingID, formatDay(w.From), formatDay(w.To))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	days, err := pgx.CollectRows(rows, pgx.RowTo[string])
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if days == nil {
		days = []string{}
	}
	return days, nil
}

// Mark makes day busy; marking a busy day again changes nothing.
func Mark(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, listingID int64, day time.Time) error {
	_, err := d.Exec(ctx, `
		INSERT INTO listing_busy_days (listing_id, day) VALUES ($1, $2::date)
		ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, listingID, formatDay(day))
	return err
}

// Unmark makes day free; unmarking a free day changes nothing.
func Unmark(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, listingID int64, day time.Time) error {
	_, err := d.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM listing_busy_days WHERE listing_id = $1 AND day = $2::date`,
		listingID, formatDay(day))
	return err
}
```

`backend/internal/busydays/handler.go`
```go
package busydays

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
)

type Handler struct {
	DB db.DBTX
	// Now returns the current time; nil means time.Now. Tests set a fixed clock.
	Now func() time.Time
	// Scope, when set, limits the admin routes to the signed-in shop owner's shop.
	Scope func(*gin.Context) int64
}

// Response is one dress's calendar: the window and the busy days inside it.
type Response struct {
	From string   `json:"from"`
	To   string   `json:"to"`
	Days []string `json:"days"`
}

// Busy days belong to any listing: /items/… serves every category, and /dresses/…
// stays for links and pages made before other categories existed.
func (h *Handler) RegisterPublic(g *gin.RouterGroup) {
	for _, base := range []string{"/dresses", "/items"} {
		g.GET(base+"/:id/busy-days", h.listPublic)
	}
}

func (h *Handler) RegisterAdmin(g *gin.RouterGroup) {
	if h.Scope != nil {
		g = g.Group("", httpx.RequireScope(h.Scope))
	}
	for _, base := range []string{"/dresses", "/items"} {
		g.GET(base+"/:id/busy-days", h.listAdmin)
		g.PUT(base+"/:id/busy-days/:day", h.mark)
		g.DELETE(base+"/:id/busy-days/:day", h.unmark)
	}
}

func (h *Handler) window() Window {
	now := time.Now
	if h.Now != nil {
		now = h.Now
	}
	return WindowAt(now())
}

func (h *Handler) listPublic(c *gin.Context) { h.list(c, true) }
func (h *Handler) listAdmin(c *gin.Context)  { h.list(c, false) }

func (h *Handler) list(c *gin.Context, publicOnly bool) {
	id, ok := h.listing(c, publicOnly)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	w := h.window()
	days, err := List(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id, w)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, Response{From: formatDay(w.From), To: formatDay(w.To), Days: days})
}

// listing reads :id and answers 404 unless it names a listing (a visible one when publicOnly).
func (h *Handler) listing(c *gin.Context, publicOnly bool) (int64, bool) {
	id, ok := httpx.ParseID(c, "id")
	if !ok {
		return 0, false
	}
	var shopID int64
	if h.Scope != nil && !publicOnly {
		shopID = h.Scope(c)
	}
	exists, err := listingExists(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id, publicOnly, shopID)
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return 0, false
	}
	if !exists {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.ErrNotFound)
		return 0, false
	}
	return id, true
}

func (h *Handler) mark(c *gin.Context)   { h.change(c, Mark) }
func (h *Handler) unmark(c *gin.Context) { h.change(c, Unmark) }

func (h *Handler) change(c *gin.Context, apply func(context.Context, db.DBTX, int64, time.Time) error) {
	id, ok := h.listing(c, false)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	day, ok := ParseDay(c.Param("day"))
	if !ok {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(map[string]string{"day": "invalid"}))
		return
	}
	if !h.window().Contains(day) {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(map[string]string{"day": "out_of_range"}))
		return
	}
	if err := apply(c.Request.Context(), h.DB, id, day); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.Status(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The shop page lists every category**

`backend/internal/items/public.go`
```go
package items

import (
	"context"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/catalog"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

const publicFrom = `
	FROM listings l
	JOIN categories cat ON cat.id = l.category_id
	JOIN shops s ON s.id = l.shop_id
	JOIN cities ci ON ci.id = s.city_id`

func where(f Filter) *listing.Where {
	w := &listing.Where{}
	if f.Category != "" {
		w.Add("cat.slug = ?", f.Category)
	}
	for _, c := range f.attrs {
		w.Add(c.SQL, c.Value)
	}
	w.Apply(f.Query)
	return w
}

// ListPublic returns a page of published listings from published shops, in every
// category or one.
func ListPublic(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, f Filter) (Page, error) {
	w := where(f)
	page := Page{Items: []ListItem{}, Page: f.Page, PerPage: f.PerPage}
	if err := dbx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*)`+publicFrom+w.SQL(), w.Args()...).Scan(&page.Total); err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	limit, args := w.Limit(f.Query)
	rows, err := dbx.Query(ctx, `SELECT l.id, cat.slug, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.attributes,
		       (SELECT i.path_small FROM images i WHERE i.listing_id = l.id ORDER BY i.position, i.id LIMIT 1),
		       s.name, s.slug, ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en`+
		publicFrom+w.SQL()+w.OrderBy()+limit, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	for rows.Next() {
		var it ListItem
		var photo *string
		if err := rows.Scan(&it.ID, &it.Category, &it.Title, &it.PriceManat, &it.PriceIsFrom, &it.Attributes, &photo,
			&it.Shop.Name, &it.Shop.Slug, &it.City.Slug, &it.City.NameTK, &it.City.NameRU, &it.City.NameEN); err != nil {
			return Page{}, err
		}
		it.Photo = photoURL(photo)
		page.Items = append(page.Items, it)
	}
	return page, rows.Err()
}

// GetPublic returns one published listing of any category; pgx.ErrNoRows otherwise.
func GetPublic(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, id int64) (PublicItem, error) {
	var it PublicItem
	var logo *string
	err := dbx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT l.id, cat.slug, l.title, l.price_manat, l.price_is_from, l.description, l.attributes,
		       s.name, s.slug, s.address, s.phone, s.instagram_url, s.tiktok_url,
		       (SELECT path_small FROM images WHERE id = s.logo_image_id),
		       ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en`+publicFrom+`
		WHERE l.id = $1 AND l.is_published AND s.is_published`, id).Scan(
		&it.ID, &it.Category, &it.Title, &it.PriceManat, &it.PriceIsFrom, &it.Description, &it.Attributes,
		&it.Shop.Name, &it.Shop.Slug, &it.Shop.Address, &it.Shop.Phone, &it.Shop.InstagramURL, &it.Shop.TikTokURL,
		&logo, &it.Shop.City.Slug, &it.Shop.City.NameTK, &it.Shop.City.NameRU, &it.Shop.City.NameEN)
	if err != nil {
		return PublicItem{}, err
	}
	it.Shop.Logo = photoURL(logo)
	it.Images, err = images.ListForListing(ctx, dbx, id)
	return it, err
}

func photoURL(path *string) *string {
	if path == nil {
		return nil
	}
	u := images.URL(*path)
	return &u
}

// ShopCategories returns the categories the shop has published listings in, in the
// catalog's display order.
func ShopCategories(ctx context.Context, dbx db.DBTX, shopSlug string) ([]string, error) {
	rows, err := dbx.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT cat.slug`+publicFrom+`
		WHERE l.is_published AND s.is_published AND s.slug = $1`, shopSlug)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	has := map[string]bool{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var slug string
		if err := rows.Scan(&slug); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		has[slug] = true
	}
	out := []string{}
	for _, c := range catalog.Categories {
		if has[c.Slug] {
			out = append(out, c.Slug)
		}
	}
	return out, rows.Err()
}
```

`backend/internal/shops/public.go`
```go
package shops

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/items"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/listing"
)

type PublicShop struct {
	Name         string          `json:"name"`
	Slug         string          `json:"slug"`
	Address      string          `json:"address"`
	Phone        string          `json:"phone"`
	InstagramURL *string         `json:"instagram_url"`
	TikTokURL    *string         `json:"tiktok_url"`
	Description  *string         `json:"description"`
	Logo         *string         `json:"logo"`
	City         listing.CityRef `json:"city"`
	Items        items.Page      `json:"items"`
	// Categories the shop has published listings in, for the page's category chips.
	Categories []string `json:"categories"`
}

func GetPublicBySlug(ctx context.Context, d db.DBTX, slug string) (PublicShop, error) {
	var s PublicShop
	var logo *string
	err := d.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT s.name, s.slug, s.address, s.phone, s.instagram_url, s.tiktok_url, s.description,
		       (SELECT path_small FROM images WHERE id = s.logo_image_id),
		       ci.slug, ci.name_tk, ci.name_ru, ci.name_en
		FROM shops s JOIN cities ci ON ci.id = s.city_id
		WHERE s.slug = $1 AND s.is_published`, slug).Scan(
		&s.Name, &s.Slug, &s.Address, &s.Phone, &s.InstagramURL, &s.TikTokURL, &s.Description,
		&logo, &s.City.Slug, &s.City.NameTK, &s.City.NameRU, &s.City.NameEN)
	if err != nil {
		return PublicShop{}, err
	}
	if logo != nil {
		u := images.URL(*logo)
		s.Logo = &u
	}
	return s, nil
}

// getPublic returns the shop and a page of its published listings in every category
// (supports the same query params as GET /items, including category).
func (h *Handler) getPublic(c *gin.Context) {
	f, errs := items.ParseFilter(c.Request.URL.Query())
	if len(errs) > 0 {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.Validation(errs))
		return
	}
	ctx := c.Request.Context()
	s, err := GetPublicBySlug(ctx, h.DB, c.Param("slug"))
	if err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	f.ShopSlug = s.Slug
	if s.Items, err = items.ListPublic(ctx, h.DB, f); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	if s.Categories, err = items.ShopCategories(ctx, h.DB, s.Slug); err != nil {
		httpx.Abort(c, err)
		return
	}
	c.JSON(http.StatusOK, s)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Link previews and routes**

`backend/internal/web/web.go`
```go
package web

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"html"
	"io/fs"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/items"
)

// Handler serves the built SPA: real files as-is, every other GET gets
// index.html. Dress pages get Open Graph tags for link previews.
type Handler struct {
	Dir     string
	DB      db.DBTX
	BaseURL string
	// ContactPhone, when set, is published to the SPA in a <meta name="wr-contact-phone"> tag.
	ContactPhone string
}

// Dress and item pages get Open Graph tags; both are listings.
var listingPath = regexp.MustCompile(`^/(?:dresses|items)/(\d+)/?$`)

func (h *Handler) Serve(c *gin.Context) {
	p := c.Request.URL.Path
	if p == "/api" || strings.HasPrefix(p, "/api/") ||
		p == "/uploads" || strings.HasPrefix(p, "/uploads/") ||
		(c.Request.Method != http.MethodGet && c.Request.Method != http.MethodHead) {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	fsys := os.DirFS(h.Dir)
	clean := path.Clean("/" + p)
	name := strings.TrimPrefix(clean, "/")
	// Vite content-hashes everything under /assets, so a stale page asking for
	// a file from an older build must fail loudly rather than get index.html.
	inAssets := clean == "/assets" || strings.HasPrefix(clean, "/assets/")
	if name != "" && name != "index.html" {
		if st, err := fs.Stat(fsys, name); err == nil && !st.IsDir() {
			if inAssets {
				c.Header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
			}
			c.FileFromFS(name, http.FS(fsys))
			return
		}
	}
	if inAssets {
		httpx.Abort(c, httpx.ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	index, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "index.html")
	if err != nil {
		c.String(http.StatusNotFound, "frontend not built: run the frontend build first")
		return
	}
	if m := listingPath.FindStringSubmatch(p); m != nil {
		if id, err := strconv.ParseInt(m[1], 10, 64); err == nil {
			index = h.injectOG(c.Request.Context(), index, id)
		}
	}
	if h.ContactPhone != "" {
		tag := `<meta name="wr-contact-phone" content="` + html.EscapeString(h.ContactPhone) + `">` + "\n"
		index = bytes.Replace(index, []byte("</head>"), []byte(tag+"</head>"), 1)
	}
	c.Header("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
	c.Data(http.StatusOK, "text/html; charset=utf-8", index)
}

func (h *Handler) injectOG(ctx context.Context, index []byte, id int64) []byte {
	d, err := items.GetPublic(ctx, h.DB, id)
	if err != nil {
		if !errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			log.Printf("og lookup for listing %d: %v", id, err)
		}
		return index
	}
	page := "/items/"
	if d.Category == "dresses" {
		page = "/dresses/"
	}
	var b strings.Builder
	meta := func(prop, content string) {
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, `<meta property="%s" content="%s">`+"\n", prop, html.EscapeString(content))
	}
	meta("og:type", "product")
	meta("og:title", d.Title+" · "+d.Shop.Name)
	meta("og:description", fmt.Sprintf("%d TMT · %s", d.PriceManat, d.Shop.City.NameTK))
	meta("og:url", h.BaseURL+page+strconv.FormatInt(id, 10))
	if len(d.Images) > 0 {
		meta("og:image", h.BaseURL+d.Images[0].Large)
	}
	return bytes.Replace(index, []byte("</head>"), []byte(b.String()+"</head>"), 1)
}
```

`backend/internal/server/server.go`
```go
package server

import (
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"

	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/auth"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/busydays"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/cities"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/db"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/dresses"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/httpx"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/images"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/items"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/owners"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/shops"
	"wedding-rentals/backend/internal/web"
)

type Deps struct {
	DB      db.DBTX
	Tokens  *auth.Tokens
	Limiter *auth.Limiter
	// ShopLimiter rate-limits shop sign-up and sign-in; nil gets its own 5-per-minute limiter.
	ShopLimiter *auth.Limiter
	// UploadLimiter caps a shop owner's image uploads; nil gets 30 per minute per owner.
	UploadLimiter *auth.Limiter
	Storage       images.Storage
	UploadDir     string
	WebDir        string
	BaseURL       string
	ContactPhone  string
	// TrustedProxies are IPs/CIDRs allowed to set X-Forwarded-For; empty trusts none.
	TrustedProxies []string
}

func New(d Deps) (*gin.Engine, error) {
	r := gin.New()
	r.Use(gin.Logger(), httpx.Recovery())
	if err := r.SetTrustedProxies(d.TrustedProxies); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	r.MaxMultipartMemory = 16 << 20

	api := r.Group("/api")
	api.GET("/health", func(c *gin.Context) { c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"status": "ok"}) })

	authH := &auth.Handler{DB: d.DB, Tokens: d.Tokens, Limiter: d.Limiter}
	citiesH := &cities.Handler{DB: d.DB}
	shopsH := &shops.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage}
	dressesH := &dresses.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage}
	imagesH := &images.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage}
	busyH := &busydays.Handler{DB: d.DB}
	itemsH := &items.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage}
	if d.ShopLimiter == nil {
		d.ShopLimiter = auth.NewLimiter(5, time.Minute)
	}
	if d.UploadLimiter == nil {
		d.UploadLimiter = auth.NewLimiter(30, time.Minute)
	}
	ownersH := &owners.Handler{DB: d.DB, Tokens: d.Tokens, Limiter: d.ShopLimiter}

	authH.RegisterPublic(api)
	citiesH.RegisterPublic(api)
	shopsH.RegisterPublic(api)
	dressesH.RegisterPublic(api)
	busyH.RegisterPublic(api)
	itemsH.RegisterPublic(api)
	ownersH.RegisterPublic(api)

	admin := api.Group("/admin", auth.Middleware(d.Tokens))
	shopsH.RegisterAdmin(admin)
	dressesH.RegisterAdmin(admin)
	imagesH.RegisterAdmin(admin)
	busyH.RegisterAdmin(admin)
	itemsH.RegisterAdmin(admin)
	ownersH.RegisterAdmin(admin)

	// Shop owners reuse the admin handlers, scoped to their own shop.
	shop := api.Group("/shop", owners.ShopMiddleware(d.Tokens, d.DB), owners.UploadLimit(d.UploadLimiter))
	ownersH.RegisterShop(shop)
	(&dresses.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage, Scope: owners.ShopID}).RegisterAdmin(shop)
	(&images.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage, Scope: owners.ShopID}).RegisterAdmin(shop)
	(&busydays.Handler{DB: d.DB, Scope: owners.ShopID}).RegisterAdmin(shop)
	(&items.Handler{DB: d.DB, Storage: d.Storage, Scope: owners.ShopID}).RegisterAdmin(shop)

	r.Static("/uploads", d.UploadDir)
	webH := &web.Handler{Dir: d.WebDir, DB: d.DB, BaseURL: d.BaseURL, ContactPhone: d.ContactPhone}
	r.NoRoute(webH.Serve)
	return r, nil
}
```

### Task 5: Frontend data layer: catalog, item filters, API calls, forms, prices

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `frontend/src/lib/catalog.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/itemFilters.ts`
- Modify:
  - `frontend/src/lib/types.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/filters.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/api.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/manage.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/forms.ts`
  - `frontend/src/components/price.ts`
  - `frontend/src/pages/admin/DressEdit.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/i18n/en.json`, `ru.json` and `tk.json`
- Test:
  - `frontend/src/lib/catalog_test.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/itemFilters_test.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/api_test.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/manage_test.ts`
  - `frontend/src/lib/forms_test.ts`
  - `frontend/src/components/price_test.ts`
  - `frontend/src/i18n/i18n_test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - from Task 2: `frontend/src/lib/catalog.json`;
  - from Tasks 3 and 4: the `/api/items` and `/{admin,shop}/items` endpoints.
- Produces:
  - **Types:**
    - `Attributes`;
    - `ListItem.category?` and `ListItem.attributes?`, which only the all-category list sends;
    - `PublicItem`, `ListingSummary`, `AdminItem` and `ItemInput`.
  - **Catalog** (`lib/catalog.ts`):
    - `FieldDef`, `CategoryDef`, `CATEGORIES`, `ITEM_CATEGORIES` and `categoryDef(slug)`;
    - `categoryHref(slug)` (`/dresses` or `/c/<slug>`) and `listingHref({id, category})` (`/dresses/:id` or `/items/:id`);
    - `catLabel`, `fieldLabel` and `optionLabel`, which return i18n keys.
  - **Filters:**
    - `lib/itemFilters.ts`: `ItemFilters{q, city, priceMin, priceMax, attrs}`, `EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS`, `parseItemFilters(c, query)`, `itemFiltersToQuery(c, f)` and `countItemFilters(f)`;
    - `lib/filters.ts` now exports `priceParam`.
  - **API:**
    - public: `api.items(query)` and `api.item(id)`; `api.busyDays(id)` now calls `/api/items/:id/busy-days`;
    - manage (admin and owner): `listings({shopId?, category?})`, `item(id)`, `createItem`, `updateItem` and `deleteItem`. The busy-day calls now use `/items/:id/busy-days`.
  - **Manage context:**
    - `Manage.dressesHref` is replaced by `listingsHref(shopId?)`;
    - new: `newListingHref(shopId?)`, `newDressHref(shopId?)`, `itemHref(id)` and `newItemHref(category, shopId?)`.
  - **Forms and prices:**
    - `ItemForm`, `emptyItemForm(category, shopId?)`, `itemToForm(item)` and `itemFormToInput(form, fields)`;
    - `priceText` adds " / hour" or " / day" for a car's `price_unit`.
  - **i18n keys:** `cat.*`, `attr.*`, `price.per_hour`, `price.per_day` and `field.unknown`.

`frontend/src/lib/catalog_test.ts`
```ts
import { assert, assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import { CATEGORIES, categoryDef, categoryHref, ITEM_CATEGORIES, listingHref } from "./catalog.ts";

Deno.test("dresses come first and keep their own pages", () => {
  assertEquals(CATEGORIES[0].slug, "dresses");
  assertEquals(CATEGORIES[0].own_pages, true);
  assertEquals(ITEM_CATEGORIES.map((c) => c.slug), ["cars", "national-clothes", "tents", "car-decorations"]);
});

Deno.test("every filter belongs to a choice or number field", () => {
  for (const c of CATEGORIES) {
    for (const f of c.fields) {
      if (!f.filter) continue;
      if (f.filter.kind === "min") assertEquals(f.kind, "number", `${c.slug}.${f.key}`);
      else assert(f.options && f.options.length > 0, `${c.slug}.${f.key} has no options`);
    }
  }
  assertEquals(categoryDef("cars")?.fields.find((f) => f.key === "seats")?.filter, { param: "seats_min", kind: "min" });
  assertEquals(categoryDef("boats"), undefined);
});

Deno.test("links go to each category's own pages", () => {
  assertEquals(categoryHref("dresses"), "/dresses");
  assertEquals(categoryHref("tents"), "/c/tents");
  assertEquals(listingHref({ id: 4, category: "dresses" }), "/dresses/4");
  assertEquals(listingHref({ id: 4 }), "/dresses/4"); // dress lists send no category
  assertEquals(listingHref({ id: 5, category: "cars" }), "/items/5");
});
```

```ts
import { assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import { categoryDef } from "./catalog.ts";
import { countItemFilters, EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, itemFiltersToQuery, parseItemFilters } from "./itemFilters.ts";

const cars = categoryDef("cars")!;

Deno.test("parseItemFilters keeps only the category's valid filters", () => {
  const f = parseItemFilters(cars, new URLSearchParams("q= ak &city=mary&price_max=900&body=suv&color=green&seats_min=70&size=M"));
  assertEquals(f, { q: "ak", city: "mary", priceMin: null, priceMax: 900, attrs: { body: "suv" } });
  assertEquals(parseItemFilters(cars, new URLSearchParams("seats_min=05")).attrs, { seats_min: "5" });
});

Deno.test("itemFiltersToQuery writes a stable order and round-trips", () => {
  const f = { ...EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, city: "mary", attrs: { seats_min: "4", body: "limousine" } };
  const query = itemFiltersToQuery(cars, f);
  assertEquals(query.toString(), "city=mary&body=limousine&seats_min=4");
  assertEquals(parseItemFilters(cars, query), f);
  // A value the category does not accept is left out.
  assertEquals(itemFiltersToQuery(cars, { ...EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, attrs: { body: "truck" } }).toString(), "");
});

Deno.test("countItemFilters counts the sheet's filters, not the search", () => {
  assertEquals(countItemFilters({ ...EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, q: "ak" }), 0);
  assertEquals(countItemFilters({ ...EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, city: "mary", priceMin: 100, attrs: { body: "suv" } }), 3);
});
```

`frontend/src/lib/api_test.ts`
```ts
import { assertEquals, assertInstanceOf, assertRejects } from "@std/assert";
import { ApiError, createApi } from "./api.ts";
import type { ShopInput } from "./types.ts";

interface Call {
  url: string;
  init: RequestInit;
}

function setup(handler: (url: string, init: RequestInit) => Response | Promise<Response>, token: string | null = "tok") {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  let unauthorized = 0;
  const fetchFn = ((input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
    const url = String(input);
    calls.push({ url, init });
    return Promise.resolve(handler(url, init));
  }) as typeof fetch;
  const api = createApi({ fetch: fetchFn, token: () => token, onUnauthorized: () => unauthorized++ });
  return { api, calls, unauthorized: () => unauthorized };
}

const json = (status: number, body: unknown) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

const shopInput: ShopInput = {
  name: "Ak Gelin", city_id: 1, address: "Aşgabat", phone: "+99365000000", instagram_url: "",
  tiktok_url: "", description: "", is_published: true, aliases: [],
};

Deno.test("cities unwraps items and sends no token", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => json(200, { items: [{ id: 1, slug: "ashgabat" }] }));
  const cities = await api.cities();
  assertEquals(cities.map((c) => c.slug), ["ashgabat"]);
  assertEquals(calls[0].url, "/api/cities");
  assertEquals(calls[0].init.method, "GET");
  assertEquals((calls[0].init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization, undefined);
});

Deno.test("dresses passes the query string through", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => json(200, { items: [], page: 1, per_page: 24, total: 0 }));
  await api.dresses(new URLSearchParams("q=ak&page=2"));
  await api.dresses();
  assertEquals(calls.map((c) => c.url), ["/api/dresses?q=ak&page=2", "/api/dresses"]);
});

Deno.test("admin calls send the bearer token and a JSON body", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => json(201, { id: 7, ...shopInput }));
  const shop = await api.admin.createShop(shopInput);
  assertEquals(shop.id, 7);
  const { url, init } = calls[0];
  const headers = init.headers as Record<string, string>;
  assertEquals(url, "/api/admin/shops");
  assertEquals(init.method, "POST");
  assertEquals(headers.Authorization, "Bearer tok");
  assertEquals(headers["Content-Type"], "application/json");
  assertEquals(JSON.parse(String(init.body)), shopInput);
});

Deno.test("admin dresses filters by shop only when a shop is given", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => json(200, { items: [] }));
  await api.admin.dresses(3);
  await api.admin.dresses();
  assertEquals(calls.map((c) => c.url), ["/api/admin/dresses?shop_id=3", "/api/admin/dresses"]);
});

Deno.test("an error envelope becomes an ApiError with code and fields", async () => {
  const { api } = setup(() =>
    json(400, { error: { code: "validation_failed", message: "bad", fields: { phone: "invalid" } } })
  );
  const err = await assertRejects(() => api.admin.createShop(shopInput), ApiError);
  assertEquals(err.status, 400);
  assertEquals(err.code, "validation_failed");
  assertEquals(err.fields, { phone: "invalid" });
});

Deno.test("a 401 on an admin call triggers onUnauthorized, a failed login does not", async () => {
  const s = setup(() => json(401, { error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "login required" } }));
  await assertRejects(() => s.api.admin.shops(), ApiError);
  assertEquals(s.unauthorized(), 1);
  const l = setup(() => json(401, { error: { code: "invalid_credentials", message: "wrong" } }));
  await assertRejects(() => l.api.login("a@b.c", "x"), ApiError);
  assertEquals(l.unauthorized(), 0);
});

Deno.test("a network failure becomes network_error", async () => {
  const { api } = setup(() => Promise.reject(new TypeError("Failed to fetch")));
  const err = await assertRejects(() => api.cities(), ApiError);
  assertEquals(err.status, 0);
  assertEquals(err.code, "network_error");
});

Deno.test("a non-JSON server error becomes internal_error", async () => {
  const { api } = setup(() => new Response("<html>bad gateway</html>", { status: 502 }));
  const err = await assertRejects(() => api.dress(1), ApiError);
  assertEquals(err.status, 502);
  assertEquals(err.code, "internal_error");
});

Deno.test("204 responses resolve to undefined", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => new Response(null, { status: 204 }));
  assertEquals(await api.admin.deleteShop(4), undefined);
  assertEquals(calls[0].init.method, "DELETE");
  assertEquals(calls[0].url, "/api/admin/shops/4");
});

Deno.test("login returns the token and reorderImages sends the ids", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup((url) =>
    url.endsWith("/login") ? json(200, { token: "jwt" }) : new Response(null, { status: 204 })
  );
  assertEquals(await api.login("a@b.c", "secret-pass"), "jwt");
  await api.admin.reorderImages([3, 1, 2]);
  assertEquals(JSON.parse(String(calls[1].init.body)), { ids: [3, 1, 2] });
  assertEquals(calls[1].init.method, "PATCH");
});

Deno.test("ApiError is an Error", () => {
  assertInstanceOf(new ApiError(404, "not_found", "x"), Error);
});

Deno.test("a 413 without a JSON body becomes body_too_large", async () => {
  const { api } = setup(() => new Response("<html>too big</html>", { status: 413 }));
  const err = await assertRejects(() => api.admin.createShop(shopInput), ApiError);
  assertEquals(err.status, 413);
  assertEquals(err.code, "body_too_large");
});

Deno.test("a request that never answers is aborted and becomes network_error", async () => {
  const fetchFn = ((_: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) =>
    new Promise<Response>((_res, rej) => {
      init.signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => rej(new DOMException("aborted", "AbortError")));
    })) as typeof fetch;
  const api = createApi({ fetch: fetchFn, token: () => null, onUnauthorized: () => {}, timeoutMs: 10 });
  const err = await assertRejects(() => api.cities(), ApiError);
  assertEquals(err.code, "network_error");
});

Deno.test("an OK response whose body cannot be read becomes network_error", async () => {
  const { api } = setup(() => new Response("<html>cut off", { status: 200 }));
  const err = await assertRejects(() => api.cities(), ApiError);
  assertEquals(err.code, "network_error");
});

Deno.test("busy days: public and admin reads, and marking a day busy or free", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup((url, init) =>
    init.method === "GET"
      ? json(200, { from: "2026-10-20", to: "2027-10-20", days: ["2026-11-14"] })
      : new Response(null, { status: 204 })
  );
  assertEquals((await api.busyDays(7)).days, ["2026-11-14"]);
  assertEquals((await api.admin.busyDays(7)).to, "2027-10-20");
  assertEquals(await api.admin.markBusy(7, "2026-11-14"), undefined);
  assertEquals(await api.admin.markFree(7, "2026-11-14"), undefined);
  assertEquals(calls.map((c) => `${c.init.method} ${c.url}`), [
    "GET /api/items/7/busy-days",
    "GET /api/admin/items/7/busy-days",
    "PUT /api/admin/items/7/busy-days/2026-11-14",
    "DELETE /api/admin/items/7/busy-days/2026-11-14",
  ]);
  assertEquals((calls[0].init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization, undefined);
  assertEquals((calls[2].init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization, "Bearer tok");
});

Deno.test("owner sign-up and sign-in send no token and return the token", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => json(200, { token: "shop-jwt" }));
  const input = { shop_name: "Ak Gelin", city_id: 1, address: "Aşgabat", phone: "65123456", password: "long-enough-1" };
  assertEquals(await api.owner.signup(input), "shop-jwt");
  assertEquals(await api.owner.login("65123456", "long-enough-1"), "shop-jwt");
  assertEquals(calls.map((c) => `${c.init.method} ${c.url}`), ["POST /api/shop/signup", "POST /api/shop/login"]);
  assertEquals(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init.body)), input);
  assertEquals(JSON.parse(String(calls[1].init.body)), { phone: "65123456", password: "long-enough-1" });
  for (const c of calls) assertEquals((c.init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization, undefined);
});

Deno.test("a failed owner sign-in does not trigger onUnauthorized, an expired owner session does", async () => {
  const s = setup(() => json(401, { error: { code: "invalid_credentials", message: "wrong" } }));
  await assertRejects(() => s.api.owner.login("65123456", "x"), ApiError);
  assertEquals(s.unauthorized(), 0);
  await assertRejects(() => s.api.owner.me(), ApiError);
  assertEquals(s.unauthorized(), 1);
});

Deno.test("owner calls go to /api/shop with the bearer token", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup((_url, init) =>
    init.method === "GET" ? json(200, { items: [] }) : new Response(null, { status: 204 })
  );
  await api.owner.me();
  await api.owner.updateShop(shopInput);
  await api.owner.dresses();
  await api.owner.deleteDress(5);
  await api.owner.reorderImages([2, 1]);
  await api.owner.deleteImage(9);
  await api.owner.markBusy(5, "2026-11-14");
  await api.owner.markFree(5, "2026-11-14");
  assertEquals(calls.map((c) => `${c.init.method} ${c.url}`), [
    "GET /api/shop/me",
    "PUT /api/shop/me/shop",
    "GET /api/shop/dresses",
    "DELETE /api/shop/dresses/5",
    "PATCH /api/shop/images/order",
    "DELETE /api/shop/images/9",
    "PUT /api/shop/items/5/busy-days/2026-11-14",
    "DELETE /api/shop/items/5/busy-days/2026-11-14",
  ]);
  for (const c of calls) assertEquals((c.init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization, "Bearer tok");
});

Deno.test("an admin sets a shop owner's password", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup(() => new Response(null, { status: 204 }));
  assertEquals(await api.admin.setOwnerPassword(4, "brand-new-pass"), undefined);
  assertEquals(`${calls[0].init.method} ${calls[0].url}`, "POST /api/admin/shops/4/owner-password");
  assertEquals(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init.body)), { password: "brand-new-pass" });
});

Deno.test("items: the public all-category list, one item, and the shared listing calls", async () => {
  const { api, calls } = setup((_url, init) =>
    init.method === "GET" ? json(200, { items: [], page: 1, per_page: 24, total: 0 }) : json(201, { id: 9 })
  );
  await api.items(new URLSearchParams("category=cars&body=suv"));
  await api.item(9);
  await api.admin.listings({ shopId: 3, category: "tents" });
  await api.owner.listings();
  const input = {
    shop_id: 3, category: "tents", title: "Ak çadyr", price_manat: 5000, price_is_from: false, description: "",
    is_published: true, attributes: { guests: 200 },
  };
  await api.owner.createItem(input);
  await api.admin.updateItem(9, input);
  await api.owner.deleteItem(9);
  assertEquals(calls.map((c) => `${c.init.method} ${c.url}`), [
    "GET /api/items?category=cars&body=suv",
    "GET /api/items/9",
    "GET /api/admin/items?shop_id=3&category=tents",
    "GET /api/shop/items",
    "POST /api/shop/items",
    "PUT /api/admin/items/9",
    "DELETE /api/shop/items/9",
  ]);
  assertEquals(JSON.parse(String(calls[4].init.body)), input);
  assertEquals((calls[0].init.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization, undefined);
});
```

`frontend/src/lib/manage_test.ts`
```ts
import { assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import { createApi } from "./api.ts";
import { adminManage, ownerManage } from "./manage.ts";
import type { Uploader } from "./upload.ts";

function setup() {
  const urls: string[] = [];
  const fetchFn = ((input: RequestInfo | URL) => {
    urls.push(String(input));
    return Promise.resolve(new Response(JSON.stringify({ items: [] }), { status: 200 }));
  }) as typeof fetch;
  const api = createApi({ fetch: fetchFn, token: () => "tok", onUnauthorized: () => {} });
  const upload = (() => Promise.reject(new Error("unused"))) as Uploader;
  return { api, upload, urls };
}

Deno.test("the admin editors reach /api/admin and link inside /admin", async () => {
  const { api, upload, urls } = setup();
  const m = adminManage(api, upload);
  await m.calls.listings({ shopId: 3 });
  await m.calls.busyDays(7);
  await m.shops?.();
  assertEquals(urls, ["/api/admin/items?shop_id=3", "/api/admin/items/7/busy-days", "/api/admin/shops"]);
  assertEquals(m.upload, upload);
  assertEquals(
    [m.listingsHref(), m.listingsHref(3), m.newListingHref(3), m.dressHref(7), m.newDressHref(3)],
    ["/admin/listings", "/admin/listings?shop_id=3", "/admin/listings/new?shop_id=3", "/admin/dresses/7", "/admin/dresses/new?shop_id=3"],
  );
  assertEquals(
    [m.itemHref(8), m.newItemHref("cars"), m.newItemHref("national-clothes", 3)],
    ["/admin/items/8", "/admin/items/new?category=cars", "/admin/items/new?category=national-clothes&shop_id=3"],
  );
});

Deno.test("the owner editors reach /api/shop, link inside /shop and never choose a shop", async () => {
  const { api, upload, urls } = setup();
  const m = ownerManage(api, upload);
  await m.calls.listings();
  await m.calls.busyDays(7);
  assertEquals(urls, ["/api/shop/items", "/api/shop/items/7/busy-days"]);
  assertEquals(
    [m.listingsHref(3), m.newListingHref(3), m.dressHref(7), m.newDressHref(3), m.itemHref(8), m.newItemHref("tents", 3)],
    ["/shop", "/shop/listings/new", "/shop/dresses/7", "/shop/dresses/new", "/shop/items/8", "/shop/items/new?category=tents"],
  );
  assertEquals(m.shops, undefined);
});
```

`frontend/src/lib/forms_test.ts`
```ts
import { assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import { categoryDef } from "./catalog.ts";
import {
  cleanShopInput, EMPTY_SHOP_INPUT, emptyItemForm, firstParam, itemFormToInput, itemToForm, ownerReturnPath, shopToInput,
} from "./forms.ts";
import type { AdminShop } from "./types.ts";

const shop: AdminShop = {
  id: 3, slug: "ak-gelin", name: "Ak Gelin", city_id: 2, address: "Mary", phone: "+99365",
  instagram_url: null, tiktok_url: "https://tiktok.com/@akgelin", description: null, is_published: true,
  logo: null, aliases: ["Ак Гелин"], owner: null, created_at: "2026-10-19T00:00:00Z", updated_at: "2026-10-19T00:00:00Z",
};

Deno.test("shopToInput turns null fields into empty strings", () => {
  assertEquals(shopToInput(shop), {
    name: "Ak Gelin", city_id: 2, address: "Mary", phone: "+99365", instagram_url: "",
    tiktok_url: "https://tiktok.com/@akgelin", description: "", is_published: true, aliases: ["Ак Гелин"],
  });
});

Deno.test("cleanShopInput trims text and drops blank or repeated aliases", () => {
  const cleaned = cleanShopInput({
    ...EMPTY_SHOP_INPUT,
    name: "  Ak Gelin ",
    phone: " +993 65 ",
    aliases: [" Ак Гелин ", "", "ак гелин", "AK GELIN", "   "],
  });
  assertEquals(cleaned, { ...EMPTY_SHOP_INPUT, name: "Ak Gelin", phone: "+993 65", aliases: ["Ак Гелин", "AK GELIN"] });
});

Deno.test("firstParam takes the first value of a search param", () => {
  assertEquals(firstParam(undefined), "");
  assertEquals(firstParam("7"), "7");
  assertEquals(firstParam(["5", "6"]), "5");
});
import { dressFormToInput, type DressForm, dressToForm, EMPTY_DRESS_FORM } from "./forms.ts";
import type { AdminDress } from "./types.ts";

Deno.test("dressFormToInput trims, parses the price and orders the sizes", () => {
  const form: DressForm = {
    ...EMPTY_DRESS_FORM, shop_id: 4, title: "  Lale ", price: " 1500 ", color: "ivory",
    silhouette: "a_line", sleeves: "none", sizes: ["L", "XS", "M"],
  };
  assertEquals(dressFormToInput(form), {
    shop_id: 4, title: "Lale", price_manat: 1500, price_is_from: false, description: "", is_published: false,
    color: "ivory", silhouette: "a_line", sleeves: "none", sizes: ["XS", "M", "L"],
  });
});

Deno.test("a blank or non-numeric price becomes 0 so the API reports it", () => {
  for (const price of ["", "12a", "-5", "1.5"]) {
    assertEquals(dressFormToInput({ ...EMPTY_DRESS_FORM, price }).price_manat, 0);
  }
});

Deno.test("dressToForm and dressFormToInput round-trip a saved dress", () => {
  const dress: AdminDress = {
    id: 9, shop_id: 2, title: "Lale", price_manat: 2400, price_is_from: true, description: null,
    is_published: true, color: "blush", silhouette: "mermaid", sleeves: "long", sizes: ["S", "M"],
    images: [], created_at: "2026-10-19T00:00:00Z", updated_at: "2026-10-19T00:00:00Z",
  };
  assertEquals(dressFormToInput(dressToForm(dress)), {
    shop_id: 2, title: "Lale", price_manat: 2400, price_is_from: true, description: "", is_published: true,
    color: "blush", silhouette: "mermaid", sleeves: "long", sizes: ["S", "M"],
  });
});

Deno.test("ownerReturnPath stays inside My shop", () => {
  for (const path of ["/shop", "/shop/dresses/4", "/shop/dresses/new", "/shop?x=1"]) {
    assertEquals(ownerReturnPath(path), path);
  }
  for (const path of ["", "/shops/ak-gelin", "/shopping", "/admin/shops", "https://evil.example/shop", "//evil.example", "/shop/login", "/shop/signup"]) {
    assertEquals(ownerReturnPath(path), "/shop", path);
  }
});

Deno.test("itemFormToInput sends only filled fields, numbers as numbers, choices in order", () => {
  const cars = categoryDef("cars")!.fields;
  const form = {
    ...emptyItemForm("cars", 3),
    title: " Ak limuzin ",
    price: "900",
    attributes: { brand_model: " Lincoln ", body: "limousine", color: "", seats: "8", price_unit: "hour" },
  };
  assertEquals(itemFormToInput(form, cars), {
    shop_id: 3, category: "cars", title: "Ak limuzin", price_manat: 900, price_is_from: false, description: "",
    is_published: false, attributes: { brand_model: "Lincoln", body: "limousine", seats: 8, price_unit: "hour" },
  });
  // Text that is not a whole number goes as is, so the API answers with the field error.
  assertEquals(itemFormToInput({ ...form, price: "9 00", attributes: { seats: "8.5" } }, cars).attributes, { seats: "8.5" });
  assertEquals(itemFormToInput({ ...form, price: "9 00" }, cars).price_manat, 0);
  const clothes = categoryDef("national-clothes")!.fields;
  const robe = { ...emptyItemForm("national-clothes"), attributes: { for_whom: "bride", sizes: ["L", "S"] } };
  assertEquals(itemFormToInput(robe, clothes).attributes, { for_whom: "bride", sizes: ["S", "L"] });
  assertEquals(itemFormToInput({ ...robe, attributes: { sizes: [] } }, clothes).attributes, {});
});

Deno.test("itemToForm turns attributes into editable text", () => {
  const form = itemToForm({
    id: 9, shop_id: 3, category: "tents", title: "Ak çadyr", price_manat: 5000, price_is_from: true, description: null,
    is_published: true, attributes: { guests: 200 }, images: [], created_at: "", updated_at: "",
  });
  assertEquals(form.attributes, { guests: "200" });
  assertEquals([form.price, form.description, form.price_is_from], ["5000", "", true]);
});
```

`frontend/src/components/price_test.ts`
```ts
import { assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import { dictionaries, i18n, translate } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { formatPrice } from "../lib/format.ts";
import { priceText } from "./price.ts";

// Built from the current language's texts, so the test does not depend on (or change) it.
const tx = (key: string, params: Record<string, string>) => translate(dictionaries[i18n.lang()], key, params);

Deno.test("priceText adds a car's unit, and nothing for other listings", () => {
  const exact = (n: number) => tx("price.exact", { price: formatPrice(n) });
  assertEquals(priceText({ price_manat: 1500, price_is_from: false }), exact(1500));
  assertEquals(
    priceText({ price_manat: 800, price_is_from: false, attributes: { price_unit: "hour" } }),
    tx("price.per_hour", { price: exact(800) }),
  );
  assertEquals(
    priceText({ price_manat: 800, price_is_from: true, attributes: { price_unit: "day" } }),
    tx("price.per_day", { price: tx("price.from", { price: formatPrice(800) }) }),
  );
  assertEquals(priceText({ price_manat: 5000, price_is_from: false, attributes: { guests: 200 } }), exact(5000));
});
```

`frontend/src/i18n/i18n_test.ts`
```ts
import { assert, assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import en from "./en.json" with { type: "json" };
import ru from "./ru.json" with { type: "json" };
import tk from "./tk.json" with { type: "json" };
import { createI18n, translate } from "./i18n.ts";
import { memoryKV } from "../lib/storage.ts";
import { CATEGORIES, catLabel, fieldLabel, optionLabel } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import { COLORS, SILHOUETTES, SLEEVES } from "../lib/types.ts";

const dicts: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = { tk, ru, en };

// Every error code the Go API can return, plus the client-side network_error.
const API_ERROR_CODES = [
  "validation_failed", "not_found", "conflict", "invalid_reference", "invalid_json",
  "body_too_large", "internal_error", "invalid_credentials", "unauthorized", "rate_limited",
  "file_too_large", "image_too_large", "invalid_image", "network_error",
];
// Every value the Go API puts in error.fields.
const FIELD_VALUES = [
  "required", "invalid", "too_long", "too_many", "too_large", "must_be_positive", "out_of_range", "taken",
  "too_short", "unknown",
  "exactly_one_of_listing_id_or_shop_id", "must_list_all_images_of_one_listing",
];

Deno.test("tk, ru and en have exactly the same keys", () => {
  const keys = (d: object) => Object.keys(d).sort();
  assertEquals(keys(tk), keys(en));
  assertEquals(keys(ru), keys(en));
});

Deno.test("every message is a non-empty string", () => {
  for (const [lang, dict] of Object.entries(dicts)) {
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(dict)) {
      assert(typeof value === "string" && value.trim() !== "", `${lang} ${key} is empty`);
    }
  }
});

Deno.test("placeholders are the same in every language", () => {
  const placeholders = (s: string) => (s.match(/\{\w+\}/g) ?? []).sort().join(",");
  for (const key of Object.keys(en)) {
    const want = placeholders(dicts.en[key]);
    assertEquals(placeholders(dicts.tk[key]), want, `tk ${key}`);
    assertEquals(placeholders(dicts.ru[key]), want, `ru ${key}`);
  }
});

Deno.test("every enum value, API error code and field value has a message", () => {
  const needed = [
    ...COLORS.map((c) => `color.${c}`),
    ...SILHOUETTES.map((s) => `silhouette.${s}`),
    ...SLEEVES.map((s) => `sleeves.${s}`),
    ...API_ERROR_CODES.map((c) => `error.${c}`),
    ...FIELD_VALUES.map((v) => `field.${v}`),
  ];
  for (const key of needed) assert(key in en, `missing ${key}`);
});

Deno.test("every category, field and option in the catalog has a message", () => {
  for (const c of CATEGORIES) {
    assert(catLabel(c.slug) in en, `missing ${catLabel(c.slug)}`);
    for (const f of c.fields) {
      assert(fieldLabel(c.slug, f.key) in en, `missing ${fieldLabel(c.slug, f.key)}`);
      for (const o of f.options ?? []) {
        assert(optionLabel(c.slug, f.key, o) in en, `missing ${optionLabel(c.slug, f.key, o)}`);
      }
    }
  }
});

Deno.test("translate fills params, then falls back to English, then to the key", () => {
  assertEquals(translate({ a: "Salam, {name}!" }, "a", { name: "Aýna" }), "Salam, Aýna!");
  assertEquals(translate({ a: "{x} {y}" }, "a", { x: 1 }), "1 {y}");
  assertEquals(translate({}, "app.name"), en["app.name"]);
  assertEquals(translate({}, "no.such.key"), "no.such.key");
});

Deno.test("createI18n defaults to Turkmen and remembers the chosen language", () => {
  const kv = memoryKV();
  const i = createI18n(kv);
  assertEquals(i.lang(), "tk");
  assertEquals(i.t("nav.dresses"), tk["nav.dresses"]);
  i.setLang("ru");
  assertEquals(i.t("nav.dresses"), ru["nav.dresses"]);
  assertEquals(kv.get("wr.lang"), "ru");
  assertEquals(createI18n(kv).lang(), "ru");
  assertEquals(createI18n(memoryKV({ "wr.lang": "de" })).lang(), "tk");
});

Deno.test("every month and weekday has a name", () => {
  for (let n = 1; n <= 12; n++) {
    for (const key of [`cal.month.${n}`, `cal.month_of.${n}`]) assert(key in en, `missing ${key}`);
  }
  for (let n = 1; n <= 7; n++) assert(`cal.weekday.${n}` in en, `missing cal.weekday.${n}`);
});
```
