```ts
import data from "./catalog.json" with { type: "json" };
import type { ListItem } from "./types.ts";

/**
 * The rental categories and their own fields. catalog.json is generated from the backend's
 * internal/catalog (go test ./internal/catalog/ -update), so both sides always agree.
 * Texts: cat.<slug>, attr.<slug>.<key> and attr.<slug>.<key>.<option> in the i18n files.
 */
export interface FieldDef {
  key: string;
  kind: "text" | "choice" | "choices" | "number";
  required: boolean;
  options?: string[];
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
  max_len?: number;
  filter?: { param: string; kind: "eq" | "has" | "min" };
}

export interface CategoryDef {
  slug: string;
  /** Dresses keep their own pages, editor and API. */
  own_pages: boolean;
  fields: FieldDef[];
}

export const CATEGORIES = data.categories as CategoryDef[];

/** Every category except dresses: the ones with /c/:slug pages and the item editor. */
export const ITEM_CATEGORIES = CATEGORIES.filter((c) => !c.own_pages);

export function categoryDef(slug: string): CategoryDef | undefined {
  return CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.slug === slug);
}

/** The public list page of a category. */
export function categoryHref(slug: string): string {
  return slug === "dresses" ? "/dresses" : `/c/${slug}`;
}

/** A listing's public page: dresses keep /dresses/:id, everything else is /items/:id. */
export function listingHref(item: Pick<ListItem, "id" | "category">): string {
  return !item.category || item.category === "dresses" ? `/dresses/${item.id}` : `/items/${item.id}`;
}

export const catLabel = (slug: string) => `cat.${slug}`;
export const fieldLabel = (slug: string, key: string) => `attr.${slug}.${key}`;
export const optionLabel = (slug: string, key: string, option: string) => `attr.${slug}.${key}.${option}`;
```

`frontend/src/lib/filters.ts`
```ts
import {
  type Color, COLORS, type Silhouette, SILHOUETTES, type Size, SIZES, type Sleeves, SLEEVES,
} from "./types.ts";

export interface Filters {
  q: string;
  city: string;
  priceMin: number | null;
  priceMax: number | null;
  size: Size | null;
  color: Color | null;
  style: Silhouette | null;
  sleeves: Sleeves | null;
}

export const EMPTY_FILTERS: Filters = {
  q: "", city: "", priceMin: null, priceMax: null, size: null, color: null, style: null, sleeves: null,
};

const MAX_PRICE = 2_147_483_647; // the API rejects anything above int4

function oneOf<T extends string>(allowed: readonly T[], v: string | null): T | null {
  return v !== null && (allowed as readonly string[]).includes(v) ? (v as T) : null;
}

/** A price from a URL query: a positive whole number the API accepts, or null. */
export function priceParam(v: string | null): number | null {
  const s = v?.trim() ?? "";
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(s)) return null;
  const n = Number(s);
  return n > 0 && n <= MAX_PRICE ? n : null;
}

/** What the price fields make of typed text: only the digits count ("1 500" is 1500). */
export function parsePriceInput(s: string): number | null {
  return priceParam(s.replace(/\D/g, ""));
}

/** Reads filters from a URL query; unknown or invalid values are dropped. */
export function parseFilters(query: URLSearchParams): Filters {
  return {
    q: (query.get("q") ?? "").trim(),
    city: (query.get("city") ?? "").trim(),
    priceMin: priceParam(query.get("price_min")),
    priceMax: priceParam(query.get("price_max")),
    size: oneOf(SIZES, query.get("size")),
    color: oneOf(COLORS, query.get("color")),
    style: oneOf(SILHOUETTES, query.get("style")),
    sleeves: oneOf(SLEEVES, query.get("sleeves")),
  };
}

/** Writes filters as a query in a fixed key order, leaving out empty values. */
export function filtersToQuery(f: Filters): URLSearchParams {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();
  const put = (key: string, value: string | number | null) => {
    if (value !== null && value !== "") query.set(key, String(value));
  };
  put("q", f.q.trim());
  put("city", f.city);
  put("price_min", f.priceMin);
  put("price_max", f.priceMax);
  put("size", f.size);
  put("color", f.color);
  put("style", f.style);
  put("sleeves", f.sleeves);
  return query;
}

/** How many filters are set in the filter sheet; the search text is not counted. */
export function countActiveFilters(f: Filters): number {
  return [f.city || null, f.priceMin, f.priceMax, f.size, f.color, f.style, f.sleeves]
    .filter((v) => v !== null).length;
}
```

`frontend/src/lib/itemFilters.ts`
```ts
import type { CategoryDef } from "./catalog.ts";
import { priceParam } from "./filters.ts";

/** A category page's filters: the shared ones plus the category's own, by query param. */
export interface ItemFilters {
  q: string;
  city: string;
  priceMin: number | null;
  priceMax: number | null;
  /** Filter param → value, e.g. { body: "suv", seats_min: "5" }. */
  attrs: Record<string, string>;
}

export const EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS: ItemFilters = { q: "", city: "", priceMin: null, priceMax: null, attrs: {} };

/** The category's filter value for param, if valid ("" otherwise). */
function attrValue(c: CategoryDef, param: string, raw: string | null): string {
  const f = c.fields.find((x) => x.filter?.param === param);
  const v = (raw ?? "").trim();
  if (!f?.filter || v === "") return "";
  if (f.filter.kind === "min") {
    const n = /^\d+$/.test(v) ? Number(v) : NaN;
    return n >= (f.min ?? 1) && n <= (f.max ?? Infinity) ? String(n) : "";
  }
  return f.options?.includes(v) ? v : "";
}

/** Reads a category page's filters from its URL query; unknown or invalid values are dropped. */
export function parseItemFilters(c: CategoryDef, query: URLSearchParams): ItemFilters {
  const attrs: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const f of c.fields) {
    if (!f.filter) continue;
    const v = attrValue(c, f.filter.param, query.get(f.filter.param));
    if (v) attrs[f.filter.param] = v;
  }
  return {
    q: (query.get("q") ?? "").trim(),
    city: (query.get("city") ?? "").trim(),
    priceMin: priceParam(query.get("price_min")),
    priceMax: priceParam(query.get("price_max")),
    attrs,
  };
}

/** Writes filters as a query in a fixed order (the catalog's), leaving out empty values. */
export function itemFiltersToQuery(c: CategoryDef, f: ItemFilters): URLSearchParams {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();
  const put = (key: string, value: string | number | null) => {
    if (value !== null && value !== "") query.set(key, String(value));
  };
  put("q", f.q.trim());
  put("city", f.city);
  put("price_min", f.priceMin);
  put("price_max", f.priceMax);
  for (const field of c.fields) {
    if (field.filter) put(field.filter.param, attrValue(c, field.filter.param, f.attrs[field.filter.param] ?? null));
  }
  return query;
}

/** How many filters are set in the filter sheet; the search text is not counted. */
export function countItemFilters(f: ItemFilters): number {
  return [f.city || null, f.priceMin, f.priceMax].filter((v) => v !== null).length +
    Object.values(f.attrs).filter(Boolean).length;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: API calls, manage links, forms, prices**

`frontend/src/lib/api.ts`
```ts
import type {
  AdminDress, AdminItem, AdminShop, BusyDays, City, DressInput, ItemInput, ListingSummary, Page, PublicDress,
  PublicItem, PublicShop, ShopInput, ShopMe, SignupInput,
} from "./types.ts";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly fields: Record<string, string>;

  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string, fields: Record<string, string> = {}) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
    this.fields = fields;
  }
}

interface ErrorBody {
  error?: { code?: string; message?: string; fields?: Record<string, string> };
}

/** Turns an error response body (or anything else) into an ApiError. */
export function toApiError(
  status: number,
  data: unknown,
  fallbackMessage: string,
  tooLargeCode = "body_too_large",
): ApiError {
  const e = (data as ErrorBody | null)?.error;
  // A proxy rejects an oversized body with its own page, not our JSON envelope.
  const fallbackCode = status === 413 ? tooLargeCode : "internal_error";
  return new ApiError(status, e?.code ?? fallbackCode, e?.message ?? fallbackMessage, e?.fields ?? {});
}

export interface ApiDeps {
  fetch: typeof fetch;
  token: () => string | null;
  onUnauthorized: () => void;
  /** How long a request may take before it is abandoned; defaults to 20 s. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

interface RequestOptions {
  body?: unknown;
  auth?: boolean;
}

const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 20_000;

function withQuery(path: string, query?: URLSearchParams): string {
  const qs = query?.toString() ?? "";
  return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path;
}

export function createApi(deps: ApiDeps) {
  async function request<T>(method: string, path: string, opts: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: "application/json" };
    if (opts.body !== undefined) headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    if (opts.auth) {
      const token = deps.token();
      if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    // AbortSignal.timeout needs Safari 16; a controller works everywhere.
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), deps.timeoutMs ?? REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
    try {
      let res: Response;
      try {
        res = await deps.fetch(path, {
          method,
          headers,
          body: opts.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(opts.body),
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
      } catch {
        throw new ApiError(0, "network_error", "network request failed");
      }
      if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
      let data: unknown;
      try {
        data = await res.json();
      } catch {
        // An error page from a proxy is fine to report by status; a cut-off success is not.
        if (res.ok) throw new ApiError(0, "network_error", "response could not be read");
        data = null;
      }
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (opts.auth && res.status === 401) deps.onUnauthorized();
        throw toApiError(res.status, data, res.statusText);
      }
      return data as T;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  const signedIn = { auth: true } as const;

  /** Listings, photos and busy days: the same calls under /api/admin and /api/shop. */
  function manage(base: string) {
    return {
      dresses: (shopId?: number) =>
        request<{ items: AdminDress[] }>(
          "GET",
          withQuery(`${base}/dresses`, shopId ? new URLSearchParams({ shop_id: String(shopId) }) : undefined),
          signedIn,
        ).then((r) => r.items),
      dress: (id: number) => request<AdminDress>("GET", `${base}/dresses/${id}`, signedIn),
      createDress: (input: DressInput) => request<AdminDress>("POST", `${base}/dresses`, { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      updateDress: (id: number, input: DressInput) =>
        request<AdminDress>("PUT", `${base}/dresses/${id}`, { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      deleteDress: (id: number) => request<void>("DELETE", `${base}/dresses/${id}`, signedIn),
      reorderImages: (ids: number[]) => request<void>("PATCH", `${base}/images/order`, { ...signedIn, body: { ids } }),
      deleteImage: (id: number) => request<void>("DELETE", `${base}/images/${id}`, signedIn),
      /** Every listing in every category, dresses included, for the listing lists. */
      listings: (filter: { shopId?: number; category?: string } = {}) => {
        const query = new URLSearchParams();
        if (filter.shopId) query.set("shop_id", String(filter.shopId));
        if (filter.category) query.set("category", filter.category);
        return request<{ items: ListingSummary[] }>("GET", withQuery(`${base}/items`, query), signedIn)
          .then((r) => r.items);
      },
      item: (id: number) => request<AdminItem>("GET", `${base}/items/${id}`, signedIn),
      createItem: (input: ItemInput) => request<AdminItem>("POST", `${base}/items`, { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      updateItem: (id: number, input: ItemInput) =>
        request<AdminItem>("PUT", `${base}/items/${id}`, { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      deleteItem: (id: number) => request<void>("DELETE", `${base}/items/${id}`, signedIn),
      // Busy days belong to any listing, dresses included.
      busyDays: (listingId: number) => request<BusyDays>("GET", `${base}/items/${listingId}/busy-days`, signedIn),
      markBusy: (listingId: number, day: string) =>
        request<void>("PUT", `${base}/items/${listingId}/busy-days/${day}`, signedIn),
      markFree: (listingId: number, day: string) =>
        request<void>("DELETE", `${base}/items/${listingId}/busy-days/${day}`, signedIn),
    };
  }

  return {
    cities: () => request<{ items: City[] }>("GET", "/api/cities").then((r) => r.items),
    dresses: (query?: URLSearchParams) => request<Page>("GET", withQuery("/api/dresses", query)),
    dress: (id: number | string) => request<PublicDress>("GET", `/api/dresses/${encodeURIComponent(String(id))}`),
    /** Published listings of every category, or of one with ?category=. */
    items: (query?: URLSearchParams) => request<Page>("GET", withQuery("/api/items", query)),
    item: (id: number | string) => request<PublicItem>("GET", `/api/items/${encodeURIComponent(String(id))}`),
    busyDays: (listingId: number) => request<BusyDays>("GET", `/api/items/${listingId}/busy-days`),
    shop: (slug: string, query?: URLSearchParams) =>
      request<PublicShop>("GET", withQuery(`/api/shops/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`, query)),
    login: (email: string, password: string) =>
      request<{ token: string }>("POST", "/api/admin/login", { body: { email, password } }).then((r) => r.token),
    admin: {
      shops: () => request<{ items: AdminShop[] }>("GET", "/api/admin/shops", signedIn).then((r) => r.items),
      shop: (id: number) => request<AdminShop>("GET", `/api/admin/shops/${id}`, signedIn),
      createShop: (input: ShopInput) => request<AdminShop>("POST", "/api/admin/shops", { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      updateShop: (id: number, input: ShopInput) =>
        request<AdminShop>("PUT", `/api/admin/shops/${id}`, { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      deleteShop: (id: number) => request<void>("DELETE", `/api/admin/shops/${id}`, signedIn),
      setOwnerPassword: (shopId: number, password: string) =>
        request<void>("POST", `/api/admin/shops/${shopId}/owner-password`, { ...signedIn, body: { password } }),
      ...manage("/api/admin"),
    },
    /** A shop owner's own account; every call acts on the signed-in owner's shop only. */
    owner: {
      signup: (input: SignupInput) =>
        request<{ token: string }>("POST", "/api/shop/signup", { body: input }).then((r) => r.token),
      login: (phone: string, password: string) =>
        request<{ token: string }>("POST", "/api/shop/login", { body: { phone, password } }).then((r) => r.token),
      me: () => request<ShopMe>("GET", "/api/shop/me", signedIn),
      updateShop: (input: ShopInput) => request<AdminShop>("PUT", "/api/shop/me/shop", { ...signedIn, body: input }),
      ...manage("/api/shop"),
    },
  };
}

export type Api = ReturnType<typeof createApi>;
/** The calls the shared dress, photo and calendar editors make, for an admin or an owner. */
export type ManageCalls = Omit<Api["owner"], "signup" | "login" | "me" | "updateShop">;
```

`frontend/src/lib/manage.ts`
```ts
import { createContext, useContext } from "solid-js";
import type { Api, ManageCalls } from "./api.ts";
import type { AdminShop } from "./types.ts";
import type { Uploader } from "./upload.ts";

/**
 * What the shared listing, photo and calendar editors need: the admin panel provides one for
 * every shop, "My shop" provides one that only ever reaches the owner's own shop.
 */
export interface Manage {
  calls: ManageCalls;
  upload: Uploader;
  /** The listing list to go back to, optionally for one shop. */
  listingsHref: (shopId?: number) => string;
  /** The "New listing" page that asks for the category. */
  newListingHref: (shopId?: number) => string;
  /** The editor page of one dress, or of a new one. */
  dressHref: (id: number) => string;
  newDressHref: (shopId?: number) => string;
  /** The editor page of one non-dress item, or of a new one in a category. */
  itemHref: (id: number) => string;
  newItemHref: (category: string, shopId?: number) => string;
  /** Admins choose a listing's shop from this list; an owner's listings always go to their own shop. */
  shops?: () => Promise<AdminShop[]>;
}

export const ManageContext = createContext<Manage>();

export function useManage(): Manage {
  const m = useContext(ManageContext);
  if (!m) throw new Error("useManage outside a ManageContext provider");
  return m;
}

/** path, with ?shop_id= when a shop is given. */
const withShop = (path: string, shopId?: number) => (shopId ? `${path}?shop_id=${shopId}` : path);

/** The admin panel: every shop, under /admin. */
export function adminManage(api: Api, upload: Uploader): Manage {
  return {
    calls: api.admin,
    upload,
    listingsHref: (shopId) => withShop("/admin/listings", shopId),
    newListingHref: (shopId) => withShop("/admin/listings/new", shopId),
    dressHref: (id) => `/admin/dresses/${id}`,
    newDressHref: (shopId) => withShop("/admin/dresses/new", shopId),
    itemHref: (id) => `/admin/items/${id}`,
    newItemHref: (category, shopId) =>
      `/admin/items/new?category=${encodeURIComponent(category)}${shopId ? `&shop_id=${shopId}` : ""}`,
    shops: () => api.admin.shops(),
  };
}

/** "My shop": the signed-in owner's own shop, under /shop. */
export function ownerManage(api: Api, upload: Uploader): Manage {
  return {
    calls: api.owner,
    upload,
    listingsHref: () => "/shop",
    newListingHref: () => "/shop/listings/new",
    dressHref: (id) => `/shop/dresses/${id}`,
    newDressHref: () => "/shop/dresses/new",
    itemHref: (id) => `/shop/items/${id}`,
    newItemHref: (category) => `/shop/items/new?category=${encodeURIComponent(category)}`,
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/lib/forms.ts`
```ts
import type { FieldDef } from "./catalog.ts";
import {
  type AdminDress, type AdminItem, type AdminShop, type Attributes, type Color, type DressInput, type ItemInput,
  type ShopInput, type Silhouette, type Size, SIZES, type Sleeves,
} from "./types.ts";

export const EMPTY_SHOP_INPUT: ShopInput = {
  name: "", city_id: 0, address: "", phone: "", instagram_url: "", tiktok_url: "", description: "",
  is_published: false, aliases: [],
};

export function shopToInput(s: AdminShop): ShopInput {
  return {
    name: s.name,
    city_id: s.city_id,
    address: s.address,
    phone: s.phone,
    instagram_url: s.instagram_url ?? "",
    tiktok_url: s.tiktok_url ?? "",
    description: s.description ?? "",
    is_published: s.is_published,
    aliases: [...s.aliases],
  };
}

/** Trims every text field and drops blank or repeated aliases (ignoring case) before saving. */
export function cleanShopInput(f: ShopInput): ShopInput {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const aliases: string[] = [];
  for (const raw of f.aliases) {
    const alias = raw.trim();
    const key = alias.toLowerCase();
    if (alias && !seen.has(key)) {
      seen.add(key);
      aliases.push(alias);
    }
  }
  return {
    ...f,
    name: f.name.trim(),
    address: f.address.trim(),
    phone: f.phone.trim(),
    instagram_url: f.instagram_url.trim(),
    tiktok_url: f.tiktok_url.trim(),
    description: f.description.trim(),
    aliases,
  };
}

/** Where a shop owner goes after signing in: only back into "My shop", never to another site. */
export function ownerReturnPath(next: string): string {
  const inside = next === "/shop" || next.startsWith("/shop/") || next.startsWith("/shop?");
  return inside && !next.startsWith("/shop/login") && !next.startsWith("/shop/signup") ? next : "/shop";
}

/** Router search params can hold one value or a list; take the first. */
export function firstParam(v: string | string[] | undefined): string {
  return (Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v) ?? "";
}

/** The dress editor's state: the price stays text while typing, and enums may be unset. */
export interface DressForm {
  shop_id: number;
  title: string;
  price: string;
  price_is_from: boolean;
  description: string;
  is_published: boolean;
  color: Color | "";
  silhouette: Silhouette | "";
  sleeves: Sleeves | "";
  sizes: Size[];
}

export const EMPTY_DRESS_FORM: DressForm = {
  shop_id: 0, title: "", price: "", price_is_from: false, description: "", is_published: false,
  color: "", silhouette: "", sleeves: "", sizes: [],
};

export function dressToForm(d: AdminDress): DressForm {
  return {
    shop_id: d.shop_id,
    title: d.title,
    price: String(d.price_manat),
    price_is_from: d.price_is_from,
    description: d.description ?? "",
    is_published: d.is_published,
    color: d.color,
    silhouette: d.silhouette,
    sleeves: d.sleeves,
    sizes: [...d.sizes],
  };
}

/**
 * Builds the API input. A blank or non-numeric price becomes 0 and unset enums stay "",
 * so the API answers with the right field errors. Sizes come out in XS…XXL order.
 */
export function dressFormToInput(f: DressForm): DressInput {
  const price = f.price.trim();
  return {
    shop_id: f.shop_id,
    title: f.title.trim(),
    price_manat: /^\d+$/.test(price) ? Number(price) : 0,
    price_is_from: f.price_is_from,
    description: f.description.trim(),
    is_published: f.is_published,
    color: f.color as Color,
    silhouette: f.silhouette as Silhouette,
    sleeves: f.sleeves as Sleeves,
    sizes: SIZES.filter((s) => f.sizes.includes(s)),
  };
}

/** The item editor's state: price and numbers stay text while typing; choices may be unset. */
export interface ItemForm {
  shop_id: number;
  category: string;
  title: string;
  price: string;
  price_is_from: boolean;
  description: string;
  is_published: boolean;
  /** Field key → text (text, choice and number fields) or picked options (choices). */
  attributes: Record<string, string | string[]>;
}

export function emptyItemForm(category: string, shopId = 0): ItemForm {
  return {
    shop_id: shopId, category, title: "", price: "", price_is_from: false, description: "", is_published: false,
    attributes: {},
  };
}

export function itemToForm(it: AdminItem): ItemForm {
  const attributes: Record<string, string | string[]> = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(it.attributes)) {
    attributes[key] = Array.isArray(value) ? [...value] : String(value);
  }
  return {
    shop_id: it.shop_id,
    category: it.category,
    title: it.title,
    price: String(it.price_manat),
    price_is_from: it.price_is_from,
    description: it.description ?? "",
    is_published: it.is_published,
    attributes,
  };
}

/**
 * Builds the API input from the category's fields. Empty values are left out, numbers are
 * sent as numbers (text that is not a whole number is sent as is, so the API reports it),
 * and a blank or non-numeric price becomes 0 so the API answers with the field error.
 */
export function itemFormToInput(f: ItemForm, fields: FieldDef[]): ItemInput {
  const price = f.price.trim();
  const attributes: Attributes = {};
  for (const field of fields) {
    const raw = f.attributes[field.key];
    if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
      if (raw.length > 0) attributes[field.key] = (field.options ?? []).filter((o) => raw.includes(o));
      continue;
    }
    const text = (raw ?? "").trim();
    if (text === "") continue;
    attributes[field.key] = field.kind === "number" && /^\d+$/.test(text) ? Number(text) : text;
  }
  return {
    shop_id: f.shop_id,
    category: f.category,
    title: f.title.trim(),
    price_manat: /^\d+$/.test(price) ? Number(price) : 0,
    price_is_from: f.price_is_from,
    description: f.description.trim(),
    is_published: f.is_published,
    attributes,
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/components/price.ts`
```ts
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { formatPrice } from "../lib/format.ts";
import type { Attributes } from "../lib/types.ts";

/** "1 500 TMT", "from 1 500 TMT", or with a car's unit "800 TMT / hour", in the current language. */
export function priceText(p: { price_manat: number; price_is_from: boolean; attributes?: Attributes }): string {
  const price = t(p.price_is_from ? "price.from" : "price.exact", { price: formatPrice(p.price_manat) });
  const unit = p.attributes?.price_unit;
  if (unit === "hour") return t("price.per_hour", { price });
  if (unit === "day") return t("price.per_day", { price });
  return price;
}
```

### Task 6: Shared building blocks, with no visible change

This task changes no behaviour. It splits the filter sheet into a reusable `Sheet` and filter fields, and extracts the "Back restores the list" logic. It also renames the dress tile and grid to listing tile and grid, and renames the calendar props from dress to listing.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `frontend/src/components/Sheet.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/components/FilterFields.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/lib/rememberedList.ts`
- Rename:

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: from Task 5, `listingHref` and `priceParam`.
- Produces:
  - `Sheet(props: {open, title, onClose, onOpen?, children, footer})`.
  - `ChoiceGroup`, `CityField`, `NumberInput` and `PriceFields`, in `components/FilterFields.tsx`.
  - `createRememberedList(memory: ListMemory<T>, key: () => string, fetchPage)`, in `lib/rememberedList.ts`.
  - `ListingTile` links with `listingHref(item)`, and `ListingGrid` replaces `DressGrid`.
  - `Availability(props: {listingId})` and `AvailabilityEditor(props: {listingId})`.

`frontend/src/components/Sheet.tsx`
```tsx
import { createEffect, type JSX, on, onCleanup, Show } from "solid-js";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { focusFirst, lockScroll, trapTab } from "../lib/dom.ts";
import { CloseIcon } from "./icons.tsx";

/**
 * A bottom-sheet dialog: it locks page scroll, moves focus inside, keeps Tab inside, closes on
 * Escape or a tap outside, and gives focus back to the opener when it closes.
 */
export default function Sheet(props: {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  /** Called each time the sheet opens, before it is shown. */
  onOpen?: () => void;
  children: JSX.Element;
  footer: JSX.Element;
}) {
  let panel: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  createEffect(on(() => props.open, (open) => {
    if (!open) return;
    props.onOpen?.();
    const opener = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const unlock = lockScroll();
    queueMicrotask(() => panel && focusFirst(panel));
    onCleanup(() => {
      unlock();
      opener?.focus();
    });
  }));

  return (
    <Show when={props.open}>
      <div
        class="fixed inset-0 z-40"
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Escape") props.onClose();
          else if (panel) trapTab(e, panel);
        }}
      >
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40" aria-hidden="true" onClick={() => props.onClose()} />
        <div
          ref={panel}
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby="sheet-title"
          tabindex="-1"
          class="sheet-enter outline-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 mx-auto flex max-h-[88vh] max-w-lg flex-col rounded-t-2xl bg-white"
        >
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-veil px-5 py-2">
            <h2 id="sheet-title" class="text-2xl">{props.title}</h2>
            <button type="button" class="btn-icon" aria-label={t("common.close")} onClick={() => props.onClose()}>
              <CloseIcon />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div class="flex-1 space-y-6 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-5">{props.children}</div>
          <div class="flex gap-3 border-t border-veil px-5 py-3">{props.footer}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Show>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/FilterFields.tsx`
```tsx
import { For, type JSX, Show } from "solid-js";
import { i18n, t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { useCities } from "../lib/cities.ts";
import { parsePriceInput } from "../lib/filters.ts";
import { cityName } from "../lib/format.ts";
import type { Color } from "../lib/types.ts";
import ColorSwatch from "./ColorSwatch.tsx";

export interface Choice<T extends string> {
  value: T;
  label: string;
  swatch?: Color;
}

/** One single-choice filter as radio chips, with "Any" first. */
export function ChoiceGroup<T extends string>(props: {
  legend: string;
  name: string;
  options: Choice<T>[];
  value: T | null;
  onChange: (v: T | null) => void;
}) {
  const chip = (value: T | null, label: string, swatch?: Color): JSX.Element => (
    <label class="chip">
      <input
        type="radio"
        class="sr-only"
        name={props.name}
        checked={props.value === value}
        onChange={() => props.onChange(value)}
      />
      <Show when={swatch}>{(s) => <ColorSwatch color={s()} />}</Show>
      <span>{label}</span>
    </label>
  );
  return (
    <fieldset>
      <legend class="text-sm font-semibold">{props.legend}</legend>
      <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {chip(null, t("filters.any"))}
        <For each={props.options}>{(o) => chip(o.value, o.label, o.swatch)}</For>
      </div>
    </fieldset>
  );
}

export function CityField(props: { value: string; onChange: (slug: string) => void }) {
  const cities = useCities();
  return (
    <label class="block">
      <span class="text-sm font-semibold">{t("filters.city")}</span>
      <select class="field-input mt-1" value={props.value} onChange={(e) => props.onChange(e.currentTarget.value)}>
        <option value="" selected={!props.value}>{t("filters.any_city")}</option>
        <For each={cities()}>
          {(c) => (
            <option value={c.slug} selected={c.slug === props.value}>
              {cityName(c, i18n.lang())}
            </option>
          )}
        </For>
      </select>
    </label>
  );
}

const numberValue = (n: number | null) => (n === null ? "" : String(n));

/** A whole-number input that shows what it understood ("1 500" becomes 1500). */
export function NumberInput(props: { value: number | null; onChange: (n: number | null) => void; label: string; small?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <label class="block">
      <span classList={{ "text-xs text-silver-ink": props.small, "text-sm font-semibold": !props.small }}>{props.label}</span>
      <input
        class="field-input mt-1"
        inputmode="numeric"
        value={numberValue(props.value)}
        onInput={(e) => {
          const n = parsePriceInput(e.currentTarget.value);
          props.onChange(n);
          e.currentTarget.value = numberValue(n); // show what was understood
        }}
      />
    </label>
  );
}

export function PriceFields(props: {
  min: number | null;
  max: number | null;
  onMin: (n: number | null) => void;
  onMax: (n: number | null) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <fieldset>
      <legend class="text-sm font-semibold">{t("filters.price")}</legend>
      <div class="mt-1 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <NumberInput small label={t("filters.price_min")} value={props.min} onChange={props.onMin} />
        <NumberInput small label={t("filters.price_max")} value={props.max} onChange={props.onMax} />
      </div>
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/FilterSheet.tsx`
```tsx
import { createSignal } from "solid-js";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { EMPTY_FILTERS, type Filters } from "../lib/filters.ts";
import { COLORS, SILHOUETTES, SIZES, SLEEVES } from "../lib/types.ts";
import { ChoiceGroup, CityField, PriceFields } from "./FilterFields.tsx";
import Sheet from "./Sheet.tsx";

/** The dress list's filters in a bottom sheet. Changes stay in a draft until "Show dresses". */
export default function FilterSheet(props: {
  open: boolean;
  value: Filters;
  onApply: (f: Filters) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = createSignal<Filters>({ ...props.value });
  const set = <K extends keyof Filters>(key: K, value: Filters[K]) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, [key]: value }));

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={props.open}
      title={t("filters.title")}
      onOpen={() => setDraft({ ...props.value })}
      onClose={props.onClose}
      footer={
        <>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" onClick={() => setDraft({ ...EMPTY_FILTERS, q: draft().q })}>
            {t("filters.reset")}
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary flex-1" onClick={() => props.onApply(draft())}>
            {t("filters.apply")}
          </button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <CityField value={draft().city} onChange={(v) => set("city", v)} />
      <PriceFields
        min={draft().priceMin}
        max={draft().priceMax}
        onMin={(n) => set("priceMin", n)}
        onMax={(n) => set("priceMax", n)}
      />
      <ChoiceGroup
        legend={t("filters.size")}
        name="size"
        options={SIZES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s }))}
        value={draft().size}
        onChange={(v) => set("size", v)}
      />
      <ChoiceGroup
        legend={t("filters.color")}
        name="color"
        options={COLORS.map((c) => ({ value: c, label: t(`color.${c}`), swatch: c }))}
        value={draft().color}
        onChange={(v) => set("color", v)}
      />
      <ChoiceGroup
        legend={t("filters.style")}
        name="style"
        options={SILHOUETTES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`silhouette.${s}`) }))}
        value={draft().style}
        onChange={(v) => set("style", v)}
      />
      <ChoiceGroup
        legend={t("filters.sleeves")}
        name="sleeves"
        options={SLEEVES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`sleeves.${s}`) }))}
        value={draft().sleeves}
        onChange={(v) => set("sleeves", v)}
      />
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { A } from "@solidjs/router";

import { listingHref } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import type { ListItem } from "../lib/types.ts";
import { FavoriteIconButton } from "./FavoriteButton.tsx";

/** One listing in a grid, of any category: photo, price, title and shop. */
export default function ListingTile(props: { item: ListItem; eager?: boolean }) {
  return (

      <A href={listingHref(props.item)} class="block">
        <div class="aspect-[3/4] overflow-hidden rounded-xs bg-veil">

                alt={props.item.title}
                loading={props.eager ? "eager" : "lazy"}
                fetchpriority={props.eager ? "high" : undefined}
                decoding="async"
                class="h-full w-full object-cover"
              />
            )}

        </div>
        <p class="mt-3 font-semibold text-garnet">{priceText(props.item)}</p>
        <h3 class="mt-0.5 truncate font-sans text-base text-ink">{props.item.title}</h3>

      </A>
      <FavoriteIconButton id={props.item.id} class="absolute right-2 top-2" />
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { For } from "solid-js";
import type { ListItem } from "../lib/types.ts";
import ListingTile from "./ListingTile.tsx";
import { GRID_CLASS } from "./grid.ts";

/** The first row is on screen at load: its photos should not wait for lazy loading. */
const EAGER_COUNT = 4;

export default function ListingGrid(props: { items: ListItem[] }) {
  return (
    <ul class={GRID_CLASS}>
      <For each={props.items}>
        {(item, i) => (
          <li>
            <ListingTile item={item} eager={i() < EAGER_COUNT} />
          </li>
        )}
      </For>
    </ul>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The remembered list and the dress list**

`frontend/src/lib/rememberedList.ts`
```ts
import { createEffect, on, untrack } from "solid-js";
import { consumeTraversal } from "./history.ts";
import { createPagedList, type PagedList, type PagedSnapshot } from "./paged.ts";
import type { Page } from "./types.ts";

/** Where a page keeps its last list, at module level so it outlives the page. */
export interface ListMemory<T> {
  saved?: { key: string; snapshot: PagedSnapshot<T> };
}

/**
 * A paged list for key (a query string). Back/Forward brings back the pages it had, filled in
 * at once so the router can restore the scroll position; any other visit, and every key
 * change, starts from a fresh first page.
 */
export function createRememberedList<T>(
  memory: ListMemory<T>,
  key: () => string,
  fetchPage: (page: number) => Promise<Page<T>>,
): PagedList<T> {
  const list = createPagedList(fetchPage);
  // The key the items on screen belong to.
  let listKey = "";
  const show = (k: string, mayRestore: boolean) => {
    listKey = k;
    if (mayRestore && memory.saved?.key === k) list.restore(memory.saved.snapshot);
    else void list.reset();
  };
  show(untrack(key), consumeTraversal());
  createEffect(on(key, (k) => show(k, false), { defer: true }));
  createEffect(() => {
    const snapshot = list.snapshot();
    if (snapshot.items.length > 0) memory.saved = { key: listKey, snapshot };
  });
  return list;
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/Dresses.tsx`
```tsx
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createMemo, createSignal, Match, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import ListingGrid from "../components/ListingGrid.tsx";
import EmptyState from "../components/EmptyState.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import FilterSheet from "../components/FilterSheet.tsx";
import { FilterIcon } from "../components/icons.tsx";
import LoadMore from "../components/LoadMore.tsx";
import SearchBox from "../components/SearchBox.tsx";
import { GridSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import { countActiveFilters, EMPTY_FILTERS, type Filters, filtersToQuery, parseFilters } from "../lib/filters.ts";
import { createRememberedList, type ListMemory } from "../lib/rememberedList.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";
import type { ListItem } from "../lib/types.ts";

const PER_PAGE = "24";

/** The last list shown, so Back from a dress finds the same pages instead of a fresh first page. */
const memory: ListMemory<ListItem> = {};

export default function Dresses() {
  usePageTitle(() => t("dresses.title"));
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const filters = createMemo(() => parseFilters(new URLSearchParams(location.search)), EMPTY_FILTERS, {
    equals: (a, b) => filtersToQuery(a).toString() === filtersToQuery(b).toString(),
  });
  const [sheetOpen, setSheetOpen] = createSignal(false);

  const list = createRememberedList(memory, () => filtersToQuery(filters()).toString(), (page) => {
    const query = filtersToQuery(filters());
    query.set("page", String(page));
    query.set("per_page", PER_PAGE);
    return api.dresses(query);
  });

  const apply = (f: Filters) => {
    setSheetOpen(false);
    const qs = filtersToQuery(f).toString();
    navigate(qs ? `/dresses?${qs}` : "/dresses");
  };
  const active = () => countActiveFilters(filters());
  const firstLoad = () => list.items().length === 0;

  return (
    <div class="pt-8">
      <h1 class="text-4xl">{t("dresses.title")}</h1>
      <div class="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div class="flex-1">
          <SearchBox value={filters().q} onSearch={(q) => apply({ ...filters(), q })} />
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="btn btn-outline"
          classList={{ "border-garnet text-garnet-deep": active() > 0 }}
          aria-haspopup="dialog"
          onClick={() => setSheetOpen(true)}
        >
          <FilterIcon />
          {active() > 0 ? t("dresses.filters_active", { count: active() }) : t("dresses.filters")}
        </button>
      </div>
      <p class="mt-4 min-h-6 text-sm text-silver-ink" aria-live="polite">
        <Show when={!(list.loading() && firstLoad()) && list.error() === null}>{t("dresses.found", { count: list.total() })}</Show>
      </p>

      <div class="mt-4">
        <Switch>
          <Match when={firstLoad() && list.error() !== null}>
            <ErrorState error={list.error()} onRetry={() => void list.retry()} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={firstLoad() && list.loading()}>
            <GridSkeleton />
          </Match>
          <Match when={firstLoad()}>
            <EmptyState title={t("dresses.empty_title")} body={t("dresses.empty_body")}>
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" onClick={() => apply(EMPTY_FILTERS)}>
                {t("dresses.clear")}
              </button>
            </EmptyState>
          </Match>
          <Match when={!firstLoad()}>
            <ListingGrid items={list.items()} />
            <LoadMore list={list} />
          </Match>
        </Switch>
      </div>

      <FilterSheet open={sheetOpen()} value={filters()} onApply={apply} onClose={() => setSheetOpen(false)} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { createResource, Match, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import ErrorState from "./ErrorState.tsx";
import MonthCalendar from "./MonthCalendar.tsx";

/** A listing page's read-only calendar of busy days, with a legend and a reminder to call. */
export default function Availability(props: { listingId: number }) {
  const [data, { refetch }] = createResource(() => props.listingId, (id) => api.busyDays(id));
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="availability-title" class="border-t border-veil pt-6">
      <h2 id="availability-title" class="text-2xl">{t("avail.title")}</h2>
      <div class="mt-4 max-w-sm">
        <Switch>
          <Match when={data.error}>
            <ErrorState error={data.error} onRetry={refetch} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={!data()}>
            <div class="h-[25rem] animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-veil" aria-busy="true" aria-label={t("common.loading")} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={data()}>
            {(d) => (
              <>
                <MonthCalendar window={d()} busy={new Set(d().days)} />
                <ul class="mt-3 flex gap-5 text-sm">
                  <li class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span aria-hidden="true" class="inline-flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded bg-blush text-[10px] text-garnet-deep line-through">14</span>
                    {t("avail.busy")}
                  </li>
                  <li class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span aria-hidden="true" class="inline-flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded border border-silver text-[10px]">14</span>
                    {t("avail.free")}
                  </li>
                </ul>
                <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("avail.note")}</p>
              </>
            )}
          </Match>
        </Switch>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
}

export default function Dress() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [dress, { refetch }] = createResource(() => params.id, (id) => api.dress(id));
  usePageTitle(() => (dress.state === "ready" ? dress()?.title : undefined));

  return (
    <Switch>
      <Match when={dress.error}>
        <Show when={isNotFound(dress.error)} fallback={<ErrorState error={dress.error} onRetry={refetch} />}>
          <NotFound />
        </Show>
      </Match>
      <Match when={!dress()}>
        <DetailSkeleton />
      </Match>
      <Match when={dress()} keyed>{(d) => <DressView dress={d} />}</Match>
    </Switch>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/admin/AvailabilityEditor.tsx`
```tsx
import { createEffect, createResource, createSignal, Match, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { t } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { ApiError } from "../../lib/api.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import ErrorState from "../ErrorState.tsx";
import MonthCalendar, { dayLabel } from "../MonthCalendar.tsx";

const withDay = (set: ReadonlySet<string>, day: string, on: boolean): ReadonlySet<string> => {
  const next = new Set(set);
  if (on) next.add(day);
  else next.delete(day);
  return next;
};

/** Tap a day to mark it busy or free; every tap saves at once and reverts if the save fails. */
export default function AvailabilityEditor(props: { listingId: number }) {
  const m = useManage();
  const [data, { refetch }] = createResource(() => props.listingId, (id) => m.calls.busyDays(id));
  const [busy, setBusy] = createSignal<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const [pending, setPending] = createSignal<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const [status, setStatus] = createSignal("");
  const [failure, setFailure] = createSignal("");
  createEffect(() => {
    if (data.state === "ready") setBusy(new Set(data()?.days ?? []));
  });

  // Set when the server says a day left the window (the page stayed open past midnight).
  let windowStale = false;

  async function toggle(day: string) {
    if (pending().has(day)) return;
    const makeBusy = !busy().has(day);
    setBusy((s) => withDay(s, day, makeBusy));
    setPending((s) => withDay(s, day, true));
    setFailure("");
    try {
      await (makeBusy ? m.calls.markBusy(props.listingId, day) : m.calls.markFree(props.listingId, day));
      setStatus(t(makeBusy ? "avail.marked_busy" : "avail.marked_free", { date: dayLabel(day) }));
    } catch (e) {
      setBusy((s) => withDay(s, day, !makeBusy));
      setFailure(t("avail.save_failed", { date: dayLabel(day) }));
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.fields.day === "out_of_range") windowStale = true;
    } finally {
      setPending((s) => withDay(s, day, false));
      // Reload the window once nothing is saving, so optimistic days are not overwritten.
      if (windowStale && pending().size === 0) {
        windowStale = false;
        void refetch();
      }
    }
  }

  return (
    <Switch>
      <Match when={data.error}>
        <ErrorState error={data.error} onRetry={refetch} />
      </Match>
      <Match when={!data()}>
        <p class="text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("common.loading")}</p>
      </Match>
      <Match when={data()}>
        {(d) => (
          <div class="max-w-sm">
            <MonthCalendar window={d()} busy={busy()} pending={pending()} onToggle={(day) => void toggle(day)} />
            <p role="status" aria-live="polite" class="sr-only">{status()}</p>
            <Show when={failure()}>
              <p role="alert" class="mt-3 text-sm text-garnet">{failure()}</p>
            </Show>
          </div>
        )}
      </Match>
    </Switch>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/admin/DressEdit.tsx`
```tsx
import { A, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createEffect, createResource, createSignal, For, Match, on, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { createStore, reconcile } from "solid-js/store";
import AvailabilityEditor from "../../components/admin/AvailabilityEditor.tsx";
import { CheckboxField, FormActions, SelectField, TextArea, TextField } from "../../components/admin/Field.tsx";
import PhotoManager from "../../components/admin/PhotoManager.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { t, tr } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { fieldMessage, validationFields } from "../../lib/errors.ts";
import { type DressForm, dressFormToInput, dressToForm, EMPTY_DRESS_FORM, firstParam } from "../../lib/forms.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../../lib/title.ts";
import { COLORS, SILHOUETTES, SIZES, SLEEVES } from "../../lib/types.ts";

/**
 * The editor for one dress (or a new one); remounted per dress by DressEdit below.
 * Admins and shop owners share it: useManage() says where it saves and links to.
 */
function DressEditor(props: { id: number | null }) {
  const m = useManage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [search] = useSearchParams();
  const id = () => props.id;
  const [dress, { mutate, refetch }] = createResource(id, (dressId) => m.calls.dress(dressId));
  const [shops] = createResource(() => m.shops?.() ?? Promise.resolve([]));
  const [form, setForm] = createStore<DressForm>({
    ...EMPTY_DRESS_FORM,
    sizes: [],
    shop_id: Number(firstParam(search.shop_id)) || 0,
  });
  // Refill the form only when the saved dress changes, not when just its photos do.
  createEffect(on(() => (dress.state === "ready" ? dress()?.updated_at : undefined), (stamp) => {
    const d = dress.state === "ready" ? dress() : undefined;
    if (stamp && d) setForm(reconcile(dressToForm(d)));
  }));
  const [errors, setErrors] = createSignal<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [error, setError] = createSignal<unknown>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = createSignal(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = createSignal(false);
  const err = (field: string) => errors()[field];
  usePageTitle(() => (id() ? form.title || t("admin.edit") : t("admin.new_dress")));

  async function save(e: SubmitEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setSaved(false);
    setErrors({});
    setError(null);
    try {
      const input = dressFormToInput({ ...form, sizes: [...form.sizes] });
      const current = id();
      const result = current ? await m.calls.updateDress(current, input) : await m.calls.createDress(input);
      setSaved(true);
      if (current) mutate((prev) => ({ ...result, images: prev?.images ?? result.images }));
      else navigate(m.dressHref(result.id), { replace: true });
    } catch (e) {
      setErrors(validationFields(e));
      setError(() => e);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function remove() {
    const current = id();
    if (!current || !confirm(t("admin.delete_dress_confirm"))) return;
    try {
      await m.calls.deleteDress(current);
      navigate(m.listingsHref(form.shop_id), { replace: true });
    } catch (e) {
      setError(() => e);
    }
  }

  const choose = { value: "", label: t("admin.choose") };
  const shopOptions = () => [
    choose,
    ...(shops.state === "ready" ? shops() ?? [] : []).map((s) => ({ value: String(s.id), label: s.name })),
  ];

  return (
    <div class="max-w-2xl">
      <A href={m.listingsHref()} class="text-sm text-silver-ink hover:text-ink">{t("common.back")}</A>
      <h1 class="mt-2 text-3xl">{id() ? form.title || t("admin.edit") : t("admin.new_dress")}</h1>
      <Switch>
        <Match when={dress.error}>
          <ErrorState error={dress.error} onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
        </Match>
        <Match when={id() !== null && !dress()}>
          <p class="mt-6 text-silver-ink">{t("common.loading")}</p>
        </Match>
        <Match when={id() === null || dress()}>
          <form class="mt-6 space-y-5 rounded-lg bg-white p-5" novalidate onSubmit={save}>
            <Show when={m.shops}>
              <SelectField
                label={t("admin.shop")}
                value={form.shop_id ? String(form.shop_id) : ""}
                options={shopOptions()}
                onChange={(v) => setForm("shop_id", Number(v) || 0)}
                error={err("shop_id")}
              />
            </Show>
            <TextField
              label={t("dressform.title")}
              value={form.title}
              onInput={(v) => setForm("title", v)}
              error={err("title")}
            />
            <div class="grid items-end gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <TextField
                label={t("dressform.price")}
                inputmode="numeric"
                value={form.price}
                onInput={(v) => setForm("price", v)}
                error={err("price_manat")}
              />
              <CheckboxField
                label={t("dressform.price_is_from")}
                checked={form.price_is_from}
                onChange={(v) => setForm("price_is_from", v)}
              />
            </div>
            <div class="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <SelectField
                label={t("dressform.color")}
                value={form.color}
                options={[choose, ...COLORS.map((c) => ({ value: c, label: t(`color.${c}`) }))]}
                onChange={(v) => setForm("color", v as DressForm["color"])}
                error={err("color")}
              />
              <SelectField
                label={t("dressform.silhouette")}
                value={form.silhouette}
                options={[choose, ...SILHOUETTES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`silhouette.${s}`) }))]}
                onChange={(v) => setForm("silhouette", v as DressForm["silhouette"])}
                error={err("silhouette")}
              />
              <SelectField
                label={t("dressform.sleeves")}
                value={form.sleeves}
                options={[choose, ...SLEEVES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`sleeves.${s}`) }))]}
                onChange={(v) => setForm("sleeves", v as DressForm["sleeves"])}
                error={err("sleeves")}
              />
            </div>
            <fieldset>
              <legend class="text-sm font-semibold">{t("dressform.sizes")}</legend>
              <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <For each={SIZES}>
                  {(size) => (
                    <label class="chip">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        class="sr-only"
                        checked={form.sizes.includes(size)}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setForm(
                            "sizes",
                            e.currentTarget.checked ? [...form.sizes, size] : form.sizes.filter((s) => s !== size),
                          )}
                      />
                      {size}
                    </label>
                  )}
                </For>
              </div>
              <Show when={err("sizes")}>
                {(e) => <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-garnet">{fieldMessage(e(), tr)}</p>}
              </Show>
            </fieldset>
            <TextArea
              label={t("dressform.description")}
              value={form.description}
              onInput={(v) => setForm("description", v)}
              error={err("description")}
            />
            <CheckboxField
              label={t("admin.published")}
              hint={t("admin.published_hint")}
              checked={form.is_published}
              onChange={(v) => setForm("is_published", v)}
            />
            <FormActions saving={saving()} saved={saved()} error={error()} onDelete={id() ? remove : undefined} />
          </form>

          <section class="mt-8 rounded-lg bg-white p-5">
            <h2 class="text-2xl">{t("photos.title")}</h2>
            <div class="mt-4">
              <Show
                when={id() !== null && dress()}
                fallback={<p class="text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("photos.save_first")}</p>}
              >
                {(d) => (
                  <PhotoManager
                    listingId={d().id}
                    title={d().title}
                    images={d().images}
                    onChange={(images) => mutate({ ...d(), images })}
                  />
                )}
              </Show>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section class="mt-8 rounded-lg bg-white p-5">
            <h2 class="text-2xl">{t("avail.title")}</h2>
            <div class="mt-4">
              <Show
                when={id() !== null && dress()}
                fallback={<p class="text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("avail.save_first")}</p>}
              >
                {(d) => <AvailabilityEditor listingId={d().id} />}
              </Show>
            </div>
          </section>
        </Match>
      </Switch>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * /admin/dresses/new (optionally ?shop_id=), /admin/dresses/:id, /shop/dresses/new and
 * /shop/dresses/:id; a fresh editor per dress.
 */
export default function DressEdit() {
  const params = useParams();
  return (
    <Show when={params.id ?? "new"} keyed>
      {(key) => <DressEditor id={key === "new" ? null : Number(key)} />}
    </Show>
  );
}
```

### Task 7: Brides browse every category

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `frontend/src/components/CategoryBar.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/components/ItemFilterSheet.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/components/AttributeList.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/pages/Category.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/pages/Item.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/pages/Search.tsx`
- Modify:
  - `frontend/src/components/icons.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/components/Header.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/lib/types.ts`
  - `frontend/src/pages/Home.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/pages/Shop.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/pages/Favorites.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/main.tsx`
  - `frontend/src/i18n/en.json`, `ru.json` and `tk.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - from Task 5: catalog helpers, `itemFilters`, `api.items`, `api.item` and `priceText`;
  - from Task 6: `Sheet`, `FilterFields`, `createRememberedList`, `ListingGrid` and `Availability`;
  - from Task 4: `PublicShop.items` and `categories`.
- Produces:
  - **Routes:** `/c/:slug` (any non-dress category; anything else shows NotFound), `/items/:id` (a dress redirects to `/dresses/:id`) and `/search?q=&category=`.
  - **Home:** a category bar and the newest listings from every category. The search box goes to `/search`.
  - **Shop page:** category chips when the shop has more than one category.
  - **Favorites:** loaded through `/api/items`.
  - `PublicShop.items` and `PublicShop.categories` replace `PublicShop.dresses`.
  - **i18n:**
    - new keys `filters.apply_items`, `filters.at_least`, `home.categories`, `home.see_all_items`, `items.empty_title`, `item.about`, `search.title`, `search.all`, `search.empty_body` and `shop.listings`;
    - several dress-only texts made general;
    - `shop.dresses` and `home.see_all` removed.

```tsx
      class={props.class ?? "h-5 w-5"}

      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      stroke-width="1.6"
      stroke-linecap="round"
```

`frontend/src/components/CategoryBar.tsx`
```tsx
import { A } from "@solidjs/router";
import { For, type JSX } from "solid-js";
import { tr } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { CATEGORIES, catLabel, categoryHref } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import { CarIcon, DressIcon, RibbonIcon, RobeIcon, TentIcon } from "./icons.tsx";

const ICONS: Record<string, (p: { class?: string }) => JSX.Element> = {
  "dresses": DressIcon,
  "cars": CarIcon,
  "national-clothes": RobeIcon,
  "tents": TentIcon,
  "car-decorations": RibbonIcon,
};

/** One button per category; on narrow screens the row scrolls sideways inside itself. */
export default function CategoryBar() {
  return (
    <nav aria-label={tr("home.categories")}>
      <ul class="-mx-4 flex snap-x scroll-px-4 gap-3 overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-2 sm:mx-0 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-5 sm:overflow-visible sm:px-0">
        <For each={CATEGORIES}>
          {(c) => {
            const Icon = ICONS[c.slug] ?? DressIcon;
            return (
              <li class="w-28 shrink-0 snap-start sm:w-auto">
                <A
                  href={categoryHref(c.slug)}
                  class="flex h-full min-h-24 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-veil bg-white px-2 py-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold hover:border-garnet hover:text-garnet"
                >
                  <Icon class="h-7 w-7 text-garnet" />
                  <span>{tr(catLabel(c.slug))}</span>
                </A>
              </li>
            );
          }}
        </For>
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/ItemFilterSheet.tsx`
```tsx
import { createSignal, For, Match, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { t, tr } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { type CategoryDef, fieldLabel, optionLabel } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import { EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, type ItemFilters } from "../lib/itemFilters.ts";
import { ChoiceGroup, CityField, NumberInput, PriceFields } from "./FilterFields.tsx";
import Sheet from "./Sheet.tsx";

/** A category page's filters, built from the category's field definitions. */
export default function ItemFilterSheet(props: {
  category: CategoryDef;
  open: boolean;
  value: ItemFilters;
  onApply: (f: ItemFilters) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = createSignal<ItemFilters>({ ...props.value });
  const setAttr = (param: string, value: string) =>
    setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, attrs: { ...d.attrs, [param]: value } }));
  const slug = () => props.category.slug;

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={props.open}
      title={t("filters.title")}
      onOpen={() => setDraft({ ...props.value })}
      onClose={props.onClose}
      footer={
        <>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" onClick={() => setDraft({ ...EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, q: draft().q })}>
            {t("filters.reset")}
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary flex-1" onClick={() => props.onApply(draft())}>
            {t("filters.apply_items")}
          </button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <CityField value={draft().city} onChange={(city) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, city }))} />
      <PriceFields
        min={draft().priceMin}
        max={draft().priceMax}
        onMin={(priceMin) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, priceMin }))}
        onMax={(priceMax) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, priceMax }))}
      />
      <For each={props.category.fields.filter((f) => f.filter)}>
        {(field) => {
          const param = field.filter!.param;
          return (
            <Switch>
              <Match when={field.filter!.kind === "min"}>
                <NumberInput
                  label={tr("filters.at_least", { field: tr(fieldLabel(slug(), field.key)) })}
                  value={draft().attrs[param] ? Number(draft().attrs[param]) : null}
                  onChange={(n) => setAttr(param, n === null ? "" : String(n))}
                />
              </Match>
              <Match when={true}>
                <ChoiceGroup
                  legend={tr(fieldLabel(slug(), field.key))}
                  name={param}
                  options={(field.options ?? []).map((o) => ({ value: o, label: tr(optionLabel(slug(), field.key, o)) }))}
                  value={draft().attrs[param] || null}
                  onChange={(v) => setAttr(param, v ?? "")}
                />
              </Match>
            </Switch>
          );
        }}
      </For>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/AttributeList.tsx`
```tsx
import { For, Show } from "solid-js";
import { tr } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { categoryDef, fieldLabel, optionLabel } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import type { Attributes } from "../lib/types.ts";

/** A listing's own details as label and value pairs, in the catalog's field order. */
export default function AttributeList(props: { category: string; attributes: Attributes }) {
  const rows = () =>
    (categoryDef(props.category)?.fields ?? [])
      // The price unit is already part of the price.
      .filter((f) => f.key !== "price_unit" && props.attributes[f.key] !== undefined)
      .map((f) => {
        const v = props.attributes[f.key];
        const value = f.kind === "choice"
          ? tr(optionLabel(props.category, f.key, String(v)))
          : f.kind === "choices"
          ? (v as string[]).map((o) => tr(optionLabel(props.category, f.key, o))).join(", ")
          : String(v);
        return { label: tr(fieldLabel(props.category, f.key)), value };
      });
  return (
    <Show when={rows().length > 0}>
      <dl class="grid grid-cols-[max-content_1fr] items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-3 text-sm">
        <For each={rows()}>
          {(row) => (
            <>
              <dt class="text-silver-ink">{row.label}</dt>
              <dd>{row.value}</dd>
            </>
          )}
        </For>
      </dl>
    </Show>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/Category.tsx`
```tsx
import { useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createMemo, createSignal, Match, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import EmptyState from "../components/EmptyState.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { FilterIcon } from "../components/icons.tsx";
import ItemFilterSheet from "../components/ItemFilterSheet.tsx";
import ListingGrid from "../components/ListingGrid.tsx";
import LoadMore from "../components/LoadMore.tsx";
import SearchBox from "../components/SearchBox.tsx";
import { GridSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { t, tr } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { type CategoryDef, categoryDef, catLabel } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import {
  countItemFilters, EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, type ItemFilters, itemFiltersToQuery, parseItemFilters,
} from "../lib/itemFilters.ts";
import { createRememberedList, type ListMemory } from "../lib/rememberedList.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";
import type { ListItem } from "../lib/types.ts";
import NotFound from "./NotFound.tsx";

const PER_PAGE = "24";

/** The last list shown, so Back from an item finds the same pages instead of a fresh first page. */
const memory: ListMemory<ListItem> = {};

function CategoryList(props: { category: CategoryDef }) {
  const c = props.category;
  usePageTitle(() => tr(catLabel(c.slug)));
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const filters = createMemo(() => parseItemFilters(c, new URLSearchParams(location.search)), EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS, {
    equals: (a, b) => itemFiltersToQuery(c, a).toString() === itemFiltersToQuery(c, b).toString(),
  });
  const [sheetOpen, setSheetOpen] = createSignal(false);
  const query = () => {
    const q = itemFiltersToQuery(c, filters());
    q.set("category", c.slug);
    return q;
  };
  const list = createRememberedList(memory, () => query().toString(), (page) => {
    const q = query();
    q.set("page", String(page));
    q.set("per_page", PER_PAGE);
    return api.items(q);
  });

  const apply = (f: ItemFilters) => {
    setSheetOpen(false);
    const qs = itemFiltersToQuery(c, f).toString();
    navigate(qs ? `/c/${c.slug}?${qs}` : `/c/${c.slug}`);
  };
  const active = () => countItemFilters(filters());
  const firstLoad = () => list.items().length === 0;

  return (
    <div class="pt-8">
      <h1 class="text-4xl">{tr(catLabel(c.slug))}</h1>
      <div class="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div class="flex-1">
          <SearchBox value={filters().q} onSearch={(q) => apply({ ...filters(), q })} />
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          class="btn btn-outline"
          classList={{ "border-garnet text-garnet-deep": active() > 0 }}
          aria-haspopup="dialog"
          onClick={() => setSheetOpen(true)}
        >
          <FilterIcon />
          {active() > 0 ? t("dresses.filters_active", { count: active() }) : t("dresses.filters")}
        </button>
      </div>
      <p class="mt-4 min-h-6 text-sm text-silver-ink" aria-live="polite">
        <Show when={!(list.loading() && firstLoad()) && list.error() === null}>{t("dresses.found", { count: list.total() })}</Show>
      </p>

      <div class="mt-4">
        <Switch>
          <Match when={firstLoad() && list.error() !== null}>
            <ErrorState error={list.error()} onRetry={() => void list.retry()} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={firstLoad() && list.loading()}>
            <GridSkeleton />
          </Match>
          <Match when={firstLoad()}>
            <EmptyState title={t("items.empty_title")} body={t("dresses.empty_body")}>
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" onClick={() => apply(EMPTY_ITEM_FILTERS)}>
                {t("dresses.clear")}
              </button>
            </EmptyState>
          </Match>
          <Match when={!firstLoad()}>
            <ListingGrid items={list.items()} />
            <LoadMore list={list} />
          </Match>
        </Switch>
      </div>

      <ItemFilterSheet
        category={c}
        open={sheetOpen()}
        value={filters()}
        onApply={apply}
        onClose={() => setSheetOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

/** /c/:slug for every category except dresses, which keep /dresses. A fresh list per category. */
export default function Category() {
  const params = useParams();
  const def = () => {
    const c = categoryDef(params.slug ?? "");
    return c && !c.own_pages ? c : undefined;
  };
  return (
    <Show when={def()} keyed fallback={<NotFound />}>
      {(c) => <CategoryList category={c} />}
    </Show>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/Item.tsx`
```tsx
import { Navigate, useParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createResource, Match, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import AttributeList from "../components/AttributeList.tsx";
import Availability from "../components/Availability.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { FavoriteButton } from "../components/FavoriteButton.tsx";
import Gallery from "../components/Gallery.tsx";
import { priceText } from "../components/price.ts";
import ShareButton from "../components/ShareButton.tsx";
import ShopCardSection from "../components/ShopCard.tsx";
import { DetailSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import { isNotFound } from "../lib/errors.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";
import type { PublicItem } from "../lib/types.ts";
import NotFound from "./NotFound.tsx";

function ItemView(props: { item: PublicItem }) {
  const it = () => props.item;
  return (
    <article class="pt-4 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,26rem)] lg:gap-12 lg:pt-8">
      <Gallery images={it().images} title={it().title} />
      <div class="mt-6 lg:mt-0">
        <h1 class="text-4xl sm:text-5xl">{it().title}</h1>
        <p class="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold text-garnet">{priceText(it())}</p>
        <div class="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <FavoriteButton id={it().id} />
          <ShareButton title={it().title} />
        </div>

        <div class="mt-8">
          <AttributeList category={it().category} attributes={it().attributes} />
        </div>

        <Show when={it().description}>
          {(text) => (
            <section class="mt-8">
              <h2 class="text-xl">{t("item.about")}</h2>
              <p class="mt-2 max-w-prose whitespace-pre-line">{text()}</p>
            </section>
          )}
        </Show>

        <div class="mt-10">
          <Availability listingId={it().id} />
        </div>

        <div class="mt-10">
          <ShopCardSection shop={it().shop} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

/** /items/:id: any category's page; a dress opened here goes to its own /dresses/:id page. */
export default function Item() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [item, { refetch }] = createResource(() => params.id, (id) => api.item(id));
  usePageTitle(() => (item.state === "ready" ? item()?.title : undefined));

  return (
    <Switch>
      <Match when={item.error}>
        <Show when={isNotFound(item.error)} fallback={<ErrorState error={item.error} onRetry={refetch} />}>
          <NotFound />
        </Show>
      </Match>
      <Match when={!item()}>
        <DetailSkeleton />
      </Match>
      <Match when={item()?.category === "dresses"}>
        <Navigate href={`/dresses/${item()!.id}`} />
      </Match>
      <Match when={item()} keyed>{(it) => <ItemView item={it} />}</Match>
    </Switch>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/Search.tsx`
```tsx
import { A, useLocation, useNavigate } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createMemo, For, Match, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import EmptyState from "../components/EmptyState.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import ListingGrid from "../components/ListingGrid.tsx";
import LoadMore from "../components/LoadMore.tsx";
import SearchBox from "../components/SearchBox.tsx";
import { GridSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { t, tr } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { CATEGORIES, catLabel, categoryDef } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import { createRememberedList, type ListMemory } from "../lib/rememberedList.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";
import type { ListItem } from "../lib/types.ts";

const PER_PAGE = "24";
const memory: ListMemory<ListItem> = {};

/** /search?q=&category=: every category at once, narrowed by the category chips. */
export default function Search() {
  usePageTitle(() => t("search.title"));
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = createMemo(() => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const category = query.get("category") ?? "";
    return { q: (query.get("q") ?? "").trim(), category: categoryDef(category) ? category : "" };
  }, { q: "", category: "" }, { equals: (a, b) => a.q === b.q && a.category === b.category });
  const href = (q: string, category: string) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams();
    if (q) query.set("q", q);
    if (category) query.set("category", category);
    const qs = query.toString();
    return qs ? `/search?${qs}` : "/search";
  };
  const list = createRememberedList(memory, () => href(params().q, params().category), (page) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ page: String(page), per_page: PER_PAGE });
    if (params().q) query.set("q", params().q);
    if (params().category) query.set("category", params().category);
    return api.items(query);
  });
  const firstLoad = () => list.items().length === 0;
  const chip = (category: string, label: string) => (
    <A
      href={href(params().q, category)}
      class="chip"
      classList={{ "border-garnet bg-blush text-garnet-deep": params().category === category }}
      aria-current={params().category === category ? "page" : undefined}
    >
      {label}
    </A>
  );

  return (
    <div class="pt-8">
      <h1 class="text-4xl">{t("search.title")}</h1>
      <div class="mt-6">
        <SearchBox value={params().q} onSearch={(q) => navigate(href(q, params().category))} />
      </div>
      <nav class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2" aria-label={t("home.categories")}>
        {chip("", t("search.all"))}
        <For each={CATEGORIES}>{(c) => chip(c.slug, tr(catLabel(c.slug)))}</For>
      </nav>
      <p class="mt-4 min-h-6 text-sm text-silver-ink" aria-live="polite">
        <Show when={!(list.loading() && firstLoad()) && list.error() === null}>{t("dresses.found", { count: list.total() })}</Show>
      </p>
      <div class="mt-4">
        <Switch>
          <Match when={firstLoad() && list.error() !== null}>
            <ErrorState error={list.error()} onRetry={() => void list.retry()} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={firstLoad() && list.loading()}>
            <GridSkeleton />
          </Match>
          <Match when={firstLoad()}>
            <EmptyState title={t("items.empty_title")} body={t("search.empty_body")} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={!firstLoad()}>
            <ListingGrid items={list.items()} />
            <LoadMore list={list} />
          </Match>
        </Switch>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
export const COLORS = ["white", "ivory", "champagne", "blush", "other"] as const;
export const SILHOUETTES = ["ball_gown", "a_line", "mermaid", "straight", "short"] as const;
export const SLEEVES = ["long", "short", "none"] as const;
export const SIZES = ["XS", "S", "M", "L", "XL", "XXL"] as const;

export type Color = (typeof COLORS)[number];
export type Silhouette = (typeof SILHOUETTES)[number];
export type Sleeves = (typeof SLEEVES)[number];
export type Size = (typeof SIZES)[number];
export type Lang = "tk" | "ru" | "en";

export interface CityRef {
  slug: string;
  name_tk: string;
  name_ru: string;
  name_en: string;
}

export interface City extends CityRef {
  id: number;
}

/** URLs are site-relative, e.g. "/uploads/ab12_s.jpg". */
export interface Image {
  id: number;
  position: number;
  small: string;
  large: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export interface ShopRef {
  name: string;
  slug: string;
}

/** A listing's own fields (lib/catalog.ts defines them per category); {} for dresses. */
export type Attributes = Record<string, string | number | string[]>;

export interface ListItem {
  id: number;
  /** A category slug; only the all-category list (/api/items) sends it. Dress lists leave it out. */
  category?: string;
  attributes?: Attributes;
  title: string;
  price_manat: number;
  price_is_from: boolean;
  photo: string | null;
  shop: ShopRef;
  city: CityRef;
}

export interface Page<T = ListItem> {
  items: T[];
  page: number;
  per_page: number;
  total: number;
}

export interface ShopCard {
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  address: string;
  phone: string;
  instagram_url: string | null;
  tiktok_url: string | null;
  logo: string | null;
  city: CityRef;
}

export interface PublicDress {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  price_manat: number;
  price_is_from: boolean;
  description: string | null;
  color: Color;
  silhouette: Silhouette;
  sleeves: Sleeves;
  sizes: Size[];
  images: Image[];
  shop: ShopCard;
}

export interface PublicShop {
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  address: string;
  phone: string;
  instagram_url: string | null;
  tiktok_url: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  logo: string | null;
  city: CityRef;
  items: Page;
  /** The categories the shop has published listings in, in display order. */
  categories: string[];
}

/** Empty strings clear the optional text fields on the server. */
export interface ShopInput {
  name: string;
  city_id: number;
  address: string;
  phone: string;
  instagram_url: string;
  tiktok_url: string;
  description: string;
  is_published: boolean;
  aliases: string[];
}

export interface AdminShop {
  id: number;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  city_id: number;
  address: string;
  phone: string;
  instagram_url: string | null;
  tiktok_url: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  is_published: boolean;
  logo: Image | null;
  aliases: string[];
  /** The shop-owner account that runs this shop, if any. */
  owner: { id: number; phone: string } | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface SignupInput {
  shop_name: string;
  city_id: number;
  address: string;
  phone: string;
  password: string;
}

/** The signed-in shop owner and their shop. */
export interface ShopMe {
  id: number;
  phone: string;
  shop: AdminShop;
}

export interface DressInput {
  shop_id: number;
  title: string;
  price_manat: number;
  price_is_from: boolean;
  description: string;
  is_published: boolean;
  color: Color;
  silhouette: Silhouette;
  sleeves: Sleeves;
  sizes: Size[];
}

export interface AdminDress {
  id: number;
  shop_id: number;
  title: string;
  price_manat: number;
  price_is_from: boolean;
  description: string | null;
  is_published: boolean;

  images: Image[];

}

/** One dress's calendar: the window (today in Turkmenistan to 12 months ahead) and its busy days. */

  to: string;

}

/** One published listing of any category, for its public page. */
export interface PublicItem {

}
```
