```tsx
import { A, useNavigate } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createResource, For, Match, Switch } from "solid-js";
import CategoryBar from "../components/CategoryBar.tsx";
import ListingGrid from "../components/ListingGrid.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import SearchBox from "../components/SearchBox.tsx";
import { GridSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { i18n, t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { useCities } from "../lib/cities.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import { EMPTY_FILTERS, filtersToQuery } from "../lib/filters.ts";
import { cityName } from "../lib/format.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";

export default function Home() {
  usePageTitle(() => undefined);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const cities = useCities();
  // The newest listings of every category.
  const [newest, { refetch }] = createResource(() => api.items(new URLSearchParams({ per_page: "8" })));
  const search = (q: string) => navigate(q ? `/search?${new URLSearchParams({ q })}` : "/search");

  const browse = (q: string, city = "") => {
    const qs = filtersToQuery({ ...EMPTY_FILTERS, q, city }).toString();
    navigate(qs ? `/dresses?${qs}` : "/dresses");
  };

  return (
    <>
      <section class="pb-12 pt-10 sm:pt-16">
        <h1 class="max-w-2xl text-5xl sm:text-6xl">{t("home.title")}</h1>
        <p class="mt-4 max-w-xl text-lg text-silver-ink">{t("home.lead")}</p>
        <div class="mt-8 max-w-2xl space-y-4">
          <SearchBox value="" large onSearch={search} />
          <label class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm">
            <span class="text-silver-ink">{t("home.city")}</span>
            <select
              class="field-input w-auto"
              value=""
              onChange={(e) => e.currentTarget.value && browse("", e.currentTarget.value)}
            >
              <option value="">{t("home.all_cities")}</option>
              <For each={cities()}>{(c) => <option value={c.slug}>{cityName(c, i18n.lang())}</option>}</For>
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="pb-12">
        <CategoryBar />
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="newest-title">
        <div class="mb-6 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4">
          <h2 id="newest-title" class="text-3xl">{t("home.newest")}</h2>
          <A href="/search" class="text-sm font-semibold text-garnet underline-offset-4 hover:underline">
            {t("home.see_all_items")}
          </A>
        </div>
        <Switch>
          <Match when={newest.error}>
            <ErrorState error={newest.error} onRetry={refetch} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={!newest()}>
            <GridSkeleton count={8} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={newest()}>{(page) => <ListingGrid items={page().items} />}</Match>
        </Switch>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/Shop.tsx`
```tsx
import { A, useLocation, useParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createEffect, createSignal, For, Match, on, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import ListingGrid from "../components/ListingGrid.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import LoadMore from "../components/LoadMore.tsx";
import { ContactButtons } from "../components/ShopCard.tsx";
import ShopLogo from "../components/ShopLogo.tsx";
import { DetailSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { i18n, t, tr } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { categoryDef, catLabel } from "../lib/catalog.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import { isNotFound } from "../lib/errors.ts";
import { cityName } from "../lib/format.ts";
import { createPagedList } from "../lib/paged.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";
import type { PublicShop } from "../lib/types.ts";
import NotFound from "./NotFound.tsx";

export default function Shop() {
  const params = useParams();
  const location = useLocation();
  /** The ?category= chip; "" shows every category. */
  const category = () => {
    const c = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("category") ?? "";
    return categoryDef(c) ? c : "";
  };
  const [info, setInfo] = createSignal<PublicShop | null>(null);
  const list = createPagedList((page) => {
    const slug = params.slug ?? "";
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ page: String(page), per_page: "24" });
    if (category()) query.set("category", category());
    return api.shop(slug, query).then((s) => {
      // Ignore the details of a shop the user has already left.
      if (page === 1 && slug === params.slug) setInfo(s);
      return s.items;
    });
  });
  createEffect(on(() => params.slug, () => {
    setInfo(null);
    void list.reset();
  }));
  // Another chip keeps the shop's details on screen and reloads only the list.
  createEffect(on(category, () => void list.reset(), { defer: true }));
  const chipHref = (c: string) => `/shops/${params.slug}${c ? `?category=${c}` : ""}`;
  const chip = (c: string, label: string) => (
    <A
      href={chipHref(c)}
      class="chip"
      classList={{ "border-garnet bg-blush text-garnet-deep": category() === c }}
      aria-current={category() === c ? "page" : undefined}
    >
      {label}
    </A>
  );
  usePageTitle(() => info()?.name);

  return (
    <Switch>
      <Match when={!info() && list.error() !== null}>
        <Show
          when={isNotFound(list.error())}
          fallback={<ErrorState error={list.error()} onRetry={() => void list.retry()} />}
        >
          <NotFound />
        </Show>
      </Match>
      <Match when={!info()}>
        <DetailSkeleton />
      </Match>
      <Match when={info()}>
        {(shop) => (
          <div class="pt-8">
            <header class="flex flex-col gap-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">
              <ShopLogo url={shop().logo} name={shop().name} size="lg" />
              <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h1 class="text-4xl sm:text-5xl">{shop().name}</h1>
                <p class="mt-1 text-silver-ink">
                  {cityName(shop().city, i18n.lang())}, {shop().address}
                </p>
                <Show when={shop().description}>
                  {(text) => <p class="mt-4 max-w-prose whitespace-pre-line">{text()}</p>}
                </Show>
                <div class="mt-6 max-w-xl">
                  <ContactButtons shop={shop()} />
                </div>
              </div>
            </header>

            <section class="mt-12" aria-labelledby="shop-dresses-title">
              <h2 id="shop-dresses-title" class="mb-6 text-3xl">{t("shop.listings")}</h2>
              <Show when={shop().categories.length > 1}>
                <nav class="mb-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2" aria-label={t("home.categories")}>
                  {chip("", t("search.all"))}
                  <For each={shop().categories}>{(c) => chip(c, tr(catLabel(c)))}</For>
                </nav>
              </Show>
              <Show when={list.items().length > 0} fallback={<p class="text-silver-ink">{t("shop.empty")}</p>}>
                <ListingGrid items={list.items()} />
                <LoadMore list={list} />
              </Show>
            </section>
          </div>
        )}
      </Match>
    </Switch>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/Favorites.tsx`
```tsx
import { A } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createEffect, createResource, Match, Switch, untrack } from "solid-js";
import ListingGrid from "../components/ListingGrid.tsx";
import EmptyState from "../components/EmptyState.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { GridSkeleton } from "../components/Skeleton.tsx";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { api } from "../lib/client.ts";
import { favorites, MAX_FAVORITES } from "../lib/favorites.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../lib/title.ts";
import type { ListItem, Page } from "../lib/types.ts";

export default function Favorites() {
  usePageTitle(() => t("favorites.title"));
  // Fetch once with the ids saved on this device; unhearting here only hides the tile.
  const [page, { refetch }] = createResource((): Promise<Page | null> => {
    const ids = untrack(favorites.ids);
    if (ids.length === 0) return Promise.resolve(null);
    return api.items(new URLSearchParams({ ids: ids.join(","), per_page: String(MAX_FAVORITES) }));
  });

  // Ids that the API no longer returns (deleted or unpublished) are dropped silently.
  createEffect(() => {
    const p = page.state === "ready" ? page() : null;
    if (p) untrack(() => favorites.keepOnly(p.items.map((i) => i.id)));
  });

  // Newest favorite first, the same order as on the device.
  const visible = (): ListItem[] => {
    const p = page.state === "ready" ? page() : null;
    if (!p) return [];
    const byId = new Map(p.items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
    return favorites.ids().flatMap((id) => byId.get(id) ?? []);
  };

  return (
    <div class="pt-8">
      <h1 class="text-4xl">{t("favorites.title")}</h1>
      <div class="mt-6">
        <Switch>
          <Match when={page.error}>
            <ErrorState error={page.error} onRetry={refetch} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={page.loading}>
            <GridSkeleton count={Math.min(favorites.ids().length, 8)} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={visible().length === 0}>
            <EmptyState title={t("favorites.empty_title")} body={t("favorites.empty_body")}>
              <A href="/" class="btn btn-primary">{t("favorites.browse")}</A>
            </EmptyState>
          </Match>
          <Match when={visible().length > 0}>
            <ListingGrid items={visible()} />
          </Match>
        </Switch>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

import { A } from "@solidjs/router";
import { Show } from "solid-js";
import { t } from "../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { favorites } from "../lib/favorites.ts";
import GolBand from "./GolBand.tsx";
import { HeartIcon } from "./icons.tsx";
import LanguageSwitch from "./LanguageSwitch.tsx";

export default function Header() {
  const count = () => favorites.ids().length;
  return (
    <header class="bg-white">
      <GolBand />
      <div class="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 px-4 py-1 sm:flex-nowrap sm:py-2">
        <A href="/" class="mr-auto font-display text-3xl leading-none text-garnet">
          {t("app.name")}
        </A>
        <nav class="flex items-center text-sm">
          <A
            href="/dresses"
            class="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full px-3 hover:bg-veil"
            activeClass="text-garnet font-semibold"
          >
            {t("nav.dresses")}
          </A>
          <A
            href="/favorites"
            class="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 hover:bg-veil"
            activeClass="text-garnet font-semibold"
            aria-label={t("nav.favorites_count", { count: count() })}
          >
            <HeartIcon filled={count() > 0} class="h-5 w-5 text-garnet" />
            <span class="hidden sm:inline">{t("nav.favorites")}</span>
            <Show when={count() > 0}>
              <span class="tabular-nums text-garnet">{count()}</span>
            </Show>
          </A>
        </nav>
        <div class="ml-auto sm:ml-0">
          <LanguageSwitch />
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

`frontend/src/components/admin/ListingList.tsx`
```tsx
import { A } from "@solidjs/router";
import { For, Show } from "solid-js";
import { tr } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { catLabel } from "../../lib/catalog.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import type { ListingSummary } from "../../lib/types.ts";
import { priceText } from "../price.ts";
import StatusBadge from "./StatusBadge.tsx";

/** The admin and owner listing lists: one row per listing of any category, linking to its editor. */
export default function ListingList(props: { items: ListingSummary[]; shopName?: (shopId: number) => string }) {
  const m = useManage();
  const href = (l: ListingSummary) => (l.category === "dresses" ? m.dressHref(l.id) : m.itemHref(l.id));
  return (
    <ul class="divide-y divide-veil overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white">
      <For each={props.items}>
        {(l) => (
          <li>
            <A href={href(l)} class="flex items-center gap-4 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-veil/60">
              <div class="h-16 w-12 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xs bg-veil">
                <Show when={l.photo}>{(src) => <img src={src()} alt="" class="h-full w-full object-cover" />}</Show>
              </div>
              <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p class="truncate font-semibold">{l.title}</p>
                <p class="truncate text-sm text-silver-ink">
                  {tr(catLabel(l.category))}
                  <Show when={props.shopName?.(l.shop_id)}>{(name) => <> · {name()}</>}</Show>
                </p>
                <p class="text-sm text-garnet">{priceText(l)}</p>
              </div>
              <StatusBadge published={l.is_published} />
            </A>
          </li>
        )}
      </For>
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/admin/AttributeFields.tsx`
```tsx
import { For, Match, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { t, tr } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { type CategoryDef, fieldLabel, optionLabel } from "../../lib/catalog.ts";
import { fieldMessage } from "../../lib/errors.ts";
import { SelectField, TextField } from "./Field.tsx";

/** A category's own fields in the item editor, built from its definitions. */
export default function AttributeFields(props: {
  category: CategoryDef;
  values: Record<string, string | string[]>;
  onChange: (key: string, value: string | string[]) => void;
  /** Raw API field values, keyed "attributes.<key>". */
  errors: Record<string, string>;
}) {
  const slug = () => props.category.slug;
  return (
    <For each={props.category.fields}>
      {(f) => {
        const label = () => tr(fieldLabel(slug(), f.key));
        const hint = () => (f.required ? undefined : t("itemform.optional"));
        const err = () => props.errors[`attributes.${f.key}`];
        const text = () => {
          const v = props.values[f.key];
          return typeof v === "string" ? v : "";
        };
        const picked = () => {
          const v = props.values[f.key];
          return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
        };
        return (
          <Switch>
            <Match when={f.kind === "choice"}>
              <SelectField
                label={label()}
                hint={hint()}
                value={text()}
                options={[
                  { value: "", label: t("admin.choose") },
                  ...(f.options ?? []).map((o) => ({ value: o, label: tr(optionLabel(slug(), f.key, o)) })),
                ]}
                onChange={(v) => props.onChange(f.key, v)}
                error={err()}
              />
            </Match>
            <Match when={f.kind === "choices"}>
              <fieldset>
                <legend class="text-sm font-semibold">{label()}</legend>
                <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <For each={f.options ?? []}>
                    {(o) => (
                      <label class="chip">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          class="sr-only"
                          checked={picked().includes(o)}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            props.onChange(
                              f.key,
                              e.currentTarget.checked ? [...picked(), o] : picked().filter((x) => x !== o),
                            )}
                        />
                        {tr(optionLabel(slug(), f.key, o))}
                      </label>
                    )}
                  </For>
                </div>
                <Show when={err()}>{(e) => <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-garnet">{fieldMessage(e(), tr)}</p>}</Show>
              </fieldset>
            </Match>
            <Match when={true}>
              <TextField
                label={label()}
                hint={hint()}
                inputmode={f.kind === "number" ? "numeric" : undefined}
                value={text()}
                onInput={(v) => props.onChange(f.key, v)}
                error={err()}
              />
            </Match>
          </Switch>
        );
      }}
    </For>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/admin/NewListing.tsx`
```tsx
import { A, useSearchParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { For } from "solid-js";
import { t, tr } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { CATEGORIES, catLabel } from "../../lib/catalog.ts";
import { firstParam } from "../../lib/forms.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../../lib/title.ts";

/** "New listing": the category comes first; a dress opens the dress editor, the rest the item editor. */
export default function NewListing() {
  usePageTitle(() => t("admin.new_listing"));
  const m = useManage();
  const [search] = useSearchParams();
  const shopId = () => Number(firstParam(search.shop_id)) || undefined;
  return (
    <div class="max-w-2xl">
      <A href={m.listingsHref(shopId())} class="text-sm text-silver-ink hover:text-ink">{t("common.back")}</A>
      <h1 class="mt-2 text-3xl">{t("admin.choose_category")}</h1>
      <ul class="mt-6 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <For each={CATEGORIES}>
          {(c) => (
            <li>
              <A
                href={c.own_pages ? m.newDressHref(shopId()) : m.newItemHref(c.slug, shopId())}
                class="flex min-h-14 items-center rounded-lg bg-white px-5 font-semibold hover:text-garnet"
              >
                {tr(catLabel(c.slug))}
              </A>
            </li>
          )}
        </For>
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/admin/ItemEdit.tsx`
```tsx
import { A, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createEffect, createResource, createSignal, Match, on, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { createStore, reconcile } from "solid-js/store";
import AttributeFields from "../../components/admin/AttributeFields.tsx";
import AvailabilityEditor from "../../components/admin/AvailabilityEditor.tsx";
import { CheckboxField, FormActions, SelectField, TextArea, TextField } from "../../components/admin/Field.tsx";
import PhotoManager from "../../components/admin/PhotoManager.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { t, tr } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { categoryDef, catLabel } from "../../lib/catalog.ts";
import { validationFields } from "../../lib/errors.ts";
import { emptyItemForm, firstParam, type ItemForm, itemFormToInput, itemToForm } from "../../lib/forms.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../../lib/title.ts";
import NotFound from "../NotFound.tsx";

/**
 * The editor for one non-dress item, or a new one in a category; remounted per item by
 * ItemEdit below. Admins and shop owners share it: useManage() says where it saves and links to.
 */
function ItemEditor(props: { id: number | null; category: string }) {
  const m = useManage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [search] = useSearchParams();
  const id = () => props.id;
  const [item, { mutate, refetch }] = createResource(id, (itemId) => m.calls.item(itemId));
  const [shops] = createResource(() => m.shops?.() ?? Promise.resolve([]));
  const [form, setForm] = createStore<ItemForm>(emptyItemForm(props.category, Number(firstParam(search.shop_id)) || 0));
  // Refill the form only when the saved item changes, not when just its photos do.
  createEffect(on(() => (item.state === "ready" ? item()?.updated_at : undefined), (stamp) => {
    const it = item.state === "ready" ? item() : undefined;
    if (stamp && it) setForm(reconcile(itemToForm(it)));
  }));
  const def = () => categoryDef(form.category);
  const categoryName = () => tr(catLabel(form.category));
  const [errors, setErrors] = createSignal<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [error, setError] = createSignal<unknown>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = createSignal(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = createSignal(false);
  const err = (field: string) => errors()[field];
  const heading = () => (id() ? form.title || t("admin.edit") : t("admin.new_item", { category: categoryName() }));
  usePageTitle(heading);

  async function save(e: SubmitEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const d = def();
    if (!d) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setSaved(false);
    setErrors({});
    setError(null);
    try {
      const input = itemFormToInput({ ...form, attributes: { ...form.attributes } }, d.fields);
      const current = id();
      const result = current ? await m.calls.updateItem(current, input) : await m.calls.createItem(input);
      setSaved(true);
      if (current) mutate((prev) => ({ ...result, images: prev?.images ?? result.images }));
      else navigate(m.itemHref(result.id), { replace: true });
    } catch (e) {
      setErrors(validationFields(e));
      setError(() => e);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function remove() {
    const current = id();
    if (!current || !confirm(t("admin.delete_item_confirm"))) return;
    try {
      await m.calls.deleteItem(current);
      navigate(m.listingsHref(form.shop_id), { replace: true });
    } catch (e) {
      setError(() => e);
    }
  }

  const shopOptions = () => [
    { value: "", label: t("admin.choose") },
    ...(shops.state === "ready" ? shops() ?? [] : []).map((s) => ({ value: String(s.id), label: s.name })),
  ];

  return (
    <div class="max-w-2xl">
      <A href={m.listingsHref()} class="text-sm text-silver-ink hover:text-ink">{t("common.back")}</A>
      <h1 class="mt-2 text-3xl">{heading()}</h1>
      <Switch>
        <Match when={item.error}>
          <ErrorState error={item.error} onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
        </Match>
        <Match when={id() !== null && !item()}>
          <p class="mt-6 text-silver-ink">{t("common.loading")}</p>
        </Match>
        <Match when={def()}>
          {(d) => (
            <>
              <form class="mt-6 space-y-5 rounded-lg bg-white p-5" novalidate onSubmit={save}>
                <p class="text-sm">
                  <span class="text-silver-ink">{t("admin.category")}:</span>{" "}
                  <span class="font-semibold">{categoryName()}</span>
                </p>
                <Show when={m.shops}>
                  <SelectField
                    label={t("admin.shop")}
                    value={form.shop_id ? String(form.shop_id) : ""}
                    options={shopOptions()}
                    onChange={(v) => setForm("shop_id", Number(v) || 0)}
                    error={err("shop_id")}
                  />
                </Show>
                <TextField label={t("itemform.title")} value={form.title} onInput={(v) => setForm("title", v)} error={err("title")} />
                <div class="grid items-end gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                  <TextField
                    label={t("dressform.price")}
                    inputmode="numeric"
                    value={form.price}
                    onInput={(v) => setForm("price", v)}
                    error={err("price_manat")}
                  />
                  <CheckboxField
                    label={t("dressform.price_is_from")}
                    checked={form.price_is_from}
                    onChange={(v) => setForm("price_is_from", v)}
                  />
                </div>
                <AttributeFields
                  category={d()}
                  values={form.attributes}
                  onChange={(key, value) => setForm("attributes", key, value)}
                  errors={errors()}
                />
                <TextArea
                  label={t("dressform.description")}
                  value={form.description}
                  onInput={(v) => setForm("description", v)}
                  error={err("description")}
                />
                <CheckboxField
                  label={t("admin.published")}
                  hint={t("admin.published_hint")}
                  checked={form.is_published}
                  onChange={(v) => setForm("is_published", v)}
                />
                <FormActions saving={saving()} saved={saved()} error={error()} onDelete={id() ? remove : undefined} />
              </form>

              <section class="mt-8 rounded-lg bg-white p-5">
                <h2 class="text-2xl">{t("photos.title")}</h2>
                <div class="mt-4">
                  <Show
                    when={id() !== null && item()}
                    fallback={<p class="text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("photos.save_first_item")}</p>}
                  >
                    {(it) => (
                      <PhotoManager
                        listingId={it().id}
                        title={it().title}
                        images={it().images}
                        onChange={(images) => mutate({ ...it(), images })}
                      />
                    )}
                  </Show>
                </div>
              </section>

              <section class="mt-8 rounded-lg bg-white p-5">
                <h2 class="text-2xl">{t("avail.title")}</h2>
                <div class="mt-4">
                  <Show
                    when={id() !== null && item()}
                    fallback={<p class="text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("avail.save_first_item")}</p>}
                  >
                    {(it) => <AvailabilityEditor listingId={it().id} />}
                  </Show>
                </div>
              </section>
            </>
          )}
        </Match>
      </Switch>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * /admin/items/new?category=…, /admin/items/:id, /shop/items/new?category=… and
 * /shop/items/:id; a fresh editor per item. A new item needs a known non-dress category.
 */
export default function ItemEdit() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [search] = useSearchParams();
  const key = () => {
    if (params.id) return params.id;
    const c = categoryDef(firstParam(search.category));
    return c && !c.own_pages ? `new:${c.slug}` : "";
  };
  return (
    <Show when={key()} keyed fallback={<NotFound />}>
      {(k) => (k.startsWith("new:") ? <ItemEditor id={null} category={k.slice(4)} /> : <ItemEditor id={Number(k)} category="" />)}
    </Show>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { A, useSearchParams } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createResource, For, Match, Switch } from "solid-js";
import ListingList from "../../components/admin/ListingList.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { PlusIcon } from "../../components/icons.tsx";
import { t, tr } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { CATEGORIES, catLabel, categoryDef } from "../../lib/catalog.ts";
import { api } from "../../lib/client.ts";
import { firstParam } from "../../lib/forms.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../../lib/title.ts";

/** The admin's listings of every shop and category, filtered by ?shop_id= and ?category=. */
export default function AdminListings() {
  usePageTitle(() => t("admin.listings"));
  const m = useManage();
  const [search, setSearch] = useSearchParams();
  const shopId = () => Number(firstParam(search.shop_id)) || 0;
  const category = () => (categoryDef(firstParam(search.category)) ? firstParam(search.category) : "");
  const [shops] = createResource(() => api.admin.shops());
  // The source is an object so "all shops" (0) still fetches.
  const [listings, { refetch }] = createResource(
    () => ({ shop: shopId(), category: category() }),
    (f) => m.calls.listings({ shopId: f.shop || undefined, category: f.category || undefined }),
  );
  const shopList = () => (shops.state === "ready" ? shops() ?? [] : []);
  const shopName = (id: number) => shopList().find((s) => s.id === id)?.name ?? "";

  return (
    <div>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <h1 class="text-3xl">{t("admin.listings")}</h1>
        <select
          class="field-input w-auto"
          aria-label={t("admin.shop")}
          value={shopId() ? String(shopId()) : ""}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch({ shop_id: e.currentTarget.value || undefined })}
        >
          <option value="" selected={!shopId()}>{t("admin.all_shops")}</option>
          <For each={shopList()}>{(s) => <option value={String(s.id)} selected={s.id === shopId()}>{s.name}</option>}</For>
        </select>
        <select
          class="field-input w-auto"
          aria-label={t("admin.category")}
          value={category()}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch({ category: e.currentTarget.value || undefined })}
        >
          <option value="" selected={!category()}>{t("admin.all_categories")}</option>
          <For each={CATEGORIES}>
            {(c) => <option value={c.slug} selected={c.slug === category()}>{tr(catLabel(c.slug))}</option>}
          </For>
        </select>
        <A href={m.newListingHref(shopId() || undefined)} class="btn btn-primary ml-auto">
          <PlusIcon />
          {t("admin.new_listing")}
        </A>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-6">
        <Switch>
          <Match when={listings.error}>
            <ErrorState error={listings.error} onRetry={refetch} />
          </Match>
          <Match when={!listings()}>
            <p class="text-silver-ink">{t("common.loading")}</p>
          </Match>
          <Match when={listings()?.length === 0}>
            <p class="text-silver-ink">{t("admin.empty_listings")}</p>
          </Match>
          <Match when={listings()}>{(list) => <ListingList items={list()} shopName={shopName} />}</Match>
        </Switch>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

import { A, Navigate, type RouteSectionProps, useLocation, useNavigate } from "@solidjs/router";
import { Show } from "solid-js";
import GolBand from "../../components/GolBand.tsx";
import LanguageSwitch from "../../components/LanguageSwitch.tsx";
import { t } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { auth } from "../../lib/auth.ts";
import { api, uploadImage } from "../../lib/client.ts";
import { mainRef } from "../../lib/dom.ts";
import { adminManage, ManageContext } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../../lib/title.ts";

const manage = adminManage(api, uploadImage);

const NAV_LINK = "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full px-3 hover:bg-veil";

/** Every /admin page: requires a token, otherwise goes to sign-in and comes back afterwards. */
export default function AdminLayout(props: RouteSectionProps) {
  usePageTitle(() => t("admin.title"));
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const signOut = () => {
    auth.clear();
    navigate("/admin/login", { replace: true });
  };
  return (
    <Show
      when={auth.token()}
      fallback={<Navigate href={`/admin/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`} />}
    >
      <div class="min-h-screen bg-veil">
        <header class="bg-white">
          <GolBand />
          <div class="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 px-4 py-2">
            <A href="/" class="font-display text-2xl text-garnet">{t("app.name")}</A>
            <span class="text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("admin.title")}</span>
            <nav class="flex text-sm" aria-label={t("admin.title")}>
              <A href="/admin/shops" class={NAV_LINK} activeClass="font-semibold text-garnet">{t("admin.shops")}</A>
              <A href="/admin/listings" class={NAV_LINK} activeClass="font-semibold text-garnet">
                {t("admin.listings")}
              </A>
            </nav>
            <div class="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1">
              <LanguageSwitch />
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-quiet" onClick={signOut}>{t("admin.sign_out")}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>
        <main ref={mainRef} id="main" tabindex="-1" class="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 outline-none">
          <ManageContext.Provider value={manage}>{props.children}</ManageContext.Provider>
        </main>
      </div>
    </Show>
  );
}

`frontend/src/pages/shop/MyShop.tsx`
```tsx
import { A } from "@solidjs/router";
import { createEffect, createResource, createSignal, For, Match, on, Show, Switch } from "solid-js";
import { createStore, reconcile } from "solid-js/store";
import { FormActions } from "../../components/admin/Field.tsx";
import LogoUpload from "../../components/admin/LogoUpload.tsx";
import ShopFields from "../../components/admin/ShopFields.tsx";
import ListingList from "../../components/admin/ListingList.tsx";
import ErrorState from "../../components/ErrorState.tsx";
import { PlusIcon } from "../../components/icons.tsx";
import { t } from "../../i18n/i18n.ts";
import { ownerApi } from "../../lib/client.ts";
import { contactPhone } from "../../lib/contact.ts";
import { validationFields } from "../../lib/errors.ts";
import { cleanShopInput, EMPTY_SHOP_INPUT, shopToInput } from "../../lib/forms.ts";
import { useManage } from "../../lib/manage.ts";
import { usePageTitle } from "../../lib/title.ts";
import type { ShopInput } from "../../lib/types.ts";

/** "My shop": the owner's listings and shop details. */
export default function MyShop() {
  usePageTitle(() => t("myshop.title"));
  const [me, { mutate, refetch }] = createResource(() => ownerApi.owner.me());
  const m = useManage();
  const [listings, listingList] = createResource(() => ownerApi.owner.listings());
  const shop = () => (me.state === "ready" ? me()?.shop : undefined);
  const [form, setForm] = createStore<ShopInput>({ ...EMPTY_SHOP_INPUT, aliases: [] });
  // Refill the form only when the saved shop changes, not when just its logo does.
  createEffect(on(() => shop()?.updated_at, (stamp) => {
    const s = shop();
    if (stamp && s) setForm(reconcile(shopToInput(s)));
  }));
  const [errors, setErrors] = createSignal<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [error, setError] = createSignal<unknown>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = createSignal(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = createSignal(false);

  async function save(e: SubmitEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setSaved(false);
    setErrors({});
    setError(null);
    try {
      const result = await ownerApi.owner.updateShop(
        cleanShopInput({ ...form, aliases: [...form.aliases] }),
      );
      setSaved(true);
      mutate((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, shop: result } : prev));
    } catch (e) {
      setErrors(validationFields(e));
      setError(() => e);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Switch>
      <Match when={me.error}>
        <ErrorState error={me.error} onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
      </Match>
      <Match when={!shop()}>
        <p class="text-silver-ink">{t("common.loading")}</p>
      </Match>
      <Match when={shop()}>
        {(s) => (
          <div class="max-w-2xl">
            <h1 class="text-3xl">{s().name}</h1>
            <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-silver-ink">{t("owner.phone")}: {me()?.phone}</p>
            <Show
              when={s().is_published}
              fallback={
                <p class="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-blush p-4 text-garnet-deep">
                  {t("myshop.hidden")}
                  <Show when={contactPhone()}>
                    {(p) => (
                      <>
                        {" "}
                        <a href={`tel:${p().replace(/\s/g, "")}`} class="font-semibold text-garnet">
                          {p()}
                        </a>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </Show>
                </p>
              }
            >
              <A
                href={`/shops/${s().slug}`}
                class="mt-2 inline-block text-sm font-semibold text-garnet"
              >
                {t("myshop.view_page")}
              </A>
            </Show>

            <section class="mt-8">
              <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <h2 class="text-2xl">{t("myshop.dresses")}</h2>
                <A href={m.newListingHref()} class="btn btn-primary ml-auto">
                  <PlusIcon />
                  {t("admin.new_listing")}
                </A>
              </div>
              <div class="mt-4">
                <Switch>
                  <Match when={listings.error}>
                    <ErrorState error={listings.error} onRetry={() => void listingList.refetch()} />
                  </Match>
                  <Match when={!listings()}>
                    <p class="text-silver-ink">{t("common.loading")}</p>
                  </Match>
                  <Match when={listings()?.length === 0}>
                    <p class="rounded-lg bg-white p-5 text-silver-ink">{t("myshop.no_dresses")}</p>
                  </Match>
                  <Match when={listings()}>{(list) => <ListingList items={list()} />}</Match>
                </Switch>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section class="mt-10">
              <h2 class="text-2xl">{t("myshop.details")}</h2>
              <form class="mt-4 space-y-5 rounded-lg bg-white p-5" novalidate onSubmit={save}>
                <ShopFields form={form} setForm={setForm} errors={errors()} />
                <FormActions saving={saving()} saved={saved()} error={error()} />
              </form>
              <div class="mt-6 rounded-lg bg-white p-5">
                <LogoUpload
                  shopId={s().id}
                  name={s().name}
                  logo={s().logo}
                  onUploaded={(logo) =>
                    mutate((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, shop: { ...prev.shop, logo } } : prev))}
                />
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>
        )}
      </Match>
    </Switch>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/main.tsx`
```tsx
import { Navigate, Route, Router } from "@solidjs/router";
import { lazy } from "solid-js";
import { render } from "solid-js/web";
import App from "./app/App.tsx";
import { i18n } from "./i18n/i18n.ts";
import { trackTraversals } from "./lib/history.ts";
import Dress from "./pages/Dress.tsx";
import Dresses from "./pages/Dresses.tsx";
import Favorites from "./pages/Favorites.tsx";
import Category from "./pages/Category.tsx";
import Home from "./pages/Home.tsx";
import Item from "./pages/Item.tsx";
import Search from "./pages/Search.tsx";
import NotFound from "./pages/NotFound.tsx";
import Shop from "./pages/Shop.tsx";
import "./index.css";

// Admin and "My shop" pages load on demand so brides never download them.
const AdminLayout = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx"));
const Login = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/Login.tsx"));
const AdminShops = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/Shops.tsx"));
const ShopEdit = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/ShopEdit.tsx"));
const AdminListings = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/Listings.tsx"));
const NewListing = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/NewListing.tsx"));
const ItemEdit = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/ItemEdit.tsx"));
const DressEdit = lazy(() => import("./pages/admin/DressEdit.tsx"));
const ShopLayout = lazy(() => import("./pages/shop/ShopLayout.tsx"));
const ShopLogin = lazy(() => import("./pages/shop/ShopLogin.tsx"));
const ShopSignup = lazy(() => import("./pages/shop/ShopSignup.tsx"));
const MyShop = lazy(() => import("./pages/shop/MyShop.tsx"));

trackTraversals();
document.documentElement.lang = i18n.lang();

render(
  () => (
    <Router root={App} scrollRestoration>
      <Route path="/" component={Home} />
      <Route path="/dresses" component={Dresses} />
      <Route path="/dresses/:id" component={Dress} />
      <Route path="/c/:slug" component={Category} />
      <Route path="/items/:id" component={Item} />
      <Route path="/search" component={Search} />
      <Route path="/shops/:slug" component={Shop} />
      <Route path="/favorites" component={Favorites} />
      <Route path="/admin/login" component={Login} />
      <Route path="/admin" component={AdminLayout}>
        <Route path="/" component={() => <Navigate href="/admin/shops" />} />
        <Route path="/shops" component={AdminShops} />
        <Route path="/shops/new" component={ShopEdit} />
        <Route path="/shops/:id" component={ShopEdit} />
        <Route path="/listings" component={AdminListings} />
        <Route path="/listings/new" component={NewListing} />
        <Route path="/dresses" component={() => <Navigate href="/admin/listings?category=dresses" />} />
        <Route path="/dresses/new" component={DressEdit} />
        <Route path="/dresses/:id" component={DressEdit} />
        <Route path="/items/new" component={ItemEdit} />
        <Route path="/items/:id" component={ItemEdit} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="/shop/signup" component={ShopSignup} />
      <Route path="/shop/login" component={ShopLogin} />
      <Route path="/shop" component={ShopLayout}>
        <Route path="/" component={MyShop} />
        <Route path="/listings/new" component={NewListing} />
        <Route path="/dresses/new" component={DressEdit} />
        <Route path="/dresses/:id" component={DressEdit} />
        <Route path="/items/new" component={ItemEdit} />
        <Route path="/items/:id" component={ItemEdit} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="*404" component={NotFound} />
    </Router>
  ),
  document.getElementById("root")!,
);
```

---

## Final verification (controller)

The controller runs this in headless Chrome, against a server built from the branch. It uses the **test** database (`TEST_DATABASE_URL`), never `wedding_dev`, and deletes every row it created afterwards.

1. **Adding a car:**
   - An owner signs up. "New listing" lists the five categories, and choosing Cars opens `/shop/items/new?category=cars`.
   - Saving an empty form shows "Required" for the name and every required car field, and "Must be more than 0" for the price.
   - The owner fills in the car and publishes it. Saving leads to `/shop/items/:id`.
   - A photo uploads, a busy day saves, and the car shows in "My listings" with the label "Cars".
2. **Brides browsing:**
   - The home page shows the five category buttons and the car among the newest listings.
   - The Cars button goes to `/c/cars`. Filtering by Limousine gives `/c/cars?body=limousine` with the car, and `color=black` gives "Nothing found".
   - The item page shows "900 TMT / hour", the details, the calendar and the shop card.
3. **Search:** `/search?q=demo` finds the car and a demo dress, and the Cars chip leaves only the car.
4. **Favorites and redirects:**
   - Favorites shows both the car and the dress.
   - `/items/<dress id>` redirects to `/dresses/<id>`, and `/c/dresses` shows Page not found.
5. **Admin:**
   - "Listings" shows both, with "Cars · <shop>" and "Dresses · <shop>".
   - The car opens in `/admin/items/:id` with its values filled in.
   - `?category=dresses` shows only the dress, and `/admin/dresses` redirects there.
6. **No sideways overflow:** at 320px in tk, ru and en, on `/`, `/c/cars`, `/c/national-clothes`, `/items/:id`, `/search?q=demo`, `/admin/listings`, `/admin/listings/new`, `/admin/items/:id` and `/admin/items/new?category=tents`.

## Spec coverage

| Spec | Where |
